```js
    const openBaziModal = (triggerEl) => {
        const modal = document.getElementById("bazi-term-modal");
        if (!modal || !currentBaziChart) return;

        const modalType = triggerEl.dataset.baziModal;
        const catEl = document.getElementById("bazi-term-modal-category");
        const titleEl = document.getElementById("bazi-term-modal-title");
        const visualEl = document.getElementById("bazi-term-modal-visual");
        const pillsEl = document.getElementById("bazi-term-modal-pills");
        const descEl = document.getElementById("bazi-term-modal-desc");
        const adviceEl = document.getElementById("bazi-term-modal-advice");

        visualEl?.classList.remove("is-zodiac-filtered");

        if (modalType === "pillar") {
            const pIdx = Number(triggerEl.dataset.pillarIdx || 0);
            const p = currentBaziChart.pillars[pIdx];
            if (!p) return;

            if (catEl) catEl.textContent = p.fullTitle;
            if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = `${p.canchi} • ${p.napAm}`;
            visualEl?.classList.add("is-zodiac-filtered");
            if (visualEl) {
                visualEl.innerHTML = `
                    <img src="./assets/icons/zodiac/${p.branchObj.zodiac}" alt="" />
                    <strong>Linh vật ${p.branchObj.animal} (${p.branchObj.name})</strong>
                `;
            }
            if (pillsEl) {
                pillsEl.innerHTML = `
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill day-term-modal__pill--highlight">Thập Thần: ${p.tenGod}</span>
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill">Trường sinh: ${p.lifeStage}</span>
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill">Nạp âm: ${p.napAm}</span>
                `;
            }
            if (descEl) {
                descEl.textContent = `${p.fullTitle} (${p.subRole}) gồm Thiên Can ${p.stemObj.name} (${p.stemObj.vnElem}) kết hợp Địa Chi ${p.branchObj.name} (${p.branchObj.vnElem}). ${TEN_GODS_DESC[p.tenGod] || ""}`;
            }
            const hiddenSummary = p.hiddenDetails.map((h) => `${h.stemName} (${h.tenGod})`).join(", ");
            if (adviceEl) {
                adviceEl.textContent = `Bên trong Địa Chi ${p.branchObj.name} tàng ẩn các Thiên Can: ${hiddenSummary}. Trạng thái năng lượng trên Vòng Trường Sinh đạt ngưỡng "${p.lifeStage}".`;
            }
        } else if (modalType === "god") {
            const role = triggerEl.dataset.godRole || "Dụng Thần";
            const elemKey = triggerEl.dataset.godKey || "earth";
            const info = ELEMENT_INFO_MAP[elemKey];

            if (catEl) catEl.textContent = "Phân Tích Dụng Thần & Cải Vận";
            if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = `${role} • ${info.name}`;
            if (visualEl) {
                visualEl.innerHTML = `
                    <img src="./assets/icons/${info.icon}" alt="" />
                    <strong>${info.name}</strong>
                `;
            }
            if (pillsEl) {
                pillsEl.innerHTML = `
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill day-term-modal__pill--highlight">${currentBaziChart.strengthState}</span>
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill">Hướng: ${info.directions}</span>
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill">${info.numbers}</span>
                `;
            }
            if (descEl) descEl.textContent = currentBaziChart.strengthAdvice;
            if (adviceEl) {
                adviceEl.textContent = `Ứng dụng ${info.name}: Ưu tiên màu sắc (${info.colors}), phương hướng (${info.directions}) và các ngành nghề/hoạt động gắn với ${info.career}.`;
            }
        } else if (modalType === "luck") {
            const lIdx = Number(triggerEl.dataset.luckIdx || 0);
            const lk = currentBaziChart.luckCycles8[lIdx];
            if (!lk) return;

            if (catEl) catEl.textContent = `Chu Kỳ Đại Vận 10 Năm (Đại Vận ${lk.step})`;
            if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = `Đại Vận ${lk.canchi} (${lk.ageRange})`;
            visualEl?.classList.add("is-zodiac-filtered");
            if (visualEl) {
                visualEl.innerHTML = `
                    <img src="./assets/icons/zodiac/${lk.branchObj.zodiac}" alt="" />
                    <strong>${lk.yearRange}</strong>
                `;
            }
            if (pillsEl) {
                pillsEl.innerHTML = `
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill day-term-modal__pill--highlight">Sao chủ vận: ${lk.tenGod}</span>
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill">Nạp âm: ${lk.napAm}</span>
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill">Vận: ${lk.lifeStage}</span>
                `;
            }
            if (descEl) {
                descEl.textContent = `Trong 10 năm từ ${lk.ageRange} (${lk.yearRange}), đại vận đi qua can chi ${lk.canchi} với sao ${lk.tenGod} làm chủ đạo. ${TEN_GODS_DESC[lk.tenGod] || ""}`;
            }
            if (adviceEl) {
                adviceEl.textContent = `Giai đoạn này mang nạp âm ${lk.napAm} và khí vận ${lk.lifeStage}. Hãy kết hợp năng lượng Dụng Thần (${ELEMENT_INFO_MAP[currentBaziChart.yongShinKey].name}) để tối ưu hóa quyết định sự nghiệp và tài chính.`;
            }
        }

        modal.hidden = false;
        document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    };

    const initBaziPage = () => {
        const mainEl = document.getElementById("main");
        if (!mainEl || !window.LunarEngine) return;
        if (mainEl.dataset.baziBound === "true") return;
        mainEl.dataset.baziBound = "true";

        const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        const dateParam = params.get("date");
        const hourParam = params.get("hour");
        const genderParam = params.get("gender");

        if (dateParam && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(dateParam)) {
            const [y, m, d] = dateParam.split("-").map(Number);
            selectedYear = y;
            selectedMonth = m;
            selectedDay = d;
        } else {
            const now = new Date();
            selectedYear = now.getFullYear();
            selectedMonth = now.getMonth() + 1;
            selectedDay = now.getDate();
            const brIdx = window.LunarEngine.getBranchIndexFromHour(now.getHours());
            selectedHourKey = BRANCH_META[brIdx]?.key || "hoi";
        }

        if (hourParam && BRANCH_META.some((b) => b.key === hourParam)) {
            selectedHourKey = hourParam;
        }
        if (genderParam === "female" || genderParam === "male") {
            selectedGender = genderParam;
        }

        yearGridStart = Math.floor(selectedYear / 12) * 12 - 8;
        renderCustomBaziPickers();

        // Đồng bộ đóng/mở giữa 5 Custom Bento Dropdowns
        const allDropdowns = Array.from(mainEl.querySelectorAll(".bazi-picker"));
        allDropdowns.forEach((current) => {
            current.ontoggle = () => {
                if (current.open) {
                    allDropdowns.forEach((other) => {
                        if (other !== current) other.removeAttribute("open");
                    });
                }
            };
        });

        document.getElementById("bazi-day-dropdown")?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
            const btn = e.target.closest("[data-bazi-day]");
            if (!btn) return;
            e.stopPropagation();
            selectedDay = Number(btn.dataset.baziDay);
            document.getElementById("bazi-day-dropdown")?.removeAttribute("open");
            renderCustomBaziPickers();
            renderBaziPage();
        });

        document.getElementById("bazi-month-dropdown")?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
            const btn = e.target.closest("[data-bazi-month]");
            if (!btn) return;
            e.stopPropagation();
            selectedMonth = Number(btn.dataset.baziMonth);
            document.getElementById("bazi-month-dropdown")?.removeAttribute("open");
            renderCustomBaziPickers();
            renderBaziPage();
        });

        document.getElementById("bazi-year-dropdown")?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
            const btn = e.target.closest("[data-bazi-year]");
            if (!btn) return;
            e.stopPropagation();
            selectedYear = Number(btn.dataset.baziYear);
            document.getElementById("bazi-year-dropdown")?.removeAttribute("open");
            renderCustomBaziPickers();
            renderBaziPage();
        });

        document.getElementById("bazi-year-prev-range")?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            yearGridStart -= 12;
            renderCustomBaziPickers();
        });

        document.getElementById("bazi-year-next-range")?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            yearGridStart += 12;
            renderCustomBaziPickers();
        });

        document.getElementById("bazi-hour-dropdown")?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
            const btn = e.target.closest("[data-bazi-hour]");
            if (!btn) return;
            e.stopPropagation();
            selectedHourKey = btn.dataset.baziHour || "hoi";
            document.getElementById("bazi-hour-dropdown")?.removeAttribute("open");
            renderCustomBaziPickers();
            renderBaziPage();
        });

        document.getElementById("bazi-region-dropdown")?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
            const btn = e.target.closest("[data-bazi-lon]");
            if (!btn) return;
            e.stopPropagation();
            selectedLongitude = Number(btn.dataset.baziLon) || 106.6;
            document.getElementById("bazi-region-dropdown")?.removeAttribute("open");
            renderCustomBaziPickers();
            renderBaziPage();
        });

        document.querySelectorAll("[data-bazi-gender]").forEach((btn) => {
            const isMatch = btn.dataset.baziGender === selectedGender;
            btn.classList.toggle("is-active", isMatch);
            btn.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(isMatch));

            btn.onclick = () => {
                selectedGender = btn.dataset.baziGender || "male";
                document.querySelectorAll("[data-bazi-gender]").forEach((b) => {
                    const active = b === btn;
                    b.classList.toggle("is-active", active);
                    b.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(active));
                });
                renderBaziPage();
            };
        });

        const nowBtn = document.getElementById("bazi-now-btn");
        if (nowBtn) {
            nowBtn.onclick = () => {
                const now = new Date();
                selectedYear = now.getFullYear();
                selectedMonth = now.getMonth() + 1;
                selectedDay = now.getDate();
                const brIdx = window.LunarEngine.getBranchIndexFromHour(now.getHours());
                selectedHourKey = BRANCH_META[brIdx]?.key || "hoi";
                yearGridStart = Math.floor(selectedYear / 12) * 12 - 8;
                renderCustomBaziPickers();
                renderBaziPage();
            };
        }

        const shareBtn = document.getElementById("bazi-share-btn");
        const shareLabel = document.getElementById("bazi-share-label");
        if (shareBtn && shareLabel) {
            shareBtn.onclick = async () => {
                const isoDate = `${selectedYear}-${pad(selectedMonth)}-${pad(selectedDay)}`;
                const shareUrl = `${window.location.origin}${window.location.pathname}?date=${isoDate}&hour=${selectedHourKey}&gender=${selectedGender}`;
                try {
                    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareUrl);
                    shareLabel.textContent = "Đã sao chép link!";
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        shareLabel.textContent = "Chia sẻ lá số";
                    }, 2000);
                } catch (e) {
                    shareLabel.textContent = "Chia sẻ lá số";
                }
            };
        }

        mainEl.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
            const triggerEl = e.target.closest("[data-bazi-modal]");
            if (!triggerEl) return;
            openBaziModal(triggerEl);
        });

        document.getElementById("bazi-term-modal-close")?.addEventListener("click", closeBaziModal);
        document.getElementById("bazi-term-modal-cancel")?.addEventListener("click", closeBaziModal);
        document.getElementById("bazi-term-modal-backdrop")?.addEventListener("click", closeBaziModal);

        document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
            const modal = document.getElementById("bazi-term-modal");
            if (e.key === "Escape" && modal && !modal.hidden) {
                closeBaziModal();
            }
        });

        window.addEventListener("converter-jump-to-date", (e) => {
            if (e.detail) {
                selectedYear = e.detail.year;
                selectedMonth = e.detail.month;
                selectedDay = e.detail.day;
                yearGridStart = Math.floor(selectedYear / 12) * 12 - 8;
                renderCustomBaziPickers();
                renderBaziPage();
            }
        });

        renderBaziPage();
    };

    if (document.readyState === "loading") {
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", initBaziPage);
    } else {
        initBaziPage();
    }
    window.addEventListener("template-loaded", initBaziPage);
})();
```

### clear-date.js

```js
// Đường dẫn trỏ tới file JSON gốc của bạn (chỉnh lại cho đúng thư mục nếu cần)
const jsonUrl = "../../api2/lich_van_nien_FINAL_366.json";

fetch(jsonUrl)
    .then((response) => {
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Không tìm thấy file JSON");
        return response.json();
    })
    .then((data) => {
        // 1. Chạy vòng lặp dọn dẹp dữ liệu
        data.forEach((item) => {
            // Sửa ID thành chuẩn ngay_dd_mm
            let dateStr = item.ngay_hien_thi.replace("/", "_");
            item.id = `ngay_${dateStr}`;

            // Làm sạch image_slug
            if (item.image_slug) {
                let indexVN = item.image_slug.indexOf("-vn-");
                if (indexVN !== -1) {
                    item.image_slug = item.image_slug.substring(0, indexVN);
                } else {
                    item.image_slug = item.image_slug.replace(/-\d+$/, "");
                }
            }
        });

        // 2. Đóng gói thành file mới
        const jsonString = JSON.stringify(data, null, 4);
        const blob = new Blob([jsonString], { type: "application/json" });

        // 3. Tự động tải về máy
        const link = document.createElement("a");
        link.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        link.download = "lich_van_nien_366_CLEANED.json"; // Tên file mới

        document.body.appendChild(link);
        link.click();
        document.body.removeChild(link);

        console.log("✅ Đã xử lý và tải xuống file thành công! Bạn kiểm tra thư mục Download nhé.");
    })
    .catch((error) => {
        console.error("❌ Lỗi:", error.message);
    });
```

### clock.js

```js
// assets/js/clock.js
// Chuyên trách Trang Đồng hồ (clock.html): Đồng hồ TimeSpot, 6 Thẻ Giờ Quốc Tế, Năng lượng Giờ, 4 Trụ Con Giáp & 12 Canh Giờ
(() => {
    let clockInterval = null;
    let lastRenderedBranchKey = "";
    let selectedBranchIndex = -1;

    const ANIMAL_VN_NAMES = [
        "Con Chuột",
        "Con Trâu",
        "Con Hổ",
        "Con Mèo",
        "Con Rồng",
        "Con Rắn",
        "Con Ngựa",
        "Con Dê",
        "Con Khỉ",
        "Con Gà",
        "Con Chó",
        "Con Hợi",
    ];

    const getZonedDateParts = (timeZone) => {
        const now = new Date();
        const formatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
            timeZone,
            year: "numeric",
            month: "2-digit",
            day: "2-digit",
            hour: "2-digit",
            minute: "2-digit",
            second: "2-digit",
            hour12: false,
        });
        const parts = Object.fromEntries(formatter.formatToParts(now).map((p) => [p.type, p.value]));
        return {
            year: Number(parts.year),
            month: Number(parts.month),
            day: Number(parts.day),
            hour: Number(parts.hour) % 24,
            minute: Number(parts.minute),
            second: Number(parts.second),
        };
    };

    const getPeriodLabel = (hour24) => {
        if (hour24 >= 5 && hour24 < 11) return "☀️ Sáng";
        if (hour24 >= 11 && hour24 < 14) return "☀️ Trưa";
        if (hour24 >= 14 && hour24 < 18) return "🌤️ Chiều";
        if (hour24 >= 18 && hour24 < 22) return "🌙 Tối";
        return "🌙 Đêm";
    };

    // 1. Cập nhật thời gian thực trên Dải 6 Thẻ Giờ Quốc Tế (Phương án 1)
    const updateWorldClockStrip = (format = "24") => {
        const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
        document.querySelectorAll(".clock-world-card").forEach((card) => {
            const tz = card.dataset.tz;
            if (!tz) return;
            const parts = getZonedDateParts(tz);
            const timeEl = card.querySelector("[data-world-tz]");
            const periodEl = card.querySelector("[data-world-period]");

            let dispH = parts.hour;
            let suffix = "";
            if (format === "12") {
                suffix = parts.hour >= 12 ? " PM" : " AM";
                dispH = parts.hour % 12 || 12;
            }

            if (timeEl) {
                timeEl.textContent = `${pad(dispH)}:${pad(parts.minute)}${suffix}`;
                timeEl.setAttribute("datetime", `${pad(parts.hour)}:${pad(parts.minute)}`);
            }
            if (periodEl) {
                periodEl.textContent = getPeriodLabel(parts.hour);
            }
        });
    };

    // 2. Cập nhật Khối Năng lượng Canh Giờ (<meter>) & Thông số giờ đang chọn (Phương án 2)
    const updateSelectedHourDetail = (hourObj, dayInfo) => {
        if (!hourObj || !dayInfo) return;

        const nameEl = document.getElementById("detail-selected-hour-name");
        const napamEl = document.getElementById("detail-selected-hour-napam");
        const dirEl = document.getElementById("detail-selected-directions");
        const termEl = document.getElementById("detail-selected-solarterm");

        if (nameEl) nameEl.textContent = `Giờ ${hourObj.canchi} (${hourObj.range})`;
        if (napamEl) napamEl.textContent = hourObj.napAm;
        if (dirEl && dayInfo.directions) {
            dirEl.textContent = `${dayInfo.directions.tai} • ${dayInfo.directions.hy}`;
        }
        if (termEl) {
            termEl.textContent = `${dayInfo.solarTerm} • Trực ${dayInfo.trucObj.name}`;
        }

        // Cập nhật thẻ <meter> Năng lượng Giờ
        const energyCard = document.getElementById("clock-energy-card");
        const energyIcon = document.getElementById("clock-energy-icon");
        const energyLabel = document.getElementById("clock-energy-label");
        const energyBadge = document.getElementById("clock-energy-lucdieu");
        const energyMeter = document.getElementById("clock-energy-meter");
        const energyDesc = document.getElementById("clock-energy-desc");

        if (energyCard) energyCard.dataset.element = hourObj.elementKey;
        if (energyIcon) energyIcon.src = `./assets/icons/${hourObj.elementIcon}`;
        if (energyLabel) energyLabel.textContent = `Năng lượng Giờ ${hourObj.canchi}`;
        if (energyBadge) {
            energyBadge.textContent = `${hourObj.lucDieuName} • ${hourObj.energyScore}%`;
            energyBadge.dataset.fortune = hourObj.lucDieuGood ? "good" : "bad";
        }
        if (energyMeter) {
            energyMeter.value = hourObj.energyScore;
            energyMeter.textContent = `${hourObj.energyScore}%`;
        }
        if (energyDesc) {
            energyDesc.textContent = `Hành ${hourObj.napAm} • Cung ${hourObj.lucDieuName}: ${hourObj.lucDieuDesc}`;
        }
    };

    // 3. Render 12 Thẻ Bento Canh Giờ (Đủ 12 con giáp, nhãn Năng lượng Ngũ Hành & Roving Tabindex)
    const renderZodiacGrid = (dayInfo) => {
        const grid = document.getElementById("clock-zodiac-grid");
        if (!grid || !dayInfo?.hours12) return;

        const activeFilter =
            document.querySelector(".clock-zodiac-section__filter-btn.is-active")?.dataset.hourFilter || "all";

        const focusTargetIdx = selectedBranchIndex !== -1 ? selectedBranchIndex : dayInfo.currentBranchIdx;

        grid.innerHTML = dayInfo.hours12
            .map((h) => {
                const isCurrent = h.index === dayInfo.currentBranchIdx;
                const isSelected = h.index === focusTargetIdx;
                const fortuneKey = h.isGood ? "good" : "bad";
                const fortuneLabel = h.isGood ? "Hoàng Đạo" : "Hắc Đạo";
                const fortuneIcon = h.isGood ? "☀️" : "🌙";
                const isDimmed = activeFilter !== "all" && activeFilter !== fortuneKey;
                const tabIdx = h.index === focusTargetIdx ? "0" : "-1";

                return `
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        class="clock-zodiac-card ${isCurrent ? "is-current" : ""} ${isSelected ? "is-selected" : ""} ${isDimmed ? "is-dimmed" : ""}"
                        tabindex="${tabIdx}"
                        ${isCurrent ? 'aria-current="time"' : ""}
                        aria-controls="clock-energy-card"
                        aria-label="Giờ ${h.canchi}, từ ${h.range}, ${fortuneLabel}, mệnh ${h.napAm}, năng lượng ${h.energyScore} phần trăm"
                        title="Giờ ${h.canchi} (${h.range}) • ${fortuneLabel} • ${h.napAm} • Năng lượng ${h.energyScore}%"
                        data-branch-index="${h.index}"
                        data-branch="${h.key}"
                        data-canchi="${h.canchi}"
                        data-range="${h.range}"
                        data-napam="${h.napAm}"
                        data-element="${h.elementKey}"
                        data-energy="${h.energyScore}"
                        data-fortune="${fortuneKey}"
                    >
                        <div class="clock-zodiac-card__top" aria-hidden="true">
                            <span class="clock-zodiac-card__name">Giờ ${h.vn}</span>
                            <span class="clock-zodiac-card__utc">${h.range}</span>
                        </div>

                        <div class="clock-zodiac-card__mid" aria-hidden="true">
                            <strong class="clock-zodiac-card__canchi">${h.canchi}</strong>
                            <span class="clock-zodiac-card__element-tag">
                                ${h.napAm} • ${h.energyScore}%
                            </span>
                        </div>

                        <div class="clock-zodiac-card__bottom" aria-hidden="true">
                            <span class="clock-zodiac-card__lucdieu">${h.lucDieuName}</span>
                            <span class="clock-zodiac-card__fortune">
                                <span>${fortuneIcon}</span> ${fortuneLabel}
                            </span>
                        </div>

                        <img
                            src="./assets/icons/zodiac/${h.icon}"
                            alt=""
                            aria-hidden="true"
                            class="clock-zodiac-card__img"
                        />
                    </button>
                `;
            })
            .join("");

        const cards = Array.from(grid.querySelectorAll(".clock-zodiac-card"));
        cards.forEach((card, idx) => {
            card.onclick = () => {
                selectedBranchIndex = Number(card.dataset.branchIndex);
                updateSelectedHourDetail(dayInfo.hours12[selectedBranchIndex], dayInfo);
                renderZodiacGrid(dayInfo);
            };

            card.onkeydown = (e) => {
                let nextIdx = -1;
                if (e.key === "ArrowRight" || e.key === "ArrowDown") nextIdx = (idx + 1) % cards.length;
                if (e.key === "ArrowLeft" || e.key === "ArrowUp") nextIdx = (idx - 1 + cards.length) % cards.length;

                if (nextIdx !== -1) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    selectedBranchIndex = Number(cards[nextIdx].dataset.branchIndex);
                    updateSelectedHourDetail(dayInfo.hours12[selectedBranchIndex], dayInfo);
                    renderZodiacGrid(dayInfo);
                    grid.querySelector(`[data-branch-index="${selectedBranchIndex}"]`)?.focus();
                }
            };
        });
    };

    const fetchWeatherAndSun = async (lat, lon, tz) => {
        try {
            const url = `https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast?latitude=${lat}&longitude=${lon}&current_weather=true&daily=sunrise,sunset&timezone=${encodeURIComponent(tz)}`;
            const res = await fetch(url);
            if (!res.ok) return;
            const data = await res.json();

            if (data.current_weather) {
                const temp = Math.round(data.current_weather.temperature);
                const isDay = data.current_weather.is_day === 1;
                const tempEl = document.getElementById("clock-weather-temp");
                const iconEl = document.getElementById("clock-weather-icon");
                if (tempEl) tempEl.textContent = `${temp}°C`;
                if (iconEl) iconEl.textContent = isDay ? "☀️" : "🌙";
            }

            if (data.daily?.sunrise?.[0] && data.daily?.sunset?.[0]) {
                const rise = data.daily.sunrise[0].split("T")[1];
                const set = data.daily.sunset[0].split("T")[1];
                const [rH, rM] = rise.split(":").map(Number);
                const [sH, sM] = set.split(":").map(Number);
                const totalMins = sH * 60 + sM - (rH * 60 + rM);
                const dH = Math.floor(totalMins / 60);
                const dM = totalMins % 60;
                const sunEl = document.getElementById("clock-sun-times");
                if (sunEl) {
                    sunEl.textContent = `${rise} - ${set} (${dH}h ${String(dM).padStart(2, "0")}m)`;
                }
            }
        } catch (err) {
            // Giữ giá trị mặc định nếu không có kết nối mạng
        }
    };

    const initClockPage = () => {
        const rootEl = document.getElementById("clock-calendar-root");
        const app = document.getElementById("clock-bento-app");
        if (!app || !window.LunarEngine) return;

        if (rootEl) rootEl.setAttribute("aria-busy", "false");
        if (app.dataset.bound === "true") return;
        app.dataset.bound = "true";

        const tickClock = () => {
            const tz = app.dataset.timezone || "Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh";
            const format = app.dataset.activeFormat || "24";
            const parts = getZonedDateParts(tz);
            const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");

            let displayHour = parts.hour;
            const ampmEl = document.getElementById("hero-clock-ampm");

            if (format === "12") {
                const suffix = parts.hour >= 12 ? "PM" : "AM";
                displayHour = parts.hour % 12 || 12;
                if (ampmEl) {
                    ampmEl.textContent = suffix;
                    ampmEl.hidden = false;
                }
            } else if (ampmEl) {
                ampmEl.hidden = true;
            }

            const hoursEl = app.querySelector('[data-clock-part="hours"]');
            const minutesEl = app.querySelector('[data-clock-part="minutes"]');
            const secondsEl = app.querySelector('[data-clock-part="seconds"]');
            const timeTag = document.getElementById("hero-clock-time");

            if (hoursEl) hoursEl.textContent = pad(displayHour);
            if (minutesEl) minutesEl.textContent = pad(parts.minute);
            if (secondsEl) secondsEl.textContent = pad(parts.second);
            if (timeTag) {
                timeTag.setAttribute("datetime", `${pad(parts.hour)}:${pad(parts.minute)}:${pad(parts.second)}`);
            }

            // Cập nhật giờ thực cho 6 thẻ Giờ Quốc Tế
            updateWorldClockStrip(format);

            const currentBranchIdx = window.LunarEngine.getBranchIndexFromHour(parts.hour);
            const branchRenderKey = `${parts.year}-${parts.month}-${parts.day}-${currentBranchIdx}-${tz}`;

            if (branchRenderKey !== lastRenderedBranchKey) {
                lastRenderedBranchKey = branchRenderKey;
                const dayInfo = window.LunarEngine.getFullDayInfo(parts.day, parts.month, parts.year, parts.hour);
                const currentHourInfo = dayInfo.hours12[currentBranchIdx];

                const statusPill = document.getElementById("clock-branch-pill");
                const statusText = document.getElementById("clock-branch-status-text");
                if (statusPill && currentHourInfo) {
                    statusPill.dataset.fortune = currentHourInfo.isGood ? "good" : "bad";
                }
                if (statusText && currentHourInfo) {
                    statusText.textContent = `Đang là Giờ ${currentHourInfo.canchi} • ${currentHourInfo.isGood ? "Hoàng Đạo" : "Hắc Đạo"}`;
                }

                const solarDateEl = document.getElementById("clock-meta-solar-date");
                const lunarDateEl = document.getElementById("clock-meta-lunar-date");
                const weekdayFormatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("vi-VN", {
                    timeZone: tz,
                    weekday: "long",
                    day: "2-digit",
                    month: "2-digit",
                    year: "numeric",
                });
                if (solarDateEl) {
                    solarDateEl.textContent = weekdayFormatter.format(new Date());
                    solarDateEl.setAttribute("datetime", `${parts.year}-${pad(parts.month)}-${pad(parts.day)}`);
                }
                if (lunarDateEl) {
                    lunarDateEl.textContent = `Âm lịch: ${pad(dayInfo.lunarDay)}/${pad(dayInfo.lunarMonth)} ${dayInfo.yearCanChi}`;
                }

                // PHƯƠNG ÁN 3: Cập nhật 4 Ô Bento Tứ Trụ kèm Icon 12 Con Giáp & Bóng Con Giáp chìm
                if (currentHourInfo) {
                    const hourIconPath = `./assets/icons/zodiac/${currentHourInfo.icon}`;
                    document.getElementById("pillar-hour-range").textContent = currentHourInfo.range;
                    document.getElementById("pillar-hour-canchi").textContent = currentHourInfo.canchi;
                    document.getElementById("pillar-hour-lucdieu").textContent =
                        `Cung: ${currentHourInfo.lucDieuName} (${currentHourInfo.energyScore}%)`;
                    document.getElementById("pillar-hour-napam").textContent = `Hành: ${currentHourInfo.napAm}`;
                    document.getElementById("pillar-hour-fortune").textContent = currentHourInfo.isGood
                        ? "Hoàng Đạo"
                        : "Hắc Đạo";
                    document.getElementById("pillar-hour-icon")?.setAttribute("src", hourIconPath);
                    document.getElementById("pillar-hour-bg")?.setAttribute("src", hourIconPath);
                }

                const dayZodiacPath = `./assets/icons/zodiac/${dayInfo.zodiacFile}.svg`;
                document.getElementById("pillar-day-truc").textContent = `Trực: ${dayInfo.trucObj.name}`;
                document.getElementById("pillar-day-canchi").textContent = dayInfo.dayCanChi;
                document.getElementById("pillar-day-element-sub").textContent = dayInfo.elementInfo.label;
                document.getElementById("pillar-day-napam").textContent = `Hành: ${dayInfo.dayNapAm}`;
                document.getElementById("pillar-day-lunar").textContent =
                    `Ngày ${pad(dayInfo.lunarDay)}/${pad(dayInfo.lunarMonth)} ÂL`;
                document.getElementById("pillar-day-icon")?.setAttribute("src", dayZodiacPath);
                document.getElementById("pillar-day-bg")?.setAttribute("src", dayZodiacPath);

                const monthZodiacPath = `./assets/icons/zodiac/${dayInfo.monthZodiacFile}.svg`;
                document.getElementById("pillar-month-num").textContent =
                    `Tháng ${dayInfo.lunarMonth} ÂL (${dayInfo.isFullMonth ? "Đủ" : "Thiếu"})`;
                document.getElementById("pillar-month-canchi").textContent = dayInfo.monthCanChi;
                document.getElementById("pillar-month-term-sub").textContent = `Tiết ${dayInfo.solarTerm}`;
                document.getElementById("pillar-month-napam").textContent = `Hành: ${dayInfo.monthNapAm}`;
                document.getElementById("pillar-month-icon")?.setAttribute("src", monthZodiacPath);
                document.getElementById("pillar-month-bg")?.setAttribute("src", monthZodiacPath);

                const yearZodiacPath = `./assets/icons/zodiac/${dayInfo.yearZodiacFile}.svg`;
                const yearAnimalName = ANIMAL_VN_NAMES[dayInfo.yearChiIdx] || "Con Ngựa";
                document.getElementById("pillar-year-solar").textContent = `Năm ${dayInfo.lunarYear}`;
                document.getElementById("pillar-year-canchi").textContent = dayInfo.yearCanChi;
                document.getElementById("pillar-year-animal-sub").textContent = `Sinh tiêu: ${yearAnimalName}`;
                document.getElementById("pillar-year-napam").textContent = `Hành: ${dayInfo.yearNapAm}`;
                document.getElementById("pillar-year-badge").textContent = yearAnimalName;
                document.getElementById("pillar-year-icon")?.setAttribute("src", yearZodiacPath);
                document.getElementById("pillar-year-bg")?.setAttribute("src", yearZodiacPath);

                const targetHour = selectedBranchIndex !== -1 ? dayInfo.hours12[selectedBranchIndex] : currentHourInfo;
                updateSelectedHourDetail(targetHour, dayInfo);
                renderZodiacGrid(dayInfo);

                const announcer = document.getElementById("clock-sr-announcer");
                if (announcer && currentHourInfo) {
                    announcer.textContent = `Đang xem múi giờ ${tz}, Giờ ${currentHourInfo.canchi}, ${currentHourInfo.isGood ? "Giờ Hoàng Đạo" : "Giờ Hắc Đạo"}`;
                }
            }
        };

        // Hàm dùng chung khi đổi Thành phố từ Dropdown hoặc từ 6 Thẻ Bento Giờ Quốc Tế
        const applyCitySelection = ({ cityId, cityName, country, lat, lon, tz, utc }) => {
            app.dataset.activeCity = cityId;
            app.dataset.timezone = tz;

            // Đồng bộ Dropdown
            app.querySelectorAll(".clock-hero__loc-option").forEach((o) => {
                const isMatch = o.dataset.cityId === cityId;
                o.classList.toggle("is-selected", isMatch);
                o.setAttribute("aria-selected", String(isMatch));
            });

            // Đồng bộ Dải 6 Thẻ Giờ Quốc Tế (Roving Tabindex + aria-pressed)
            app.querySelectorAll(".clock-world-card").forEach((card) => {
                const isMatch = card.dataset.cityId === cityId;
                card.classList.toggle("is-active", isMatch);
                card.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(isMatch));
                card.setAttribute("tabindex", isMatch ? "0" : "-1");
            });

            document.getElementById("clock-loc-label").textContent = `${cityName}, ${country}`;
            document.getElementById("clock-city-heading").innerHTML = `${cityName},<br />${country}`;
            document.getElementById("clock-meta-utc").textContent = utc;
            document.getElementById("schema-lat")?.setAttribute("content", lat);
            document.getElementById("schema-lon")?.setAttribute("content", lon);

            selectedBranchIndex = -1;
            lastRenderedBranchKey = "";
            tickClock();
            fetchWeatherAndSun(lat, lon, tz);
        };

        // Sự kiện Nút 12h / 24h
        app.querySelectorAll(".clock-hero__format-btn").forEach((btn) => {
            btn.onclick = () => {
                app.dataset.activeFormat = btn.dataset.timeFormat;
                app.querySelectorAll(".clock-hero__format-btn").forEach((b) => {
                    const isMatch = b === btn;
                    b.classList.toggle("is-active", isMatch);
                    b.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(isMatch));
                });
                tickClock();
            };
        });

        // Sự kiện Dropdown Khu vực
        const locDropdown = document.getElementById("clock-loc-dropdown");
        if (locDropdown) {
            const summary = locDropdown.querySelector("summary");
            locDropdown.ontoggle = () => {
                summary?.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(locDropdown.open));
            };
        }

        app.querySelectorAll(".clock-hero__loc-option").forEach((opt) => {
            opt.onclick = () => {
                applyCitySelection(opt.dataset);
                locDropdown?.removeAttribute("open");
            };
        });

        // Sự kiện Click & Phím mũi tên trên Dải 6 Thẻ Giờ Quốc Tế
        const worldCards = Array.from(app.querySelectorAll(".clock-world-card"));
        worldCards.forEach((card, idx) => {
            card.onclick = () => applyCitySelection(card.dataset);

            card.onkeydown = (e) => {
                let nextIdx = -1;
                if (e.key === "ArrowRight" || e.key === "ArrowDown") nextIdx = (idx + 1) % worldCards.length;
                if (e.key === "ArrowLeft" || e.key === "ArrowUp")
                    nextIdx = (idx - 1 + worldCards.length) % worldCards.length;

                if (nextIdx !== -1) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const targetCard = worldCards[nextIdx];
                    applyCitySelection(targetCard.dataset);
                    targetCard.focus();
                }
            };
        });

        // Sự kiện Bộ lọc Hoàng Đạo / Hắc Đạo
        app.querySelectorAll(".clock-zodiac-section__filter-btn").forEach((btn) => {
            btn.onclick = () => {
                app.querySelectorAll(".clock-zodiac-section__filter-btn").forEach((b) => {
                    const isMatch = b === btn;
                    b.classList.toggle("is-active", isMatch);
                    b.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(isMatch));
                });
                const parts = getZonedDateParts(app.dataset.timezone || "Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh");
                renderZodiacGrid(window.LunarEngine.getFullDayInfo(parts.day, parts.month, parts.year, parts.hour));
            };
        });

        lastRenderedBranchKey = "";
        tickClock();
        if (clockInterval) clearInterval(clockInterval);
        clockInterval = setInterval(tickClock, 1000);
        fetchWeatherAndSun("10.8231", "106.6297", "Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh");
    };

    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", initClockPage);
    window.addEventListener("template-loaded", initClockPage);
})();
```

### converter-modal.js

```js
// assets/js/converter-modal.js
// Chuyên trách duy nhất Bento Modal Đổi Ngày Âm ⇄ Dương (Gọi trực tiếp window.LunarEngine)
(() => {
    let isModalKeyBound = false;
    let convertMode = "solar2lunar";
    let selectedInputDay = 7;
    let selectedInputMonth = 11;
    let selectedInputYear = 2026;
    let yearGridStart = 2020;
    let resolvedSolarDate = { day: 7, month: 11, year: 2026 };

    const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");

    const VI_SOLAR_MONTH_NAMES = [
        "",
        "Tháng Một",
        "Tháng Hai",
        "Tháng Ba",
        "Tháng Tư",
        "Tháng Năm",
        "Tháng Sáu",
        "Tháng Bảy",
        "Tháng Tám",
        "Tháng Chín",
        "Tháng Mười",
        "Tháng Mười Một",
        "Tháng Mười Hai",
    ];

    const VI_LUNAR_MONTH_NAMES = [
        "",
        "Tháng Giêng",
        "Tháng Hai",
        "Tháng Ba",
        "Tháng Tư",
        "Tháng Năm",
        "Tháng Sáu",
        "Tháng Bảy",
        "Tháng Tám",
        "Tháng Chín",
        "Tháng Mười",
        "Tháng Mười Một",
        "Tháng Chạp",
    ];

    const toggleConverterModal = (forceOpen = null) => {
        const modal = document.getElementById("converter-modal");
        const triggerBtn = document.getElementById("widget-trigger-btn");
        if (!modal) return;

        const shouldOpen = forceOpen !== null ? forceOpen : modal.hidden;
        modal.hidden = !shouldOpen;

        if (triggerBtn) {
            triggerBtn.classList.toggle("is-active", shouldOpen);
            triggerBtn.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(shouldOpen));
        }

        document.body.style.overflow = shouldOpen ? "hidden" : "";
    };

    window.openConverterModal = toggleConverterModal;

    const renderCustomBentoPickers = () => {
        const dayListEl = document.getElementById("conv-day-list");
        const monthListEl = document.getElementById("conv-month-list");
        const yearListEl = document.getElementById("conv-year-list");
        const dayTextEl = document.getElementById("conv-day-text");
        const monthTextEl = document.getElementById("conv-month-text");
        const yearTextEl = document.getElementById("conv-year-text");
        const yearRangeLabel = document.getElementById("conv-year-range-label");

        if (!dayListEl || !monthListEl || !yearListEl) return;

        const maxDays =
            convertMode === "solar2lunar" ? new Date(selectedInputYear, selectedInputMonth, 0).getDate() : 30;

        if (selectedInputDay > maxDays) selectedInputDay = maxDays;

        if (dayTextEl) dayTextEl.textContent = `Ngày ${pad(selectedInputDay)}`;
        if (monthTextEl) monthTextEl.textContent = `Tháng ${pad(selectedInputMonth)}`;
        if (yearTextEl) yearTextEl.textContent = `Năm ${selectedInputYear}`;

        let daysHtml = "";
        for (let d = 1; d <= maxDays; d++) {
            const isSel = d === selectedInputDay;
            daysHtml += `<li><button type="button" role="option" aria-selected="${isSel}" data-conv-day="${d}" class="converter-modal__option-btn ${isSel ? "is-selected" : ""}">${pad(d)}</button></li>`;
        }
        dayListEl.innerHTML = daysHtml;

        let monthsHtml = "";
        for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) {
            const isSel = m === selectedInputMonth;
            monthsHtml += `<li><button type="button" role="option" aria-selected="${isSel}" data-conv-month="${m}" class="converter-modal__option-btn ${isSel ? "is-selected" : ""}">Tháng ${m}</button></li>`;
        }
        monthListEl.innerHTML = monthsHtml;

        if (yearRangeLabel) yearRangeLabel.textContent = `${yearGridStart} - ${yearGridStart + 11}`;
        let yearsHtml = "";
        for (let y = yearGridStart; y < yearGridStart + 12; y++) {
            const isSel = y === selectedInputYear;
            yearsHtml += `<li><button type="button" role="option" aria-selected="${isSel}" data-conv-year="${y}" class="converter-modal__option-btn ${isSel ? "is-selected" : ""}">${y}</button></li>`;
        }
        yearListEl.innerHTML = yearsHtml;
    };

    const syncLeapToggleUI = (lunarLeapFromResult = 0) => {
        const leapToggleBtn = document.getElementById("conv-leap-toggle-btn");
        const leapCheckbox = document.getElementById("conv-leap-checkbox");
        const leapStatusText = document.getElementById("conv-leap-status-text");
        if (!leapToggleBtn || !leapCheckbox || !leapStatusText) return;

        if (convertMode === "solar2lunar") {
            leapToggleBtn.classList.add("is-disabled");
            leapToggleBtn.classList.remove("is-active");
            leapCheckbox.checked = Boolean(lunarLeapFromResult);
            leapToggleBtn.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(Boolean(lunarLeapFromResult)));
            leapStatusText.textContent = lunarLeapFromResult ? "Tự động (Tháng nhuận)" : "Tự động (Tháng thường)";
        } else {
            leapToggleBtn.classList.remove("is-disabled");
            const isChecked = Boolean(leapCheckbox.checked);
            leapToggleBtn.classList.toggle("is-active", isChecked);
            leapToggleBtn.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(isChecked));
            leapStatusText.textContent = isChecked ? "Đang chọn: Tháng Nhuận" : "Tính theo: Tháng thường";
        }
    };

    const runConversionAndRender = () => {
        if (!window.LunarEngine) return;

        const leapCheckbox = document.getElementById("conv-leap-checkbox");
        const isLeapChecked = leapCheckbox?.checked ? 1 : 0;

        let sDay = selectedInputDay;
        let sMonth = selectedInputMonth;
        let sYear = selectedInputYear;

        if (convertMode === "lunar2solar") {
            let converted = window.LunarEngine.convertLunar2Solar(
                selectedInputDay,
                selectedInputMonth,
                selectedInputYear,
                isLeapChecked,
            );
            if (!converted || converted.sDay === 0) {
                if (leapCheckbox) leapCheckbox.checked = false;
                converted = window.LunarEngine.convertLunar2Solar(
                    selectedInputDay,
                    selectedInputMonth,
                    selectedInputYear,
                    0,
                );
            }
            if (converted && converted.sDay > 0) {
                sDay = converted.sDay;
                sMonth = converted.sMonth;
                sYear = converted.sYear;
            }
        }

        resolvedSolarDate = { day: sDay, month: sMonth, year: sYear };
        const info = window.LunarEngine.getFullDayInfo(sDay, sMonth, sYear);

        syncLeapToggleUI(info.lunarLeap);

        document.getElementById("conv-res-solar").textContent = `${pad(sDay)}/${pad(sMonth)}/${sYear}`;
        document.getElementById("conv-res-weekday").textContent = info.bilingualWeekday;
        document.getElementById("conv-res-solar-full").textContent =
            `${VI_SOLAR_MONTH_NAMES[sMonth]} (${info.solarMonthEnLabel}) • Năm ${sYear}`;

        const lunarTagEl = document.getElementById("conv-res-lunar-tag");
        if (lunarTagEl) {
            const sizeText = info.isFullMonth ? "Tháng Đủ" : "Tháng Thiếu";
            const leapText = info.lunarLeap ? " • Nhuận" : "";
            lunarTagEl.textContent = `Âm lịch • ${sizeText}${leapText}`;
        }

        document.getElementById("conv-res-lunar").textContent =
            `${pad(info.lunarDay)}/${pad(info.lunarMonth)}/${info.lunarYear}`;
        document.getElementById("conv-res-year-canchi").textContent = `Năm ${info.yearCanChi}`;
        document.getElementById("conv-res-year-napam").textContent = `Mệnh năm: ${info.yearNapAm}`;
        document
            .getElementById("conv-res-year-zodiac")
            ?.setAttribute("src", `./assets/icons/zodiac/${info.yearZodiacFile}.svg`);

        const dayZodiacPath = `./assets/icons/zodiac/${info.zodiacFile}.svg`;
        const monthZodiacPath = `./assets/icons/zodiac/${info.monthZodiacFile}.svg`;

        document.getElementById("conv-icon-day-zodiac")?.setAttribute("src", dayZodiacPath);
        document.getElementById("conv-bg-day-zodiac")?.setAttribute("src", dayZodiacPath);
        document.getElementById("conv-res-day-canchi").textContent = info.dayCanChi;
        document.getElementById("conv-res-day-status-sub").textContent =
            info.fortuneStatus === "good"
                ? "Ngày Hoàng Đạo"
                : info.fortuneStatus === "bad"
                  ? "Ngày Hắc Đạo"
                  : "Ngày Bình Thường";

        document.getElementById("conv-icon-month-zodiac")?.setAttribute("src", monthZodiacPath);
        document.getElementById("conv-bg-month-zodiac")?.setAttribute("src", monthZodiacPath);
        document.getElementById("conv-res-month-canchi").textContent = info.monthCanChi;
        document.getElementById("conv-res-month-lunar-sub").textContent =
            `${VI_LUNAR_MONTH_NAMES[info.lunarMonth]} (${info.isFullMonth ? "Đủ" : "Thiếu"})`;

        document.getElementById("conv-icon-element")?.setAttribute("src", `./assets/icons/${info.elementInfo.icon}`);
        document.getElementById("conv-res-napam").textContent = info.dayNapAm;
        document.getElementById("conv-res-element-sub").textContent = info.elementInfo.label;

        document.getElementById("conv-res-term").textContent = info.solarTerm;
        document.getElementById("conv-res-truc").textContent = `Trực ${info.trucObj.name} • ${info.trucObj.short}`;

        const fortunePill = document.getElementById("conv-res-fortune-pill");
        const fortuneTitle = document.getElementById("conv-res-fortune-title");
        const hoursGrid = document.getElementById("conv-res-hours-grid");

        if (fortunePill) fortunePill.dataset.fortune = info.fortuneStatus || "normal";
        if (fortuneTitle) {
            fortuneTitle.textContent =
                info.fortuneStatus === "good"
                    ? "Ngày Hoàng Đạo (Tốt)"
                    : info.fortuneStatus === "bad"
                      ? "Ngày Hắc Đạo (Xấu)"
                      : "Ngày Bình Thường";
        }

        if (hoursGrid) {
            hoursGrid.innerHTML = info.goodHourCards
                .map(
                    (card) => `
                        <article class="converter-modal__hour-card" title="Giờ Hoàng Đạo: ${card.hourCanChi} (${card.range})">
                            <div class="converter-modal__hour-top">
                                <span class="converter-modal__hour-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span>
                                <strong class="converter-modal__hour-name">${card.hourCanChi}</strong>
                            </div>
                            <span class="converter-modal__hour-time">${card.range}</span>
                            <img
                                src="./assets/icons/zodiac/${card.icon}"
                                alt=""
                                aria-hidden="true"
                                class="converter-modal__hour-zodiac"
                            />
                        </article>
                    `,
                )
                .join("");
        }
    };

    const setConverterToToday = () => {
        if (!window.LunarEngine) return;
        const now = new Date();
        const leapCheckbox = document.getElementById("conv-leap-checkbox");
        if (leapCheckbox) leapCheckbox.checked = false;

        if (convertMode === "solar2lunar") {
            selectedInputDay = now.getDate();
            selectedInputMonth = now.getMonth() + 1;
            selectedInputYear = now.getFullYear();
        } else {
            const lunarToday = window.LunarEngine.convertSolar2Lunar(
                now.getDate(),
                now.getMonth() + 1,
                now.getFullYear(),
            );
            selectedInputDay = lunarToday.lunarDay;
            selectedInputMonth = lunarToday.lunarMonth;
            selectedInputYear = lunarToday.lunarYear;
            if (leapCheckbox) leapCheckbox.checked = Boolean(lunarToday.lunarLeap);
        }

        yearGridStart = Math.floor(selectedInputYear / 12) * 12 - 4;
        renderCustomBentoPickers();
        runConversionAndRender();
    };

    const initConverterModal = () => {
        const modal = document.getElementById("converter-modal");
        const widgetBtn = document.getElementById("widget-trigger-btn");
        if (!modal) return;

        if (widgetBtn) {
            widgetBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                toggleConverterModal();
            };
        }

        if (modal.dataset.bound === "true") return;
        modal.dataset.bound = "true";

        const closeBtn = document.getElementById("converter-modal-close");
        const backdrop = document.getElementById("converter-modal-backdrop");
        const dayDropdown = document.getElementById("conv-day-dropdown");
        const monthDropdown = document.getElementById("conv-month-dropdown");
        const yearDropdown = document.getElementById("conv-year-dropdown");
        const prevRangeBtn = document.getElementById("conv-year-prev-range");
        const nextRangeBtn = document.getElementById("conv-year-next-range");
        const leapToggleBtn = document.getElementById("conv-leap-toggle-btn");
        const leapCheckbox = document.getElementById("conv-leap-checkbox");
        const swapBtn = document.getElementById("conv-swap-direction-btn");
        const todayBtn = document.getElementById("conv-btn-today");
        const jumpBtn = document.getElementById("conv-btn-jump");
        const tabs = modal.querySelectorAll(".converter-modal__tab");

        if (closeBtn) closeBtn.onclick = () => toggleConverterModal(false);
        if (backdrop) backdrop.onclick = () => toggleConverterModal(false);

        const switchConvertMode = (nextMode) => {
            convertMode = nextMode;
            tabs.forEach((t) => {
                const isMatch = t.dataset.convertMode === convertMode;
                t.classList.toggle("is-active", isMatch);
                t.setAttribute("aria-selected", String(isMatch));
            });

            const isLunarInput = convertMode === "lunar2solar";
            document.getElementById("conv-day-label").textContent = isLunarInput ? "Ngày Âm" : "Ngày Dương";
            document.getElementById("conv-month-label").textContent = isLunarInput ? "Tháng Âm" : "Tháng Dương";
            if (!isLunarInput && leapCheckbox) leapCheckbox.checked = false;

            renderCustomBentoPickers();
            runConversionAndRender();
        };

        tabs.forEach((tab) => {
            tab.onclick = () => switchConvertMode(tab.dataset.convertMode || "solar2lunar");
        });

        if (swapBtn) {
            swapBtn.onclick = () => {
                switchConvertMode(convertMode === "solar2lunar" ? "lunar2solar" : "solar2lunar");
            };
        }

        if (leapToggleBtn && leapCheckbox) {
            leapToggleBtn.onclick = () => {
                if (convertMode !== "lunar2solar") return;
                leapCheckbox.checked = !leapCheckbox.checked;
                runConversionAndRender();
            };
        }

        [dayDropdown, monthDropdown, yearDropdown].forEach((current) => {
            if (!current) return;
            current.ontoggle = () => {
                if (current.open) {
                    [dayDropdown, monthDropdown, yearDropdown].forEach((other) => {
                        if (other && other !== current) other.removeAttribute("open");
                    });
                }
            };
        });

        dayDropdown?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
            const btn = e.target.closest("[data-conv-day]");
            if (!btn) return;
            e.stopPropagation();
            selectedInputDay = Number(btn.dataset.convDay);
            dayDropdown.removeAttribute("open");
            renderCustomBentoPickers();
            runConversionAndRender();
        });

        monthDropdown?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
            const btn = e.target.closest("[data-conv-month]");
            if (!btn) return;
            e.stopPropagation();
            selectedInputMonth = Number(btn.dataset.convMonth);
            monthDropdown.removeAttribute("open");
            renderCustomBentoPickers();
            runConversionAndRender();
        });

        yearDropdown?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
            const btn = e.target.closest("[data-conv-year]");
            if (!btn) return;
            e.stopPropagation();
            selectedInputYear = Number(btn.dataset.convYear);
            yearDropdown.removeAttribute("open");
            renderCustomBentoPickers();
            runConversionAndRender();
        });

        if (prevRangeBtn) {
            prevRangeBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                yearGridStart -= 12;
                renderCustomBentoPickers();
            };
        }

        if (nextRangeBtn) {
            nextRangeBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                yearGridStart += 12;
                renderCustomBentoPickers();
            };
        }

        if (todayBtn) todayBtn.onclick = setConverterToToday;

        if (jumpBtn) {
            jumpBtn.onclick = () => {
                const { day, month, year } = resolvedSolarDate;
                toggleConverterModal(false);

                if (
                    document.getElementById("month-cal-grid") ||
                    document.getElementById("day-lucdieu-grid") ||
                    document.getElementById("bazi-pillars-grid")
                ) {
                    window.dispatchEvent(
                        new CustomEvent("converter-jump-to-date", {
                            detail: { year, month, day },
                        }),
                    );
                    document.getElementById("bloc-media-box")?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" });
                } else {
                    window.location.href = `./day.html?date=${year}-${pad(month)}-${pad(day)}`;
                }
            };
        }

        setConverterToToday();
    };

    if (!isModalKeyBound) {
        isModalKeyBound = true;
        document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
            const modal = document.getElementById("converter-modal");
            if (e.key === "Escape" && modal && !modal.hidden) {
                toggleConverterModal(false);
            }
        });
    }

    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", initConverterModal);
    window.addEventListener("template-loaded", initConverterModal);
})();
```

### day-calendar.js

```js
// assets/js/day-calendar.js
// Chuyên trách Trang Ngày (day.html): Tích hợp 39 Icon SVG trong ./assets/icons/fengshui/ & ./assets/icons/weather/,
// Khóa 3 Cặp Hàng Ngang chống lệch Ngã tư, 6 Animation Tiết Khí + 6 Animation Lục Diệu + 2 Animation Hoàng/Hắc Đạo (Phương án 1)
(() => {
    const DEFAULT_COVER_IMG = "./assets/images/calendar-covers/thang-11/nguyen-thi-minh-khai-01-11-1910-28-08-1941.jpg";

    let viewingDate = new Date();
    let activeHourFilter = "all"; // "all" | "good" | "bad"
    let currentDayHoursData = [];
    let currentDayContext = null;
    const monthApiCache = new Map();
    let activeDayVnEvents = [];

    const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");

    const toSlug = (str) =>
        String(str || "")
            .normalize("NFD")
            .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
            .replace(/đ/g, "d")
            .replace(/Đ/g, "D")
            .toLowerCase()
            .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
            .replace(/(^-|-$)/g, "");

    const fullViDateFormatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("vi-VN", {
        weekday: "long",
        day: "2-digit",
        month: "2-digit",
        year: "numeric",
    });

    const buildBentoTooltipHtml = (title, desc, tagLabel) => `
        <span class="bento-tooltip" role="tooltip">
            <strong class="bento-tooltip__title">✦ ${title}</strong>
            <span class="bento-tooltip__desc">${desc}</span>
            <span class="bento-tooltip__tag">${tagLabel} &#8594;</span>
        </span>
    `;

    const buildWuXingSvgHtml = (activeElementsSet, hourElementKey, isLarge = false) => {
        const cls = (elKey) => {
            const state = activeElementsSet.has(elKey) ? "is-active" : "is-dimmed";
            const hourCls = elKey === hourElementKey ? "is-hour-element" : "";
            return `${state} ${hourCls}`.trim();
        };

        return `
            <svg class="wuxing-mini ${isLarge ? "wuxing-mini--lg" : ""}" viewBox="0 0 120 120" role="img" aria-label="Biểu đồ Bát Tự Ngũ Hành">
                <circle class="wuxing-mini__cycle" cx="60" cy="60" r="39" />
                <polygon class="wuxing-mini__star" points="60,21 83,91 23,48 97,48 37,91" />
                <g class="wuxing-mini__node wuxing-mini__node--fire ${cls("fire")}">
                    <circle cx="60" cy="21" r="15.5" /><text x="60" y="21">Hỏa</text>
                </g>
                <g class="wuxing-mini__node wuxing-mini__node--earth ${cls("earth")}">
                    <circle cx="97" cy="48" r="15.5" /><text x="97" y="48">Thổ</text>
                </g>
                <g class="wuxing-mini__node wuxing-mini__node--metal ${cls("metal")}">
                    <circle cx="83" cy="91" r="15.5" /><text x="83" y="91">Kim</text>
                </g>
                <g class="wuxing-mini__node wuxing-mini__node--water ${cls("water")}">
                    <circle cx="37" cy="91" r="15.5" /><text x="37" y="91">Thủy</text>
                </g>
                <g class="wuxing-mini__node wuxing-mini__node--wood ${cls("wood")}">
                    <circle cx="23" cy="48" r="15.5" /><text x="23" y="48">Mộc</text>
                </g>
            </svg>
        `;
    };

    const getSolarTermRangeInfo = (dd, mm, yy) => {
        const code = mm * 100 + dd;
        for (const t of SOLAR_TERMS_24) {
            const c1 = t.m1 * 100 + t.d1;
            const c2 = t.m2 * 100 + t.d2;
            if (c1 <= c2) {
                if (code >= c1 && code <= c2) {
                    return {
                        ...t,
                        start: `${pad(t.d1)}/${pad(t.m1)}/${yy}`,
                        end: `${pad(t.d2)}/${pad(t.m2)}/${yy}`,
                    };
                }
            } else if (code >= c1 || code <= c2) {
                const startYear = mm === 1 ? yy - 1 : yy;
                const endYear = mm === 12 ? yy + 1 : yy;
                return {
                    ...t,
                    start: `${pad(t.d1)}/${pad(t.m1)}/${startYear}`,
                    end: `${pad(t.d2)}/${pad(t.m2)}/${endYear}`,
                };
            }
        }
        return {
            ...SOLAR_TERMS_24[20],
            start: `07/11/${yy}`,
            end: `21/11/${yy}`,
        };
    };

    const closeDayTermModal = () => {
        const modal = document.getElementById("day-term-modal");
        if (!modal) return;
        modal.hidden = true;
        document.body.style.overflow = "";
    };

    const openDayTermModal = (colType, hourIdx, seoHref, extraKey = "") => {
        const modal = document.getElementById("day-term-modal");
        if (!modal) return;

        const catEl = document.getElementById("day-term-modal-category");
        const titleEl = document.getElementById("day-term-modal-title");
        const visualEl = document.getElementById("day-term-modal-visual");
        const pillsEl = document.getElementById("day-term-modal-pills");
        const descEl = document.getElementById("day-term-modal-desc");
        const adviceEl = document.getElementById("day-term-modal-advice");
        const seoLinkEl = document.getElementById("day-term-modal-seo-link");
        const seoLabelEl = document.getElementById("day-term-modal-seo-label");

        visualEl.classList.remove("is-zodiac-filtered");

        if (colType === "direction" && currentDayContext) {
            const deity = DIRECTION_DEITIES[extraKey] || DIRECTION_DEITIES.hy;
            const dirVal = currentDayContext.directions[extraKey] || "Đông Nam";
            catEl.textContent = "Hướng Xuất Hành Theo Thiên Can";
            titleEl.textContent = `${deity.name} • Hướng ${dirVal}`;
            visualEl.classList.add("is-zodiac-filtered");
            visualEl.innerHTML = `
                <img src="${deity.icon}" alt="" />
                <strong>Hướng ${dirVal}</strong>
            `;
            pillsEl.innerHTML = `
                <span class="day-term-modal__pill day-term-modal__pill--highlight">${deity.status}</span>
                <span class="day-term-modal__pill">Ngày: ${currentDayContext.dayCanChi}</span>
                <span class="day-term-modal__pill">Phương vị: ${dirVal}</span>
            `;
            descEl.textContent = deity.desc;
            adviceEl.textContent = `${deity.advice} (Trong ngày ${currentDayContext.dayCanChi} - ${currentDayContext.dayNapAm}, hướng ${deity.name} nằm tại phương ${dirVal}).`;
            seoLabelEl.textContent = `Tra cứu Hướng Xuất Hành & Cát Thần`;
        } else if (colType === "solarterm" && currentDayContext) {
            const term = currentDayContext.termRange;
            catEl.textContent = "Nhị Thập Tứ Tiết Khí";
            titleEl.textContent = `Tiết ${term.name}`;
            visualEl.classList.add("is-zodiac-filtered");
            visualEl.innerHTML = `
                <img src="./assets/icons/weather/${term.icon}" alt="" />
                <strong>${term.season}</strong>
            `;
            pillsEl.innerHTML = `
                <span class="day-term-modal__pill day-term-modal__pill--highlight">${term.season}</span>
                <span class="day-term-modal__pill">Bắt đầu: ${term.start}</span>
                <span class="day-term-modal__pill">Kết thúc: ${term.end}</span>
            `;
            descEl.textContent = `Tiết ${term.name}: ${term.meaning}`;
            adviceEl.textContent = term.advice;
            seoLabelEl.textContent = `Xem bảng 24 Tiết Khí trong năm`;
        } else {
            const rowData = currentDayHoursData[hourIdx];
            if (!rowData) return;

            const daoImgPath = rowData.isHoangDao
                ? "./assets/icons/fengshui/hoang-dao.svg"
                : "./assets/icons/fengshui/hac-dao.svg";

            if (colType === "time") {
                catEl.textContent = "Canh Giờ Âm Lịch";
                titleEl.textContent = `Giờ ${rowData.hCanChi} (${rowData.h.range})`;
                visualEl.classList.add("is-zodiac-filtered");
                visualEl.innerHTML = `
                    <img src="./assets/icons/zodiac/${rowData.h.icon}" alt="" />
                    <strong>${rowData.h.animalName}</strong>
                `;
                pillsEl.innerHTML = `
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill day-term-modal__pill--highlight">
                        <img src="${daoImgPath}" alt="" />
                        <span>${rowData.isHoangDao ? "Giờ Hoàng Đạo (Tốt)" : "Giờ Hắc Đạo (Xấu)"}</span>
                    </span>
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill">Hợp mệnh: ${rowData.harmonyObj.good}</span>
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill">Kỵ mệnh: ${rowData.harmonyObj.bad}</span>
                `;
                descEl.textContent = `Canh giờ ${rowData.hCanChi} bắt đầu từ ${rowData.h.range}. Trong ngày ${rowData.dayCanChi}, đây là khung giờ ${rowData.isHoangDao ? "Hoàng Đạo (Cát thần bảo hộ, thuận lợi cho việc quan trọng)" : "Hắc Đạo (Nên thận trọng khi khởi sự việc lớn)"}.`;
                adviceEl.textContent = `${rowData.h.baguaAdvice} • ${rowData.harmonyObj.note}`;
                seoLabelEl.textContent = "Xem trên Đồng hồ 12 Canh Giờ";
            } else if (colType === "napam") {
                catEl.textContent = "Nạp Âm Lục Thập Hoa Giáp";
                titleEl.textContent = rowData.hNapAm;
                visualEl.innerHTML = `
                    <img src="./assets/icons/${rowData.elemIcon}" alt="" />
                    <strong>${rowData.elemShort}</strong>
                `;
                pillsEl.innerHTML = `
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill day-term-modal__pill--highlight">${rowData.elemShort}</span>
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill">Hợp mệnh: ${rowData.harmonyObj.good}</span>
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill">Kỵ mệnh: ${rowData.harmonyObj.bad}</span>
                `;
                descEl.textContent = rowData.napAmObj.meaning;
                adviceEl.textContent = `Giờ ${rowData.hCanChi} mang nạp âm ${rowData.hNapAm} (${rowData.elemShort}). ${rowData.harmonyObj.note}`;
                seoLabelEl.textContent = `Tìm hiểu chuyên sâu về ${rowData.hNapAm}`;
            } else if (colType === "lucdieu") {
                catEl.textContent = "Khổng Minh Lục Diệu";
                titleEl.textContent = `Sao ${rowData.starObj.name} • Giờ ${rowData.hCanChi}`;
                visualEl.classList.add("is-zodiac-filtered");
                visualEl.innerHTML = `
                    <img src="${rowData.starObj.icon}" alt="" />
                    <strong>Giờ ${rowData.h.chi} (${rowData.h.short}h)</strong>
                `;
                pillsEl.innerHTML = `
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill day-term-modal__pill--highlight">${rowData.starObj.isGood ? "Cát Tinh (Tốt)" : "Hung Tinh (Xấu)"}</span>
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill">Hợp mệnh: ${rowData.harmonyObj.good}</span>
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill">Lưu ý mệnh: ${rowData.harmonyObj.bad}</span>
                `;
                descEl.textContent = rowData.starObj.desc;
                adviceEl.textContent = `${rowData.starObj.advice} • Ngũ hành giờ thuộc ${rowData.elemShort}: Rất tốt cho người mệnh ${rowData.harmonyObj.good}, người mệnh ${rowData.harmonyObj.bad} nên lưu ý.`;
                seoLabelEl.textContent = `Tra cứu thuật Khổng Minh Lục Diệu`;
            } else if (colType === "wuxing") {
                catEl.textContent = "Bát Tự Ngũ Hành Tứ Trụ";
                titleEl.textContent = `Ngũ Hành Giờ ${rowData.hCanChi}`;
                visualEl.innerHTML = buildWuXingSvgHtml(rowData.activeElementsForHour, rowData.hourElementKey, true);
                pillsEl.innerHTML = `
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill day-term-modal__pill--highlight">Hội tụ (Sáng): ${rowData.activeNames}</span>
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill">Đang khuyết (Chìm): ${rowData.missingNames}</span>
                `;
                descEl.textContent = `Tứ Trụ tại thời điểm này gồm: Năm ${rowData.yearCanChi} • Tháng ${rowData.monthCanChi} • Ngày ${rowData.dayCanChi} • Giờ ${rowData.hCanChi}. Các đỉnh sáng trên biểu đồ là các hành xuất hiện trong Thiên Can của 4 trụ.`;
                adviceEl.textContent =
                    rowData.missingNames === "Không khuyết"
                        ? "Khung giờ này hội tụ đầy đủ Ngũ Hành cân bằng, rất tốt cho các hoạt động tổng hợp."
                        : `Khung giờ này đang khuyết hành ${rowData.missingNames}. Người có Dụng Thần hoặc bản mệnh hợp với ${rowData.harmonyObj.good} sẽ rất vượng khí khi hành sự vào giờ này.`;
                seoLabelEl.textContent = "Lập Lá Số Bát Tự Chuyên Sâu";
            } else {
                catEl.textContent = "Phương Vị Bát Quái & Linh Vật";
                titleEl.textContent = `Quẻ ${rowData.baguaName} • Hướng ${rowData.baguaDir}`;
                visualEl.classList.add("is-zodiac-filtered");
                visualEl.innerHTML = `
                    <img src="./assets/icons/zodiac/${rowData.h.icon}" alt="" />
                    <strong>${rowData.h.animalName}</strong>
                `;
                pillsEl.innerHTML = `
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill day-term-modal__pill--highlight">Quẻ ${rowData.baguaName}</span>
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill">Phương vị: ${rowData.baguaDir}</span>
                    <span class="day-term-modal__pill">Hợp mệnh: ${rowData.harmonyObj.good}</span>
                `;
                descEl.textContent = rowData.h.baguaDesc;
                adviceEl.textContent = `${rowData.h.baguaAdvice} (${rowData.harmonyObj.note})`;
                seoLabelEl.textContent = `Tra cứu Bát Quái & 12 Linh Vật`;
            }
        }

        if (seoLinkEl) seoLinkEl.href = seoHref || "./utilities.html";
        modal.hidden = false;
        document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    };

    const computeSunTimes4Regions = (dd, mm, yy) => {
        const startOfYear = new Date(yy, 0, 0);
        const current = new Date(yy, mm - 1, dd);
        const dayOfYear = Math.floor((current - startOfYear) / 86400000);
        const seasonalWave = Math.sin(((dayOfYear - 80) / 365) * 2 * Math.PI);

        const regions = [
            {
                region: "Bắc",
                place: "Hà Nội - Hải Phòng (Duyên hải Bắc Bộ)",
                baseRiseMin: 350,
                baseSetMin: 1055,
                amp: 24,
            },
            {
                region: "Trung",
                place: "Đà Nẵng - Huế (Bắc & Nam Trung Bộ)",
                baseRiseMin: 344,
                baseSetMin: 1054,
                amp: 16,
            },
            { region: "Nam", place: "TP. Hồ Chí Minh (Đông Nam Bộ)", baseRiseMin: 345, baseSetMin: 1062, amp: 11 },
            { region: "ĐBSCL", place: "Cần Thơ - Cà Mau (Tây Nam Bộ)", baseRiseMin: 349, baseSetMin: 1067, amp: 10 },
        ];

        const formatMins = (totalMins) => {
            const h = Math.floor(totalMins / 60);
            const m = Math.round(totalMins % 60);
            return `${pad(h)}:${pad(m)}:20`;
        };

        const rows = regions.map((r) => {
            const rise = Math.round(r.baseRiseMin - seasonalWave * r.amp);
            const set = Math.round(r.baseSetMin + seasonalWave * r.amp);
            return {
                region: r.region,
                place: r.place,
                riseStr: formatMins(rise),
                setStr: formatMins(set),
                dayLengthMins: set - rise,
            };
        });

        const avgLen = Math.round(rows.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.dayLengthMins, 0) / rows.length);
        return {
            dayLengthLabel: `${Math.floor(avgLen / 60)} giờ ${pad(avgLen % 60)} phút`,
            rows,
        };
    };

    const fetchMonthHistoryData = async (monthNum) => {
        const m = Number(monthNum) || 11;
        if (monthApiCache.has(m)) return monthApiCache.get(m);

        for (const path of [`./api/thang-${m}.json`, `./api/thang-${pad(m)}.json`]) {
            try {
                const res = await fetch(path);
                if (res.ok) {
                    const data = await res.json();
                    const normalized = Array.isArray(data) ? data : data?.data || data?.events || [];
                    monthApiCache.set(m, normalized);
                    return normalized;
                }
            } catch (err) {
                // Thử đường dẫn tiếp theo
            }
        }
        monthApiCache.set(m, []);
        return [];
    };

    const buildDetailUrlFromItem = (item, monthNum) => {
        if (!item) return "./detail.html";
        const rawSlug = item.image_slug || item.slug || "";
        const cleanFolder = rawSlug.replace(/-\d{2}-\d{2}-\d{4}.*$/, "") || rawSlug;
        const typeFolder = String(item.category_id || item.loai || "")
            .toLowerCase()
            .includes("event")
            ? "events"
            : "figures";
        const params = new URLSearchParams({
            type: typeFolder,
            folder: cleanFolder,
            slug: rawSlug,
            month: String(monthNum),
        });
        return `./detail.html?${params.toString()}`;
    };

    const setCoverImageWithFallback = (imgEl, monthNum, imageSlug, altText) => {
        if (!imgEl) return;
        if (altText) imgEl.alt = altText;
        if (!imageSlug) {
            imgEl.onerror = null;
            imgEl.src = DEFAULT_COVER_IMG;
            return;
        }
        const cleanSlug = imageSlug
            .split("/")
            .pop()
            .trim()
            .replace(/\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/i, "");
        const basePath = `./assets/images/calendar-covers/thang-${monthNum}/${cleanSlug}`;
        imgEl.onerror = () => {
            imgEl.onerror = () => {
                imgEl.onerror = null;
                imgEl.src = DEFAULT_COVER_IMG;
            };
            imgEl.src = `${basePath}.webp`;
        };
        imgEl.src = `${basePath}.jpg`;
    };

    const renderHistoryItemToBloc = (item, monthNum, solarDay) => {
        const imgEl = document.getElementById("detail-history-img");
        const mediaLinkEl = document.getElementById("detail-history-link");
        const captionLinkEl = document.getElementById("detail-history-caption");
        const captionTextEl = document.getElementById("detail-history-caption-text");
        const quoteTextEl = document.getElementById("detail-quote-text");
        const quoteAuthorEl = document.getElementById("detail-quote-author");

        const paddedDay = pad(solarDay);
        const paddedMonth = pad(monthNum);

        if (!item) {
            if (imgEl) {
                imgEl.onerror = null;
                imgEl.src = DEFAULT_COVER_IMG;
            }
            if (captionTextEl) {
                captionTextEl.textContent = `Sự kiện & Nhân vật lịch sử ngày ${paddedDay}/${paddedMonth}`;
            }
            if (mediaLinkEl) mediaLinkEl.href = `./detail.html?day=${solarDay}&month=${monthNum}`;
            if (captionLinkEl) captionLinkEl.href = `./detail.html?day=${solarDay}&month=${monthNum}`;
            return;
        }

        const occasionText = item.hien_thi_nhan_dip || item.ten || `Sự kiện lịch sử ngày ${paddedDay}/${paddedMonth}`;
        const detailHref = buildDetailUrlFromItem(item, monthNum);
        setCoverImageWithFallback(imgEl, monthNum, item.image_slug || item.slug, occasionText);

        if (captionTextEl) captionTextEl.textContent = occasionText;
        if (mediaLinkEl) mediaLinkEl.href = detailHref;
        if (captionLinkEl) captionLinkEl.href = detailHref;
        if (quoteTextEl) {
            const raw = (item.quote || item.danh_ngon || "").trim();
            quoteTextEl.textContent = raw ? (raw.startsWith('"') ? raw : `"${raw}"`) : `"${occasionText}"`;
        }
        if (quoteAuthorEl) {
            quoteAuthorEl.textContent = `- ${item.ten || item.tac_gia || "Lịch sử Việt Nam"} -`;
        }
    };

    const updateHistoryForDay = async (solarDay, monthNum) => {
        const sameDayBtn = document.getElementById("btn-same-day-events");
        const sameDayLabel = document.getElementById("label-same-day-events");
        const intlBtn = document.getElementById("btn-intl-events");
        const intlLabel = document.getElementById("label-intl-events");

        const paddedDay = pad(solarDay);
        const paddedMonth = pad(monthNum);
        const targetDatePadded = `${paddedDay}/${paddedMonth}`;
        const targetDateShort = `${Number(solarDay)}/${Number(monthNum)}`;
        const targetSlugDate = `-${paddedDay}-${paddedMonth}-`;

        const allMonthItems = await fetchMonthHistoryData(monthNum);

        const matchedItems = allMonthItems.filter((entry) => {
            const displayDate = String(entry.ngay_hien_thi || entry.ngay_duong || entry.date || "").trim();
            const occasionStr = String(entry.hien_thi_nhan_dip || "");
            const slugStr = String(entry.image_slug || "");

            return (
                displayDate.startsWith(targetDatePadded) ||
                displayDate.startsWith(targetDateShort) ||
                displayDate === String(solarDay) ||
                Number(entry.day) === Number(solarDay) ||
                occasionStr.includes(`(${targetDatePadded}`) ||
                slugStr.includes(targetSlugDate)
            );
        });

        activeDayIntlEvents = matchedItems.filter((entry) => {
            const scope = String(entry.pham_vi || entry.scope || entry.category_id || "").toLowerCase();
            return Boolean(entry.is_international) || scope.includes("quoc-te") || scope.includes("international");
        });
        activeDayVnEvents = matchedItems.filter((entry) => !activeDayIntlEvents.includes(entry));
        currentVnEventIndex = 0;
        currentIntlEventIndex = 0;

        if (sameDayBtn) {
            if (activeDayVnEvents.length > 1) {
                sameDayBtn.hidden = false;
                sameDayBtn.classList.add("is-active");
                if (sameDayLabel) sameDayLabel.textContent = `Sự kiện cùng ngày (${activeDayVnEvents.length})`;
            } else {
```
