```js
                sameDayBtn.hidden = true;
                sameDayBtn.classList.remove("is-active");
            }
        }

        if (intlBtn) {
            if (activeDayIntlEvents.length > 0) {
                intlBtn.hidden = false;
                intlBtn.classList.remove("is-active");
                if (intlLabel) intlLabel.textContent = `Sự kiện quốc tế (${activeDayIntlEvents.length})`;
            } else {
                intlBtn.hidden = true;
                intlBtn.classList.remove("is-active");
            }
        }

        renderHistoryItemToBloc(activeDayVnEvents[0] || activeDayIntlEvents[0] || null, monthNum, solarDay);
    };

    const applyHourRowsFilter = () => {
        document.querySelectorAll(".day-hour-row").forEach((row) => {
            const fortune = row.dataset.hourFortune || "normal";
            const isDimmed = activeHourFilter !== "all" && activeHourFilter !== fortune;
            row.classList.toggle("is-dimmed", isDimmed);
        });
    };

    const renderDayPage = (dateObj) => {
        if (!window.LunarEngine) return;

        viewingDate = new Date(dateObj.getFullYear(), dateObj.getMonth(), dateObj.getDate());
        const sDay = viewingDate.getDate();
        const sMonth = viewingDate.getMonth() + 1;
        const sYear = viewingDate.getFullYear();
        const isoDateStr = `${sYear}-${pad(sMonth)}-${pad(sDay)}`;

        const info = window.LunarEngine.getFullDayInfo(sDay, sMonth, sYear);
        const yearStemIdx = (info.lunarYear + 6) % 10;
        const monthStemIdx = (yearStemIdx * 2 + info.lunarMonth + 1) % 10;
        const dayStemIdx = (info.jd + 9) % 10;
        const ratHourStemIdx = ((dayStemIdx % 5) * 2) % 10;

        const toolbarHeading = document.getElementById("day-toolbar-heading");
        if (toolbarHeading) toolbarHeading.textContent = fullViDateFormatter.format(viewingDate);

        // 1. CỘT TRÁI (Tờ Lịch Bloc, Giờ Hoàng/Hắc Đạo, Trực/Sao & Việc nên làm động theo Trực)
        document.getElementById("detail-solar-year").textContent = String(sYear);
        document.getElementById("detail-solar-month-vn").textContent = info.solarMonthVnLabel;
        document.getElementById("detail-solar-month-en").textContent = info.solarMonthEnLabel;
        document.getElementById("detail-solar-day").textContent = String(sDay);
        document.getElementById("detail-lunar-year").textContent = `NĂM ${info.yearCanChi.toUpperCase()}`;
        document.getElementById("detail-weekday").textContent = info.bilingualWeekday;

        const statusEl = document.getElementById("detail-day-status");
        if (statusEl) {
            statusEl.textContent = info.badgeText
                ? `Ngày ${info.badgeText}`
                : info.fortuneStatus === "good"
                  ? "Ngày Hoàng Đạo"
                  : info.fortuneStatus === "bad"
                    ? "Ngày Hắc Đạo"
                    : "Ngày bình thường";
        }

        document.getElementById("detail-lunar-day").textContent = String(info.lunarDay);
        document.getElementById("detail-lunar-month").textContent = info.lunarMonthFullLabel;
        document.getElementById("detail-canchi-month").textContent = `Tháng ${info.monthCanChi}`;
        document.getElementById("detail-canchi-day").textContent = `Ngày ${info.dayCanChi}`;
        document.getElementById("detail-canchi-hour").textContent = `Giờ ${info.ratHourCanChi}`;
        document.getElementById("detail-element").textContent = info.dayNapAm;
        document.getElementById("detail-solar-term").textContent = info.solarTerm;
        document.getElementById("detail-truc-name").textContent = info.trucObj.name;

        document.getElementById("detail-good-hours").textContent = info.goodHoursStr;
        document.getElementById("detail-bad-hours").textContent = info.badHoursStr;
        document.getElementById("detail-truc-title").textContent = info.trucObj.name;
        document.getElementById("detail-truc-desc").textContent = info.trucObj.desc;
        document.getElementById("detail-age-good").textContent = info.ageHarmony.good;
        document.getElementById("detail-age-bad").textContent = info.ageHarmony.bad;

        const actionSet = TRUC_ACTIONS_MAP[info.trucObj.name] || TRUC_ACTIONS_MAP["Bế"];
        const doListEl = document.getElementById("detail-do-list");
        const dontListEl = document.getElementById("detail-dont-list");
        if (doListEl) {
            doListEl.innerHTML = actionSet.doList
                .map((item) => `<li class="fengshui-actions__chip">${item}</li>`)
                .join("");
        }
        if (dontListEl) {
            dontListEl.innerHTML = actionSet.dontList
                .map((item) => `<li class="fengshui-actions__chip">${item}</li>`)
                .join("");
        }

        updateHistoryForDay(sDay, sMonth);

        // 2. CHUẨN BỊ DỮ LIỆU NGỮ CẢNH NGÀY & 12 CANH GIỜ
        const sexagenaryIdx = (((6 * dayStemIdx - 5 * info.dayChiIdx) % 60) + 60) % 60;
        const hyDir = HY_THAN_BY_STEM[dayStemIdx] || "Tây Bắc";
        const taiDir = TAI_THAN_BY_STEM[dayStemIdx] || "Đông Nam";
        const hacDir = HAC_THAN_DIRECTIONS[Math.floor(sexagenaryIdx / 7) % 8] || "Chính Nam";
        const termRange = getSolarTermRangeInfo(sDay, sMonth, sYear);
        const dayLucDieuIdx = (info.lunarMonth + info.lunarDay - 2) % 6;
        const dayLucDieuObj = LUC_DIEU_6[dayLucDieuIdx] || LUC_DIEU_6[0];

        currentDayContext = {
            dayCanChi: info.dayCanChi,
            dayNapAm: info.dayNapAm,
            directions: { hy: hyDir, tai: taiDir, hac: hacDir },
            termRange,
        };

        const goodHourSet = new Set(info.goodHoursStr.split(",").map((s) => s.trim().split(" ")[0]));
        const baseThreeElements = [
            STEM_TO_ELEMENT[yearStemIdx],
            STEM_TO_ELEMENT[monthStemIdx],
            STEM_TO_ELEMENT[dayStemIdx],
        ];
        const allFiveKeys = ["fire", "earth", "metal", "water", "wood"];

        currentDayHoursData = HOUR_LABELS_12.map((h, hIdx) => {
            const hStemIdx = (ratHourStemIdx + hIdx) % 10;
            const hStemName = window.LunarEngine.THIEN_CAN[hStemIdx];
            const hCanChi = `${hStemName} ${h.chi}`;
            const hSexagenary = (((6 * hStemIdx - 5 * hIdx) % 60) + 60) % 60;
            const napAmObj = NAP_AM_30_INFO[Math.floor(hSexagenary / 2)] || NAP_AM_30_INFO[6];
            const hNapAm = napAmObj.name;

            let elemIcon = "earth.svg";
            let elemShort = "Hành Thổ";
            let napAmElementKey = "earth";
            if (hNapAm.endsWith("Kim")) {
                elemIcon = "metal.svg";
                elemShort = "Hành Kim";
                napAmElementKey = "metal";
            } else if (hNapAm.endsWith("Mộc")) {
                elemIcon = "wood.svg";
                elemShort = "Hành Mộc";
                napAmElementKey = "wood";
            } else if (hNapAm.endsWith("Thủy")) {
                elemIcon = "water.svg";
                elemShort = "Hành Thủy";
                napAmElementKey = "water";
            } else if (hNapAm.endsWith("Hỏa")) {
                elemIcon = "fire.svg";
                elemShort = "Hành Hỏa";
                napAmElementKey = "fire";
            }

            const harmonyObj = ELEMENT_HARMONY_MAP[napAmElementKey] || ELEMENT_HARMONY_MAP.earth;
            const starObj = LUC_DIEU_6[(dayLucDieuIdx + hIdx) % 6];
            const isHoangDao = goodHourSet.has(h.chi);
            const fortuneKey = isHoangDao ? "good" : "bad";

            const hourElementKey = STEM_TO_ELEMENT[hStemIdx];
            const activeElementsForHour = new Set([...baseThreeElements, hourElementKey]);

            const activeNames = allFiveKeys
                .filter((k) => activeElementsForHour.has(k))
                .map((k) => ELEMENT_VN_NAME[k])
                .join(", ");
            const missingNames =
                allFiveKeys
                    .filter((k) => !activeElementsForHour.has(k))
                    .map((k) => ELEMENT_VN_NAME[k])
                    .join(", ") || "Không khuyết";

            const baguaParts = h.bagua.split("•");
            const baguaName = baguaParts[0].trim();
            const baguaDir = baguaParts[1].trim();

            return {
                h,
                hIdx,
                hStemName,
                hCanChi,
                napAmObj,
                hNapAm,
                elemIcon,
                elemShort,
                napAmElementKey,
                harmonyObj,
                starObj,
                isHoangDao,
                fortuneKey,
                hourElementKey,
                activeElementsForHour,
                activeNames,
                missingNames,
                baguaName,
                baguaDir,
                yearCanChi: info.yearCanChi,
                monthCanChi: info.monthCanChi,
                dayCanChi: info.dayCanChi,
                dayNapAm: info.dayNapAm,
            };
        });

        // 3. CỘT PHẢI — KHỐI 1: HƯỚNG XUẤT HÀNH
        const dirGridEl = document.getElementById("day-directions-grid");
        if (dirGridEl) {
            const dirItems = [
                { code: "hy", mod: "hy", dirVal: hyDir },
                { code: "tai", mod: "tai", dirVal: taiDir },
                { code: "hac", mod: "hac", dirVal: hacDir },
            ];

            dirGridEl.innerHTML = dirItems
                .map(({ code, mod, dirVal }) => {
                    const d = DIRECTION_DEITIES[code];
                    return `
                        <a
                            href="./utilities.html?direction=${d.key}"
                            class="day-dir-box day-dir-box--${mod} has-bento-tooltip"
                            data-col-modal="direction"
                            data-extra-key="${code}"
                        >
                            <span class="day-dir-box__card-bg" aria-hidden="true">
                                <img src="${d.icon}" alt="" class="day-dir-box__watermark" />
                            </span>

                            <div class="day-dir-box__top">
                                <span class="day-dir-box__icon-wrap">
                                    <img src="${d.icon}" alt="" aria-hidden="true" />
                                </span>
                                <span class="day-dir-box__label">${d.name} (${code === "hac" ? "Xấu" : "Tốt"})</span>
                            </div>

                            <div class="day-dir-box__body">
                                <strong class="day-dir-box__value">${dirVal}</strong>
                                <span class="day-dir-box__sub">${d.sub}</span>
                            </div>

                            ${buildBentoTooltipHtml(
                                `${d.name} • Hướng ${dirVal}`,
                                `${d.desc} ${d.advice}`,
                                `✦ Bấm để mở thẻ giải nghĩa ${d.name}`,
                            )}
                        </a>
                    `;
                })
                .join("");
        }

        // 4. CỘT PHẢI — KHỐI 2: THÔNG TIN TIẾT KHÍ (GẮN data-term-anim CHO PHƯƠNG ÁN 1)
        const termSubEl = document.getElementById("day-term-summary-sub");
        if (termSubEl) {
            termSubEl.textContent = `Tiết ${termRange.name} (${termRange.season}) — ${termRange.meaning}`;
        }

        const termGridEl = document.getElementById("day-solarterm-grid");
        if (termGridEl) {
            const termIconPath = `./assets/icons/weather/${termRange.icon}`;
            const termHref = `./utilities.html?term=${toSlug(termRange.name)}`;

            const termBoxes = [
                {
                    label: "Tiết khí hiện tại",
                    val: termRange.name,
                    sub: termRange.season,
                    isPrimary: true,
                    tipTitle: `Tiết ${termRange.name}`,
                    tipDesc: `${termRange.meaning} ${termRange.advice}`,
                },
                {
                    label: "Ngày bắt đầu",
                    val: termRange.start,
                    sub: `Khởi tiết ${termRange.name}`,
                    isPrimary: false,
                    tipTitle: `Khởi đầu tiết ${termRange.name}`,
                    tipDesc: `Tiết ${termRange.name} bắt đầu từ ngày ${termRange.start}. ${termRange.meaning}`,
                },
                {
                    label: "Ngày kết thúc",
                    val: termRange.end,
                    sub: "Chuyển sang tiết kế tiếp",
                    isPrimary: false,
                    tipTitle: `Chu kỳ tiết ${termRange.name}`,
                    tipDesc: `Tiết ${termRange.name} kéo dài từ ${termRange.start} đến hết ngày ${termRange.end}. ${termRange.advice}`,
                },
            ];

            termGridEl.innerHTML = termBoxes
                .map(
                    (b) => `
                        <a
                            href="${termHref}"
                            class="day-solarterm-box ${b.isPrimary ? "day-solarterm-box--primary" : ""} has-bento-tooltip"
                            data-term-anim="${termRange.animGroup || "wind"}"
                            data-col-modal="solarterm"
                        >
                            <span class="day-solarterm-box__card-bg" aria-hidden="true">
                                <img src="${termIconPath}" alt="" class="day-solarterm-box__watermark" />
                            </span>

                            <div class="day-solarterm-box__top">
                                <span class="day-solarterm-box__icon-wrap">
                                    <img src="${termIconPath}" alt="" aria-hidden="true" />
                                </span>
                                <span class="day-solarterm-box__label">${b.label}</span>
                            </div>

                            <div class="day-solarterm-box__body">
                                <strong class="day-solarterm-box__val">${b.val}</strong>
                                <span class="day-solarterm-box__sub">${b.sub}</span>
                            </div>

                            ${buildBentoTooltipHtml(
                                b.tipTitle,
                                b.tipDesc,
                                `✦ Bấm để mở thẻ giải nghĩa Tiết ${termRange.name}`,
                            )}
                        </a>
                    `,
                )
                .join("");
        }

        // 5. CỘT PHẢI — KHỐI 3: KHỔNG MINH LỤC DIỆU (6 ANIMATION ICON RIÊNG BIỆT)
        document.getElementById("day-lucdieu-day-badge").textContent = dayLucDieuObj.name;
        document.getElementById("day-lucdieu-summary").textContent =
            `Ngày ${dayLucDieuObj.name} — ${dayLucDieuObj.desc}`;

        const lucDieuGridEl = document.getElementById("day-lucdieu-grid");
        if (lucDieuGridEl) {
            lucDieuGridEl.innerHTML = currentDayHoursData
                .map((rowData) => {
                    const { h, hIdx, hCanChi, starObj, harmonyObj, elemShort } = rowData;
                    return `
                        <a
                            href="./utilities.html?lucdieu=${starObj.slug}"
                            class="day-lucdieu-cell ${starObj.isGood ? "day-lucdieu-cell--good" : "day-lucdieu-cell--bad"} has-bento-tooltip"
                            data-lucdieu-slug="${starObj.slug}"
                            data-col-modal="lucdieu"
                            data-hour-idx="${hIdx}"
                        >
                            <span class="day-lucdieu-cell__card-bg" aria-hidden="true">
                                <img src="${starObj.icon}" alt="" class="day-lucdieu-cell__watermark" />
                            </span>

                            <div class="day-lucdieu-cell__head">
                                <strong class="day-lucdieu-cell__name">${starObj.name}</strong>
                                <img src="${starObj.icon}" alt="" aria-hidden="true" class="day-lucdieu-cell__icon" />
                            </div>

                            <span class="day-lucdieu-cell__time">Giờ ${h.chi} (${h.short}h)</span>

                            <span class="day-lucdieu-cell__harmony">
                                Hợp: <strong>${harmonyObj.good}</strong>
                            </span>

                            ${buildBentoTooltipHtml(
                                `Sao ${starObj.name} • Giờ ${hCanChi}`,
                                `${starObj.desc} Giờ thuộc ${elemShort}: Tốt cho người mệnh ${harmonyObj.good}, lưu ý mệnh ${harmonyObj.bad}.`,
                                `✦ Bấm để xem luận giải cung ${starObj.name}`,
                            )}
                        </a>
                    `;
                })
                .join("");
        }

        // 6. CỘT PHẢI — KHỐI 4: MẶT TRỜI 4 MIỀN
        const sunInfo = computeSunTimes4Regions(sDay, sMonth, sYear);
        document.getElementById("day-sun-date-label").textContent = `${pad(sDay)}/${pad(sMonth)}/${sYear}`;
        document.getElementById("day-sun-length-label").textContent = sunInfo.dayLengthLabel;

        const sunRowsEl = document.getElementById("day-sun-rows");
        if (sunRowsEl) {
            sunRowsEl.innerHTML = sunInfo.rows
                .map(
                    (r) => `
                        <div class="day-sun-table__row" role="row">
                            <span class="day-sun-table__region" role="cell">${r.region}</span>
                            <span class="day-sun-table__place" role="cell">${r.place}</span>
                            <span class="day-sun-table__time" role="cell">
                                <img
                                    src="./assets/icons/weather/sun-rise.svg"
                                    alt=""
                                    aria-hidden="true"
                                    class="day-sun-table__time-icon"
                                />
                                <span>${r.riseStr}</span>
                            </span>
                            <span class="day-sun-table__time" role="cell">
                                <img
                                    src="./assets/icons/weather/sun-set.svg"
                                    alt=""
                                    aria-hidden="true"
                                    class="day-sun-table__time-icon"
                                />
                                <span>${r.setStr}</span>
                            </span>
                        </div>
                    `,
                )
                .join("");
        }

        // 7. TẦNG DƯỚI: 12 CANH GIỜ (ĐỒNG BỘ ANIMATION LỤC DIỆU & HOÀNG/HẮC ĐẠO Ở CỘT 3)
        const hourlyListEl = document.getElementById("day-hourly-list");
        if (hourlyListEl) {
            hourlyListEl.innerHTML = currentDayHoursData
                .map((rowData) => {
                    const {
                        h,
                        hIdx,
                        hCanChi,
                        napAmObj,
                        hNapAm,
                        elemIcon,
                        elemShort,
                        harmonyObj,
                        starObj,
                        isHoangDao,
                        fortuneKey,
                        hourElementKey,
                        activeElementsForHour,
                        activeNames,
                        missingNames,
                        baguaName,
                        baguaDir,
                    } = rowData;

                    const wuxingSvg = buildWuXingSvgHtml(activeElementsForHour, hourElementKey, false);
                    const daoIconPath = isHoangDao
                        ? "./assets/icons/fengshui/hoang-dao.svg"
                        : "./assets/icons/fengshui/hac-dao.svg";

                    return `
                        <article class="day-hour-row" data-hour-fortune="${fortuneKey}">
                            <!-- CỘT 1: CANH GIỜ -->
                            <a
                                href="./clock.html?branch=${h.key}"
                                class="day-hour-row__col-link day-hour-row__col-time has-bento-tooltip"
                                data-col-modal="time"
                                data-hour-idx="${hIdx}"
                            >
                                <strong>${hCanChi}</strong>
                                <span>${h.range}</span>
                                ${buildBentoTooltipHtml(
                                    `Giờ ${hCanChi} (${h.range})`,
                                    `${isHoangDao ? "Giờ Hoàng Đạo (Cát thần bảo hộ)." : "Giờ Hắc Đạo (Nên cẩn trọng việc lớn)."} Hợp mệnh ${harmonyObj.good}, kỵ mệnh ${harmonyObj.bad}.`,
                                    "✦ Bấm để mở thẻ luận giải Canh Giờ",
                                )}
                            </a>

                            <!-- CỘT 2: NẠP ÂM NGŨ HÀNH -->
                            <a
                                href="./utilities.html?napam=${toSlug(hNapAm)}"
                                class="day-hour-row__col-link day-hour-row__col-napam has-bento-tooltip"
                                data-col-modal="napam"
                                data-hour-idx="${hIdx}"
                            >
                                <span class="day-hour-row__napam-icon-box">
                                    <img src="./assets/icons/${elemIcon}" alt="" aria-hidden="true" />
                                </span>
                                <span class="day-hour-row__napam-info">
                                    <strong>${hNapAm}</strong>
                                    <small>${elemShort} • Hợp: ${harmonyObj.good}</small>
                                </span>
                                ${buildBentoTooltipHtml(
                                    hNapAm,
                                    `${napAmObj.meaning} Rất tốt cho người mệnh ${harmonyObj.good}.`,
                                    "✦ Bấm để mở thẻ giải nghĩa Nạp Âm",
                                )}
                            </a>

                            <!-- CỘT 3: KHỔNG MINH LỤC DIỆU & ICON SVG HOÀNG/HẮC ĐẠO (CÓ ANIMATION ĐỒNG BỘ) -->
                            <a
                                href="./utilities.html?lucdieu=${starObj.slug}"
                                class="day-hour-row__col-link day-hour-row__col-lucdieu has-bento-tooltip"
                                data-col-modal="lucdieu"
                                data-hour-idx="${hIdx}"
                            >
                                <span
                                    class="day-hour-row__badge ${starObj.isGood ? "day-hour-row__badge--good" : "day-hour-row__badge--bad"}"
                                    data-lucdieu-slug="${starObj.slug}"
                                >
                                    <img src="${starObj.icon}" alt="" aria-hidden="true" />
                                    <span>${starObj.name}</span>
                                </span>
                                <span class="day-hour-row__dao-sub">
                                    <img
                                        src="${daoIconPath}"
                                        alt=""
                                        aria-hidden="true"
                                        class="day-hour-row__dao-icon"
                                        data-dao-type="${fortuneKey}"
                                    />
                                    <span>${isHoangDao ? "Giờ Hoàng Đạo" : "Giờ Hắc Đạo"}</span>
                                </span>
                                ${buildBentoTooltipHtml(
                                    `Sao ${starObj.name}`,
                                    `${starObj.desc} ${starObj.advice}`,
                                    "✦ Bấm để mở thẻ lời khuyên Lục Diệu",
                                )}
                            </a>

                            <!-- CỘT 4: VÒNG SVG NGŨ HÀNH ĐỘNG -->
                            <a
                                href="./bazi.html?date=${isoDateStr}&hour=${h.key}"
                                class="day-hour-row__col-link day-hour-row__col-wuxing has-bento-tooltip"
                                data-col-modal="wuxing"
                                data-hour-idx="${hIdx}"
                            >
                                ${wuxingSvg}
                                ${buildBentoTooltipHtml(
                                    `Ngũ Hành Tứ Trụ Giờ ${hCanChi}`,
                                    `Hội tụ (Sáng): ${activeNames} • Đang khuyết (Chìm): ${missingNames}.`,
                                    "✦ Bấm để mở biểu đồ Bát Tự Tứ Trụ",
                                )}
                            </a>

                            <!-- CỘT 5: LINH VẬT & QUẺ BÁT QUÁI -->
                            <a
                                href="./utilities.html?bagua=${toSlug(baguaName)}"
                                class="day-hour-row__col-link day-hour-row__col-bagua has-bento-tooltip"
                                data-col-modal="bagua"
                                data-hour-idx="${hIdx}"
                            >
                                <span class="day-hour-row__bagua-ring">
                                    <img src="./assets/icons/zodiac/${h.icon}" alt="" aria-hidden="true" />
                                </span>
                                <span class="day-hour-row__bagua-text">
                                    <strong>Quẻ ${baguaName}</strong>
                                    <small>Hướng ${baguaDir}</small>
                                </span>
                                ${buildBentoTooltipHtml(
                                    `Quẻ ${baguaName} • Hướng ${baguaDir}`,
                                    `${h.baguaDesc} (${h.animalName}).`,
                                    "✦ Bấm để mở thẻ phương vị Bát Quái",
                                )}
                            </a>
                        </article>
                    `;
                })
                .join("");

            applyHourRowsFilter();
        }
    };

    const initDayCalendarPage = () => {
        const prevBtn = document.getElementById("day-prev-btn");
        const nextBtn = document.getElementById("day-next-btn");
        const todayBtn = document.getElementById("day-today-btn");
        const sameDayBtn = document.getElementById("btn-same-day-events");
        const intlBtn = document.getElementById("btn-intl-events");
        const shareBtn = document.getElementById("day-share-btn");
        const mainDayContainer = document.getElementById("main");

        if (prevBtn) {
            prevBtn.onclick = () => {
                const prev = new Date(viewingDate);
                prev.setDate(prev.getDate() - 1);
                renderDayPage(prev);
            };
        }

        if (nextBtn) {
            nextBtn.onclick = () => {
                const next = new Date(viewingDate);
                next.setDate(next.getDate() + 1);
                renderDayPage(next);
            };
        }

        if (todayBtn) {
            todayBtn.onclick = () => renderDayPage(new Date());
        }

        if (sameDayBtn) {
            sameDayBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                e.stopPropagation();
                if (!activeDayVnEvents.length) return;
                currentVnEventIndex = (currentVnEventIndex + 1) % activeDayVnEvents.length;
                sameDayBtn.classList.add("is-active");
                intlBtn?.classList.remove("is-active");
                renderHistoryItemToBloc(
                    activeDayVnEvents[currentVnEventIndex],
                    viewingDate.getMonth() + 1,
                    viewingDate.getDate(),
                );
            };
        }

        if (intlBtn) {
            intlBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                e.stopPropagation();
                if (!activeDayIntlEvents.length) return;
                const nextItem = activeDayIntlEvents[currentIntlEventIndex];
                currentIntlEventIndex = (currentIntlEventIndex + 1) % activeDayIntlEvents.length;
                intlBtn.classList.add("is-active");
                sameDayBtn?.classList.remove("is-active");
                renderHistoryItemToBloc(nextItem, viewingDate.getMonth() + 1, viewingDate.getDate());
            };
        }

        if (shareBtn) {
            shareBtn.onclick = async () => {
                const y = viewingDate.getFullYear();
                const m = pad(viewingDate.getMonth() + 1);
                const d = pad(viewingDate.getDate());
                const shareUrl = `${window.location.origin}${window.location.pathname}?date=${y}-${m}-${d}`;
                try {
                    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareUrl);
                } catch (e) {
                    // Fallback
                }
            };
        }

        if (mainDayContainer) {
            mainDayContainer.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
                const linkEl = e.target.closest("[data-col-modal]");
                if (!linkEl) return;
                if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.shiftKey || e.button === 1) return;

                e.preventDefault();
                const colType = linkEl.dataset.colModal;
                const hourIdx = Number(linkEl.dataset.hourIdx || 0);
                const extraKey = linkEl.dataset.extraKey || "";
                const seoHref = linkEl.getAttribute("href");
                openDayTermModal(colType, hourIdx, seoHref, extraKey);
            });
        }

        document.getElementById("day-term-modal-close")?.addEventListener("click", closeDayTermModal);
        document.getElementById("day-term-modal-cancel")?.addEventListener("click", closeDayTermModal);
        document.getElementById("day-term-modal-backdrop")?.addEventListener("click", closeDayTermModal);

        document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
            const modal = document.getElementById("day-term-modal");
            if (e.key === "Escape" && modal && !modal.hidden) {
                closeDayTermModal();
            }
        });

        document.querySelectorAll(".day-hourly-section__filter-btn").forEach((btn) => {
            btn.onclick = () => {
                activeHourFilter = btn.dataset.dayHourFilter || "all";
                document.querySelectorAll(".day-hourly-section__filter-btn").forEach((b) => {
                    b.classList.toggle("is-active", b === btn);
                });
                applyHourRowsFilter();
            };
        });

        window.addEventListener("converter-jump-to-date", (e) => {
            if (e.detail) {
                renderDayPage(new Date(e.detail.year, e.detail.month - 1, e.detail.day));
            }
        });

        const urlDateParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("date");
        if (urlDateParam && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(urlDateParam)) {
            const [y, m, d] = urlDateParam.split("-").map(Number);
            renderDayPage(new Date(y, m - 1, d));
        } else {
            renderDayPage(new Date());
        }
    };

    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", initDayCalendarPage);
})();
```

### detail.js

```js
// assets/js/detail.js
// Chuyên trách Giai đoạn 4 (detail.html): Đọc tham số URL, nạp ./api/thang-${month}.json,
// đồng bộ 4 Bento Card Con Giáp/Ngũ Hành, Bộ lọc sự kiện cùng tháng & Lightbox phóng to ảnh
// ĐÃ FIX: Khóa scrollRestoration và thay scrollIntoView() bằng scrollTop nội bộ để trang không tự cuộn lên trên
(() => {
    const DEFAULT_COVER_IMG = "./assets/images/calendar-covers/thang-11/nguyen-thi-minh-khai-01-11-1910-28-08-1941.jpg";

    let allMonthItemsCache = [];
    let currentSelectedItem = null;
    let currentEventMonth = 11;
    let activeRelatedFilter = "all"; // "all" | "vn" | "intl"

    const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");

    // 1. Tắt chế độ tự ghi nhớ vị trí cuộn cũ của trình duyệt & đưa trang về đỉnh tuyệt đối (top: 0)
    if ("scrollRestoration" in window.history) {
        window.history.scrollRestoration = "manual";
    }
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, left: 0, behavior: "instant" });

    const fetchMonthData = async (monthNum) => {
        const m = Number(monthNum) || 11;
        const candidatePaths = [`./api/thang-${m}.json`, `./api/thang-${pad(m)}.json`];

        for (const path of candidatePaths) {
            try {
                const res = await fetch(path);
                if (res.ok) {
                    const data = await res.json();
                    return Array.isArray(data) ? data : data?.data || data?.events || [];
                }
            } catch (err) {
                // Thử đường dẫn tiếp theo
            }
        }
        return [];
    };

    const parseDayMonthFromItem = (item, fallbackMonth) => {
        const displayDate = String(item?.ngay_hien_thi || item?.ngay_duong || "").trim();
        if (/^\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}/.test(displayDate)) {
            const [d, m] = displayDate.split("/").map(Number);
            return { day: d || 1, month: m || fallbackMonth };
        }
        const slugStr = String(item?.image_slug || "");
        const match = slugStr.match(/-(\d{2})-(\d{2})-\d{4}/);
        if (match) {
            return { day: Number(match[1]) || 1, month: Number(match[2]) || fallbackMonth };
        }
        return { day: Number(item?.day) || 1, month: fallbackMonth };
    };

    const isInternationalEvent = (entry) => {
        const scope = String(entry?.pham_vi || entry?.scope || entry?.category_id || "").toLowerCase();
        return Boolean(entry?.is_international) || scope.includes("quoc-te") || scope.includes("international");
    };

    const buildDetailHref = (item, monthNum) => {
        if (!item) return "./index.html";
        const rawSlug = item.image_slug || item.slug || "";
        const cleanFolder = rawSlug.replace(/-\d{2}-\d{2}-\d{4}.*$/, "") || rawSlug;
        const catStr = String(item.category_id || item.loai || "").toLowerCase();
        const typeFolder = catStr.includes("event") || catStr.includes("su-kien") ? "events" : "figures";

        const params = new URLSearchParams({
            type: typeFolder,
            folder: cleanFolder,
            slug: rawSlug,
            month: String(monthNum),
        });
        return `./detail.html?${params.toString()}`;
    };

    // Điều khiển Lightbox phóng to ảnh
    const openLightbox = (imgSrc, captionText) => {
        const lightbox = document.getElementById("detail-lightbox");
        const lightboxImg = document.getElementById("detail-lightbox-img");
        const lightboxCap = document.getElementById("detail-lightbox-caption");
        if (!lightbox || !lightboxImg) return;

        lightboxImg.src = imgSrc;
        lightboxImg.alt = captionText || "Hình ảnh tư liệu lịch sử";
        if (lightboxCap) lightboxCap.textContent = captionText || "";

        lightbox.hidden = false;
        document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    };

    const closeLightbox = () => {
        const lightbox = document.getElementById("detail-lightbox");
        if (!lightbox) return;
        lightbox.hidden = true;
        document.body.style.overflow = "";
    };

    const setHeroImageWithFallback = (imgEl, typeFolder, actorFolder, monthNum, rawSlug, altText) => {
        if (!imgEl) return;
        if (altText) imgEl.alt = altText;

        const cleanSlug = String(rawSlug || "")
            .split("/")
            .pop()
            .replace(/\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/i, "");

        const candidateUrls = [
            `./assets/images/calendar-covers/thang-${monthNum}/${cleanSlug}.jpg`,
            `./assets/images/calendar-covers/thang-${monthNum}/${cleanSlug}.webp`,
            `./assets/images/${typeFolder}/${actorFolder}/${actorFolder}.webp`,
            `./assets/images/${typeFolder}/${actorFolder}/${actorFolder}.jpg`,
            `./assets/images/${typeFolder}/${actorFolder}/1.webp`,
            `./assets/images/${typeFolder}/${actorFolder}/1.jpg`,
            DEFAULT_COVER_IMG,
        ];

        let idx = 0;
        const tryNext = () => {
            if (idx >= candidateUrls.length) {
                imgEl.onerror = null;
                return;
            }
            imgEl.src = candidateUrls[idx++];
        };

        imgEl.onerror = tryNext;
        tryNext();
    };

    // THAY THẾ HÀM renderActorGallery TRONG assets/js/detail.js
    const renderActorGallery = (item, typeFolder, actorFolder, monthNum, rawSlug) => {
        const gridEl = document.getElementById("article-gallery-grid");
        if (!gridEl) return;

        const folderBasePath = `./assets/images/${typeFolder}/${actorFolder}`;
        const cleanSlug = String(rawSlug || "")
            .split("/")
            .pop()
            .replace(/\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/i, "");
        const coverUrl = `./assets/images/calendar-covers/thang-${monthNum}/${cleanSlug}.jpg`;

        const customImages = Array.isArray(item?.images)
            ? item.images
            : Array.isArray(item?.gallery)
              ? item.gallery
              : [];

        const galleryCards = [
            {
                primaryUrl: `${folderBasePath}/${actorFolder}.webp`,
                fallback1: `${folderBasePath}/1.jpg`,
                fallback2: coverUrl,
                badge: "Tư liệu",
                caption: item?.ten || actorFolder,
            },
            {
                primaryUrl: customImages[0] ? `${folderBasePath}/${customImages[0]}` : `${folderBasePath}/2.webp`,
                fallback1: `${folderBasePath}/2.jpg`,
                fallback2: "",
                badge: "Mở rộng",
                caption: item?.hien_thi_nhan_dip || "Tư liệu lịch sử lưu trữ",
            },
        ];

        gridEl.classList.remove("detail-gallery__grid--single");

        gridEl.innerHTML = galleryCards
            .map(
                (card) => `
                    <figure class="detail-gallery__item js-gallery-figure" data-caption="${card.caption}">
                        <img
                            src="${card.primaryUrl}"
                            data-fallback-one="${card.fallback1}"
                            data-fallback-two="${card.fallback2}"
                            alt="${card.caption}"
                            class="detail-gallery__img js-gallery-img"
                            loading="lazy"
                        />
                        <figcaption class="detail-gallery__caption">
                            <span class="detail-gallery__caption-badge" aria-hidden="true">${card.badge}</span>
                            <span class="detail-gallery__caption-text">${card.caption}</span>
                        </figcaption>
                    </figure>
                `,
            )
            .join("");

        gridEl.querySelectorAll(".js-gallery-img").forEach((img) => {
            let step = 0;
            img.onerror = () => {
                step++;
                if (step === 1 && img.dataset.fallbackOne) {
                    img.src = img.dataset.fallbackOne;
                } else if (step === 2 && img.dataset.fallbackTwo) {
                    img.src = img.dataset.fallbackTwo;
                } else {
                    img.onerror = null;
                    const parentFig = img.closest(".js-gallery-figure");
                    if (parentFig) parentFig.remove();
                    if (gridEl.children.length === 1) {
                        gridEl.classList.add("detail-gallery__grid--single");
                    }
                }
            };
        });

        gridEl.querySelectorAll(".js-gallery-figure").forEach((fig) => {
            fig.onclick = () => {
                const img = fig.querySelector("img");
                if (img?.src) openLightbox(img.src, fig.dataset.caption);
            };
        });
    };

    // Vẽ danh sách sự kiện cùng tháng ở Cột Phải (CHỈ cuộn nội bộ thẻ #related-events-list, KHÔNG cuộn window)
    const renderRelatedSidebarList = () => {
        const relatedCountEl = document.getElementById("related-events-count");
        const relatedListEl = document.getElementById("related-events-list");
        if (!relatedListEl) return;

        const filteredItems = allMonthItemsCache.filter((entry) => {
            if (activeRelatedFilter === "vn") return !isInternationalEvent(entry);
            if (activeRelatedFilter === "intl") return isInternationalEvent(entry);
            return true;
        });

        if (relatedCountEl) relatedCountEl.textContent = `${filteredItems.length} mục`;

        relatedListEl.innerHTML = filteredItems
            .map((item) => {
                const dm = parseDayMonthFromItem(item, currentEventMonth);
                const isCurrent = item === currentSelectedItem;
                const href = buildDetailHref(item, currentEventMonth);
                return `
                    <a
                        href="${href}"
                        class="detail-sidebar__item ${isCurrent ? "is-current" : ""}"
                        ${isCurrent ? 'aria-current="page"' : ""}
                    >
                        <div class="detail-sidebar__date-box">
                            <small>T${dm.month}</small>
                            <strong>${pad(dm.day)}</strong>
                        </div>
                        <div class="detail-sidebar__item-info">
                            <strong class="detail-sidebar__item-name">${item.ten || "Sự kiện lịch sử"}</strong>
                            <span class="detail-sidebar__item-occasion">${item.hien_thi_nhan_dip || ""}</span>
                        </div>
                    </a>
                `;
            })
            .join("");

        // ✅ ĐÃ FIX: Chỉ tính toán scrollTop bên trong khung #related-events-list, tuyệt đối không dùng scrollIntoView()
        const activeSidebarItem = relatedListEl.querySelector(".detail-sidebar__item.is-current");
        if (activeSidebarItem) {
            const targetScrollTop =
                activeSidebarItem.offsetTop -
                relatedListEl.offsetTop -
                relatedListEl.clientHeight / 2 +
                activeSidebarItem.clientHeight / 2;

            relatedListEl.scrollTo({
                top: Math.max(0, targetScrollTop),
                behavior: "smooth",
            });
        }
    };

    const initDetailPage = async () => {
        // Đảm bảo cửa sổ trình duyệt luôn ở vị trí trên cùng (top: 0) khi bắt đầu khởi tạo trang
        window.scrollTo({ top: 0, left: 0, behavior: "instant" });

        const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        const typeParam = params.get("type") || "figures";
        const folderParam = params.get("folder") || "nguyen-thi-minh-khai";
        const slugParam = params.get("slug") || "nguyen-thi-minh-khai-01-11-1910-28-08-1941";
        const monthParam = Number(params.get("month")) || 11;
        const dayParam = Number(params.get("day")) || null;

        currentEventMonth = monthParam;
        allMonthItemsCache = await fetchMonthData(monthParam);

        currentSelectedItem =
            allMonthItemsCache.find((entry) => entry.image_slug === slugParam || entry.slug === slugParam) ||
            allMonthItemsCache.find((entry) => String(entry.image_slug || "").startsWith(folderParam)) ||
            (dayParam
                ? allMonthItemsCache.find((entry) => {
                      const dm = parseDayMonthFromItem(entry, monthParam);
                      return dm.day === dayParam;
                  })
                : null) ||
            allMonthItemsCache[0] ||
            null;

        const { day: eventDay, month: eventMonth } = parseDayMonthFromItem(currentSelectedItem, monthParam);
        const currentYear = 2026;
        const calendarJumpUrl = `./index.html?date=${currentYear}-${pad(eventMonth)}-${pad(eventDay)}`;

        document.getElementById("detail-back-link")?.setAttribute("href", calendarJumpUrl);
        document.getElementById("article-view-on-cal-btn")?.setAttribute("href", calendarJumpUrl);

        // Nút Sự kiện Trước / Tiếp theo trên thanh điều hướng đầu trang
        if (allMonthItemsCache.length > 0 && currentSelectedItem) {
            const currentIndex = allMonthItemsCache.indexOf(currentSelectedItem);
            const prevIndex = (currentIndex - 1 + allMonthItemsCache.length) % allMonthItemsCache.length;
            const nextIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % allMonthItemsCache.length;

            document
                .getElementById("detail-prev-item-btn")
                ?.setAttribute("href", buildDetailHref(allMonthItemsCache[prevIndex], eventMonth));
            document
                .getElementById("detail-next-item-btn")
                ?.setAttribute("href", buildDetailHref(allMonthItemsCache[nextIndex], eventMonth));
        }

        const isEventCategory =
            typeParam === "events" ||
            String(currentSelectedItem?.category_id || "")
                .toLowerCase()
                .includes("event") ||
            String(currentSelectedItem?.category_id || "")
                .toLowerCase()
                .includes("su-kien");

        const titleText = currentSelectedItem?.ten || "Nguyễn Thị Minh Khai";
        const occasionText =
            currentSelectedItem?.hien_thi_nhan_dip ||
            `Sự kiện & Nhân vật lịch sử ngày ${pad(eventDay)}/${pad(eventMonth)}`;
        const rawSlug = currentSelectedItem?.image_slug || slugParam;
        const resolvedFolder = rawSlug.replace(/-\d{2}-\d{2}-\d{4}.*$/, "") || folderParam;

        document.title = `${titleText} - Lịch Phong Thủy`;

        document.getElementById("article-cat-badge").textContent = isEventCategory
            ? "Sự kiện lịch sử"
            : "Nhân vật lịch sử";
        document.getElementById("article-date-badge").textContent = `Ngày ${pad(eventDay)}/${pad(eventMonth)}`;
        document.getElementById("article-occasion-text").textContent = occasionText;
        document.getElementById("article-main-title").textContent = titleText;
        document.getElementById("article-type-label").textContent = isEventCategory
            ? "Sự kiện Trọng đại"
            : "Danh nhân Việt Nam";

        const quoteTextEl = document.getElementById("article-quote-text");
        const quoteAuthorEl = document.getElementById("article-quote-author");
        const rawQuote = (currentSelectedItem?.quote || currentSelectedItem?.danh_ngon || "").trim();
        if (quoteTextEl) {
            quoteTextEl.textContent = rawQuote
                ? rawQuote.startsWith('"')
                    ? rawQuote
                    : `"${rawQuote}"`
                : `"${occasionText}"`;
        }
        if (quoteAuthorEl) {
            quoteAuthorEl.textContent = `— ${currentSelectedItem?.tac_gia || titleText}`;
        }

        // Đồng bộ 4 Bento Stat Cards bằng window.LunarEngine (Con Giáp + Ngũ Hành)
        document.getElementById("article-solar-date").textContent =
            `${pad(eventDay)}/${pad(eventMonth)}/${currentYear}`;

        if (window.LunarEngine) {
            const info = window.LunarEngine.getFullDayInfo(eventDay, eventMonth, currentYear);

            document.getElementById("article-weekday-sub").textContent = info.bilingualWeekday;
            document.getElementById("article-lunar-date").textContent =
                `${pad(info.lunarDay)}/${pad(info.lunarMonth)} ${info.yearCanChi}`;
            document.getElementById("article-lunar-month-sub").textContent = info.lunarMonthFullLabel;

            const yearZodiacPath = `./assets/icons/zodiac/${info.yearZodiacFile}.svg`;
            document.getElementById("article-year-zodiac-icon")?.setAttribute("src", yearZodiacPath);
            document.getElementById("article-year-zodiac-bg")?.setAttribute("src", yearZodiacPath);

            const dayZodiacPath = `./assets/icons/zodiac/${info.zodiacFile}.svg`;
            document.getElementById("article-day-zodiac-icon")?.setAttribute("src", dayZodiacPath);
            document.getElementById("article-day-zodiac-bg")?.setAttribute("src", dayZodiacPath);
            document.getElementById("article-canchi-date").textContent = `Ngày ${info.dayCanChi}`;
            document.getElementById("article-fortune-sub").textContent =
                info.fortuneStatus === "good"
                    ? "🌟 Ngày Hoàng Đạo"
                    : info.fortuneStatus === "bad"
                      ? "🌙 Ngày Hắc Đạo"
                      : `Tháng ${info.monthCanChi}`;

            document
                .getElementById("article-element-icon")
                ?.setAttribute("src", `./assets/icons/${info.elementInfo.icon}`);
            document.getElementById("article-napam-val").textContent = info.dayNapAm;
            document.getElementById("article-term-sub").textContent =
                `Tiết ${info.solarTerm} • Trực ${info.trucObj.name}`;
        }

        // Nạp ảnh Hero 16:9 & Gắn sự kiện phóng to Lightbox
        const heroImgEl = document.getElementById("article-hero-img");
        const heroFigureEl = document.getElementById("detail-hero-figure");
        setHeroImageWithFallback(
            heroImgEl,
            isEventCategory ? "events" : "figures",
            resolvedFolder,
            eventMonth,
            rawSlug,
            titleText,
        );

        if (heroFigureEl && heroImgEl) {
            heroFigureEl.onclick = () => openLightbox(heroImgEl.src, occasionText);
        }

        // Đổ nội dung bài viết
        const bodyEl = document.getElementById("article-body-content");
        if (bodyEl) {
            const rawDesc =
                currentSelectedItem?.mo_ta ||
                currentSelectedItem?.noi_dung ||
                currentSelectedItem?.description ||
                occasionText;
            const paragraphs = String(rawDesc)
                .split(/\n+/)
                .map((p) => p.trim())
                .filter(Boolean);

            bodyEl.innerHTML = paragraphs.map((p) => `<p>${p}</p>`).join("");
        }

        renderActorGallery(
            currentSelectedItem,
            isEventCategory ? "events" : "figures",
            resolvedFolder,
            eventMonth,
            rawSlug,
        );

        const sourcesListEl = document.getElementById("article-sources-list");
        if (sourcesListEl) {
            const sources = Array.isArray(currentSelectedItem?.historical_sources)
                ? currentSelectedItem.historical_sources
                : currentSelectedItem?.nguon_su_lieu
                  ? [currentSelectedItem.nguon_su_lieu]
                  : ["Đại cương Lịch sử Việt Nam", "Bảo tàng Lịch sử Quốc gia Việt Nam"];

            sourcesListEl.innerHTML = sources
                .map((src) => {
                    const label =
                        typeof src === "string" ? src : src.ten_nguon || src.title || src.name || "Tài liệu lịch sử";
                    return `<li class="detail-sources__item">${label}</li>`;
                })
                .join("");
        }

        // Bộ lọc Tất cả / Việt Nam / Quốc tế trên Cột Phải
        document.getElementById("related-month-title").textContent = `Sự kiện Tháng ${eventMonth}`;
        document.querySelectorAll(".detail-sidebar__filter-btn").forEach((btn) => {
            btn.onclick = () => {
                activeRelatedFilter = btn.dataset.relatedFilter || "all";
                document.querySelectorAll(".detail-sidebar__filter-btn").forEach((b) => {
                    b.classList.toggle("is-active", b === btn);
                });
                renderRelatedSidebarList();
            };
        });

        renderRelatedSidebarList();

        // Nút Sao chép liên kết chia sẻ
        const copyBtn = document.getElementById("detail-copy-link-btn");
        const copyLabel = document.getElementById("detail-copy-label");
        if (copyBtn && copyLabel) {
            copyBtn.onclick = async () => {
                try {
                    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(window.location.href);
                    copyLabel.textContent = "Đã sao chép!";
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        copyLabel.textContent = "Chia sẻ";
                    }, 2000);
                } catch (e) {
                    copyLabel.textContent = "Chia sẻ";
                }
            };
        }

        // Sự kiện đóng Lightbox
        document.getElementById("detail-lightbox-close")?.addEventListener("click", closeLightbox);
        document.getElementById("detail-lightbox-backdrop")?.addEventListener("click", closeLightbox);
        document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
            if (e.key === "Escape") closeLightbox();
        });
    };

    // Giữ trạng thái Active cho mục "Ngày - tháng" trên Sidebar khi đang ở trang detail.html
    const keepSidebarActiveOnDetail = () => {
        const homeSidebarLink = document.querySelector('.sidebar__link[data-nav-page="index.html"]');
        if (homeSidebarLink) {
            homeSidebarLink.classList.add("sidebar__link--active");
            homeSidebarLink.setAttribute("aria-current", "page");
        }
    };

    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
        initDetailPage();
        keepSidebarActiveOnDetail();
    });
    window.addEventListener("template-loaded", keepSidebarActiveOnDetail);
})();
```

### festival-calendar.js

```js
// assets/js/festival-calendar.js
// Bản Sạch Hoàn Chỉnh (Không có listener scroll gây giật + Đã đo tự động --countdown-exact-h khóa chuẩn Gap 2rem)
(() => {
    let countdownInterval = null;
    const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
    const viWeekdayFormatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("vi-VN", { weekday: "long" });

    // 1. Tự động lấp đầy hàng cuối của Lưới Bento 3 cột
    const syncBentoRowFill = (visibleTiles) => {
        visibleTiles.forEach((t) => t.removeAttribute("data-fill-span"));
        if (!visibleTiles.length) return;

        const cols = window.innerWidth <= 991 ? 2 : 3;
        const totalUnits = visibleTiles.reduce((sum, tile) => sum + (tile.dataset.major === "true" ? 4 : 1), 0);
        const remainder = totalUnits % cols;
        if (remainder === 0) return;

        const neededExtraCols = cols - remainder;
        const normalTiles = visibleTiles.filter((t) => t.dataset.major !== "true");
        const lastNormal = normalTiles[normalTiles.length - 1];

        if (lastNormal && neededExtraCols <= 2) {
            lastNormal.dataset.fillSpan = String(1 + neededExtraCols);
        }
    };

    // 2. Tính ngày mục tiêu trong cửa sổ 365 ngày tới (Nếu sự kiện năm nay đã qua, tự động tính kỳ lễ năm tiếp theo)
    const resolveSmartCountdownTarget = (tile) => {
        const now = new Date();
        const todayStart = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());

        const [origY, origM, origD] = String(tile.dataset.date || "2026-11-20")
            .split("-")
            .map(Number);
        let targetDate = new Date(origY, origM - 1, origD, 0, 0, 0);
        let isRolloverNextYear = false;

        if (targetDate < todayStart) {
            isRolloverNextYear = true;
            const nextYear = now.getFullYear() + 1;

            if (tile.dataset.calendarType === "lunar" && window.LunarEngine && tile.dataset.lunarDay) {
                const lDay = Number(tile.dataset.lunarDay);
                const lMonth = Number(tile.dataset.lunarMonth);
                const lYearToConvert = lMonth === 12 ? nextYear - 1 : nextYear;
                const conv = window.LunarEngine.convertLunar2Solar(lDay, lMonth, lYearToConvert, 0);
                if (conv && conv.sDay > 0) {
                    targetDate = new Date(conv.sYear, conv.sMonth - 1, conv.sDay, 0, 0, 0);
                } else {
                    targetDate = new Date(nextYear, origM - 1, origD, 0, 0, 0);
                }
            } else {
                targetDate = new Date(nextYear, origM - 1, origD, 0, 0, 0);
            }
        }

        const diffDaysFromToday = Math.round((targetDate - todayStart) / 86400000);
        return { targetDate, isRolloverNextYear, diffDaysFromToday };
    };

    // 3. Duy nhất 1 Singleton Interval đọc data-target-iso qua dataset (0 Memory Leak)
    const tickMasterCountdown = () => {
        if (document.hidden) return;
        const timerEl = document.getElementById("festival-master-timer");
        if (!timerEl || !timerEl.dataset.targetIso) return;

        const targetMs = new Date(timerEl.dataset.targetIso).getTime();
        const diffSec = Math.max(0, Math.floor((targetMs - Date.now()) / 1000));

        const days = Math.floor(diffSec / 86400);
        const hours = Math.floor((diffSec % 86400) / 3600);
        const minutes = Math.floor((diffSec % 3600) / 60);
        const seconds = diffSec % 60;

        const dEl = timerEl.querySelector('[data-timer-part="days"]');
        const hEl = timerEl.querySelector('[data-timer-part="hours"]');
        const mEl = timerEl.querySelector('[data-timer-part="minutes"]');
        const sEl = timerEl.querySelector('[data-timer-part="seconds"]');

        if (dEl) dEl.textContent = pad(days);
        if (hEl) hEl.textContent = pad(hours);
        if (mEl) mEl.textContent = pad(minutes);
        if (sEl) sEl.textContent = pad(seconds);
    };

    const initFestivalCalendar = () => {
        const rootEl = document.getElementById("festival-calendar-root");
        const app = document.getElementById("festival-cal-app");
        if (!app) return;

        if (rootEl) rootEl.setAttribute("aria-busy", "false");
        if (app.dataset.bound === "true") return;
        app.dataset.bound = "true";

        const allTiles = Array.from(app.querySelectorAll(".festival-tile"));
        const filterBtns = Array.from(app.querySelectorAll("[data-festival-filter]"));
        const monthTabs = Array.from(app.querySelectorAll("[data-month-filter]"));
        const moreBtn = document.getElementById("festival-more-btn");
        const moreLabel = document.getElementById("festival-more-label");
        const totalCountEl = document.getElementById("festival-total-count");
        const yearDropdown = document.getElementById("festival-year-dropdown");
        const prevYearBtn = document.getElementById("festival-prev-year");
        const nextYearBtn = document.getElementById("festival-next-year");
        const scrollViewport = document.getElementById("festival-scroll-viewport");

        const customDropdown = document.getElementById("festival-countdown-dropdown");
        const pickerListEl = document.getElementById("festival-picker-list");
        const pickerSearchInput = document.getElementById("festival-picker-search");
        const pickerTabs = Array.from(app.querySelectorAll("[data-picker-filter]"));
        const pickerFootEl = document.getElementById("festival-picker-foot");

        // Đo chiều cao thực tế của thanh Countdown để khóa chuẩn khoảng cách Gap 2rem xuống Hero & List Event
        const syncCountdownStickyGap = () => {
            const cdBar = document.getElementById("festival-countdown-bar");
            if (cdBar) {
                app.style.setProperty("--countdown-exact-h", `${cdBar.offsetHeight}px`);
            }
        };
        syncCountdownStickyGap();
        window.addEventListener("resize", syncCountdownStickyGap, { passive: true });

        // 4. Render danh sách sự kiện vào Custom Dropdown Kiểu A (Khóa giới hạn trong vòng 365 ngày tới)
        const renderCustomPickerOptions = () => {
            if (!pickerListEl) return;
            const currentVal = app.dataset.activeEvent || "nha-giao-viet-nam";
            const activePickerTab = app.dataset.pickerTab || "upcoming";
            const query = (pickerSearchInput?.value || "").trim().toLowerCase();

            const enrichedEvents = allTiles
                .map((tile) => {
                    const { targetDate, diffDaysFromToday } = resolveSmartCountdownTarget(tile);
                    const id = tile.dataset.eventId;
                    const name = tile.querySelector('[itemprop="name"]')?.textContent?.trim() || "";
                    const calType = tile.dataset.calendarType || "solar";
                    const isMajor = tile.dataset.major === "true";
                    const dispDate = `${pad(targetDate.getDate())}/${pad(targetDate.getMonth() + 1)}/${targetDate.getFullYear()}`;
                    const shortDate = `${pad(targetDate.getDate())}/${pad(targetDate.getMonth() + 1)}`;

                    return {
                        tile,
                        id,
                        name,
                        calType,
                        isMajor,
                        dispDate,
                        shortDate,
                        diffDaysFromToday,
                    };
                })
                .filter((item) => item.diffDaysFromToday >= 0 && item.diffDaysFromToday <= 370)
                .sort((a, b) => a.diffDaysFromToday - b.diffDaysFromToday);

            let visibleCount = 0;

            pickerListEl.innerHTML = enrichedEvents
                .map((item, idx) => {
                    const isSelected = item.id === currentVal;

                    let matchesTab = true;
                    if (!query) {
                        if (activePickerTab === "upcoming") {
                            matchesTab = item.diffDaysFromToday <= 120 || item.isMajor || idx < 12;
                        } else if (activePickerTab === "lunar") {
                            matchesTab = item.calType === "lunar";
                        } else if (activePickerTab === "solar") {
                            matchesTab = item.calType === "solar";
                        }
                    } else {
                        const haystack = `${item.name} ${item.dispDate} ${item.shortDate}`.toLowerCase();
                        matchesTab = haystack.includes(query);
                    }

                    if (matchesTab) visibleCount++;

                    return `
                        <li role="presentation" ${matchesTab ? "" : "hidden"}>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                role="option"
                                aria-selected="${isSelected}"
                                class="festival-picker__option ${isSelected ? "is-selected" : ""}"
                                data-pick-event-id="${item.id}"
                                data-pick-date="${item.dispDate}"
                                title="${item.name} — Diễn ra ngày ${item.dispDate} (Còn ${item.diffDaysFromToday} ngày)"
                            >
                                <span class="festival-picker__opt-name">${item.name}</span>
                                <small class="festival-picker__opt-date">${item.dispDate}</small>
                            </button>
                        </li>
                    `;
                })
                .join("");

            if (pickerFootEl) {
                pickerFootEl.textContent = query
                    ? `Tìm thấy ${visibleCount} sự kiện khớp từ khóa`
                    : `Hiển thị ${visibleCount} sự kiện trong vòng 365 ngày tới`;
            }
        };

        // 5. Cập nhật Hàng Countdown trên cùng
        const syncTileToMasterCountdown = (tile) => {
            if (!tile) return;
            const eventId = tile.dataset.eventId;
            const titleText = tile.querySelector('[itemprop="name"]')?.textContent?.trim() || "";
            app.dataset.activeEvent = eventId;

            const { targetDate, isRolloverNextYear, diffDaysFromToday } = resolveSmartCountdownTarget(tile);
            const tYear = targetDate.getFullYear();
            const tMonth = targetDate.getMonth() + 1;
            const tDay = targetDate.getDate();
            const isoTarget = `${tYear}-${pad(tMonth)}-${pad(tDay)}T00:00:00`;
            const dispDate = `${pad(tDay)}/${pad(tMonth)}/${tYear}`;

            const curNameEl = document.getElementById("festival-picker-current-name");
            const curDateEl = document.getElementById("festival-picker-current-date");
            if (curNameEl) curNameEl.textContent = titleText;
            if (curDateEl) curDateEl.textContent = dispDate;

            const timerEl = document.getElementById("festival-master-timer");
            if (timerEl) {
                timerEl.dataset.targetIso = isoTarget;
                timerEl.setAttribute("datetime", `${isoTarget}+07:00`);
            }

            const headingEl = document.getElementById("festival-countdown-heading");
            const nameEl = document.getElementById("festival-countdown-event-name");
            const rolloverTag = document.getElementById("festival-countdown-rollover-tag");
            const fullSolarEl = document.getElementById("festival-countdown-full-solar");
            const fullLunarEl = document.getElementById("festival-countdown-full-lunar");

            if (nameEl) nameEl.textContent = titleText;
            if (headingEl) {
                headingEl.setAttribute("title", `Thời gian còn lại đến ${titleText} (${dispDate})`);
            }

            if (rolloverTag) {
                rolloverTag.textContent = isRolloverNextYear
                    ? `Kỳ lễ tiếp theo (${tYear}) • Còn ${diffDaysFromToday} ngày`
                    : `Sắp diễn ra • Còn ${diffDaysFromToday} ngày`;
            }

            const weekdayStr = viWeekdayFormatter.format(targetDate);
            const solarLineStr = `📅 ${weekdayStr}, ngày ${pad(tDay)} tháng ${pad(tMonth)} năm ${tYear} Dương lịch`;
            if (fullSolarEl) {
                fullSolarEl.textContent = solarLineStr;
                fullSolarEl.setAttribute("title", solarLineStr);
            }

            if (window.LunarEngine && fullLunarEl) {
                const lInfo = window.LunarEngine.getFullDayInfo(tDay, tMonth, tYear);
                const lunarLineStr = `🌙 Nhằm ngày ${pad(lInfo.lunarDay)}/${pad(lInfo.lunarMonth)} Âm lịch (Năm ${lInfo.yearCanChi} • Ngày ${lInfo.dayCanChi} - ${lInfo.dayNapAm})`;
                fullLunarEl.textContent = lunarLineStr;
                fullLunarEl.setAttribute("title", lunarLineStr);
            }

            renderCustomPickerOptions();
            tickMasterCountdown();
            syncCountdownStickyGap();
        };

        // 6. Cập nhật Thẻ Hero bên trái
        const syncTileToHero = (tile, alsoUpdateCountdown = false) => {
            if (!tile) return;
            const { date, solar, lunar, weekday, calendarType, zodiac, major, desc } = tile.dataset;
            const titleText = tile.querySelector('[itemprop="name"]')?.textContent?.trim() || "";
            const activeYear = app.dataset.activeYear || "2026";

            const [y, m, d] = String(date).split("-").map(Number);
            const eventDate = new Date(y, m - 1, d);
            const today = new Date();
            today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
            const diffDays = Math.round((eventDate - today) / 86400000);

            let subtlePrediction = `Hôm nay diễn ra ${titleText}`;
            if (diffDays > 0) {
                subtlePrediction = `Còn ${diffDays} ngày nữa là đến ${titleText}`;
            } else if (diffDays < 0) {
                const { targetDate, diffDaysFromToday } = resolveSmartCountdownTarget(tile);
                subtlePrediction = `Kỳ tới còn ${diffDaysFromToday} ngày (${pad(targetDate.getDate())}/${pad(targetDate.getMonth() + 1)}/${targetDate.getFullYear()})`;
            }

            const heroPillEl = document.getElementById("festival-hero-countdown");
            if (heroPillEl) {
                heroPillEl.textContent = subtlePrediction;
                heroPillEl.setAttribute(
                    "title",
                    diffDays < 0 ? `Đã qua ${Math.abs(diffDays)} ngày • ${subtlePrediction}` : subtlePrediction,
                );
            }

            document.getElementById("festival-hero-type").dataset.calendarType = calendarType;
            document.getElementById("festival-hero-type-text").textContent =
                calendarType === "lunar"
                    ? major === "true"
                        ? "Đại lễ Âm lịch"
                        : "Lễ Âm lịch"
                    : major === "true"
                      ? "Đại lễ Dương lịch"
                      : "Lễ Dương lịch";

            const dotEl = document.querySelector("#festival-hero-type .festival-cal__dot");
            if (dotEl) {
                dotEl.className = `festival-cal__dot festival-cal__dot--${calendarType === "lunar" ? "lunar" : "solar"}`;
            }

            document.getElementById("festival-hero-solar").textContent = solar;
            document.getElementById("festival-hero-solar").setAttribute("datetime", date);
            document.getElementById("festival-hero-weekday").textContent = weekday;

            const descText =
                desc ||
                tile.querySelector(".festival-tile__desc")?.textContent?.trim() ||
                `Sự kiện ${titleText} diễn ra vào ngày ${solar}/${activeYear} Dương lịch (nhằm ngày ${lunar} Âm lịch).`;
            const heroDescEl = document.getElementById("festival-hero-desc");
            if (heroDescEl) {
                heroDescEl.textContent = descText;
                heroDescEl.setAttribute("title", descText);
            }

            const heroLunarEl = document.getElementById("festival-hero-lunar");
            const heroHoursEl = document.getElementById("festival-hero-hours");

            if (window.LunarEngine && y && m && d) {
                const info = window.LunarEngine.getFullDayInfo(d, m, y);
                document.getElementById("festival-hero-year").textContent = `Năm ${activeYear} (${info.yearCanChi})`;
                if (heroLunarEl) {
                    heroLunarEl.innerHTML = `Nhằm ngày <strong>${lunar} Âm lịch</strong> (Ngày ${info.dayCanChi} • ${info.dayNapAm})`;
                    heroLunarEl.setAttribute(
                        "title",
                        `Nhằm ngày ${lunar} Âm lịch (Ngày ${info.dayCanChi} • ${info.dayNapAm})`,
                    );
                }
                if (heroHoursEl) {
                    heroHoursEl.textContent = info.goodHoursStr;
                    heroHoursEl.setAttribute("title", info.goodHoursStr);
                }
            }

            const heroTitleEl = document.getElementById("festival-hero-title");
            if (heroTitleEl) {
                heroTitleEl.textContent = titleText;
                heroTitleEl.setAttribute("title", titleText);
            }

            document.getElementById("festival-hero-link").href = `./day.html?date=${date}`;
            document.getElementById("festival-hero-zodiac").src = `./assets/icons/zodiac/${zodiac || "horse"}.svg`;

            if (alsoUpdateCountdown) {
                allTiles.forEach((el) => {
                    const isSel = el === tile;
                    el.classList.toggle("is-active", isSel);
                    el.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(isSel));
                    el.setAttribute("tabindex", isSel ? "0" : "-1");
                });
                syncTileToMasterCountdown(tile);
            }
        };

        // 7. Sự kiện Hover & Click trên 38 Card <button>
        allTiles.forEach((tile) => {
            tile.addEventListener("mouseenter", () => syncTileToHero(tile, false));
            tile.addEventListener("focus", () => syncTileToHero(tile, false));
            tile.addEventListener("click", () => syncTileToHero(tile, true));

            tile.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
                const visibleTiles = allTiles.filter((t) => !t.hidden);
                const curVisIdx = visibleTiles.indexOf(tile);
                if (curVisIdx === -1) return;

                let nextTile = null;
                if (e.key === "ArrowRight" || e.key === "ArrowDown") {
                    nextTile = visibleTiles[(curVisIdx + 1) % visibleTiles.length];
                } else if (e.key === "ArrowLeft" || e.key === "ArrowUp") {
                    nextTile = visibleTiles[(curVisIdx - 1 + visibleTiles.length) % visibleTiles.length];
                }

                if (nextTile) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    syncTileToHero(nextTile, true);
                    nextTile.focus();
                }
            });
        });

        // 8. Sự kiện trên Custom Dropdown chọn sự kiện (Kiểu A + Search + 3 Tabs)
        if (customDropdown) {
            const summaryEl = customDropdown.querySelector("summary");
            customDropdown.addEventListener("toggle", () => {
                summaryEl?.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(customDropdown.open));
                if (customDropdown.open) {
                    renderCustomPickerOptions();
                }
            });

            pickerSearchInput?.addEventListener("input", () => {
                renderCustomPickerOptions();
            });

            pickerTabs.forEach((tab) => {
                tab.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    app.dataset.pickerTab = tab.dataset.pickerFilter || "upcoming";
                    pickerTabs.forEach((t) => {
                        const isMatch = t === tab;
                        t.classList.toggle("is-active", isMatch);
                        t.setAttribute("aria-selected", String(isMatch));
                        t.setAttribute("tabindex", isMatch ? "0" : "-1");
                    });
                    renderCustomPickerOptions();
                });
            });

            pickerListEl?.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
                const optBtn = e.target.closest("[data-pick-event-id]");
                if (!optBtn) return;
                e.stopPropagation();

                const chosenId = optBtn.dataset.pickEventId;
                const targetTile = allTiles.find((t) => t.dataset.eventId === chosenId);
                customDropdown.removeAttribute("open");

                if (!targetTile) return;

                if (targetTile.hidden) {
                    app.dataset.expanded = "true";
                    app.dataset.activeFilter = "all";
                    app.dataset.activeMonth = "all";
                    applyGridFilters();
                }

                syncTileToHero(targetTile, true);

                if (scrollViewport) {
                    const topPos =
                        targetTile.offsetTop -
                        scrollViewport.offsetTop -
                        scrollViewport.clientHeight / 2 +
                        targetTile.clientHeight / 2;
                    scrollViewport.scrollTo({ top: Math.max(0, topPos), behavior: "smooth" });
                }
            });
        }

        // 9. Hàm lọc O(n) trong 1 vòng lặp duy nhất
        const applyGridFilters = () => {
            const { activeFilter = "all", activeMonth = "all", expanded } = app.dataset;
            const isExpanded = expanded === "true";

            const now = new Date();
            const todayIso = `${now.getFullYear()}-${pad(now.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(now.getDate())}`;
            const isFilteringSpecific = activeFilter !== "all" || activeMonth !== "all";

            const matchingAll = [];
            const visibleTiles = [];

            let countMonthAll = 0;
            let countMonthLunar = 0;
            let countMonthSolar = 0;
            let countMonthUpcoming = 0;

            allTiles.forEach((tile) => {
                const { calendarType, month, date = "", extra } = tile.dataset;

                const isBelongToMonth = activeMonth === "all" || String(month) === String(activeMonth);
                if (isBelongToMonth) {
                    countMonthAll++;
                    if (calendarType === "lunar") countMonthLunar++;
                    if (calendarType === "solar") countMonthSolar++;
                    if (date >= todayIso) countMonthUpcoming++;
                }

                let matchesCriteria = isBelongToMonth;
                if (activeFilter === "lunar" && calendarType !== "lunar") matchesCriteria = false;
                if (activeFilter === "solar" && calendarType !== "solar") matchesCriteria = false;
                if (activeFilter === "upcoming" && date < todayIso) matchesCriteria = false;

                if (matchesCriteria) {
                    matchingAll.push(tile);
                    const shouldShow = isFilteringSpecific || isExpanded || extra !== "true";
                    tile.hidden = !shouldShow;
                    if (shouldShow) visibleTiles.push(tile);
                } else {
                    tile.hidden = true;
                }
            });

            document.getElementById("count-filter-all").textContent = String(countMonthAll);
            document.getElementById("count-filter-lunar").textContent = String(countMonthLunar);
            document.getElementById("count-filter-solar").textContent = String(countMonthSolar);
            document.getElementById("count-filter-upcoming").textContent = String(countMonthUpcoming);

            syncBentoRowFill(visibleTiles);

            const emptyStateEl = document.getElementById("festival-empty-state");
            if (emptyStateEl) emptyStateEl.hidden = visibleTiles.length > 0;

            const hasActiveVisible = visibleTiles.some((t) => t.classList.contains("is-active"));
            if (!hasActiveVisible && visibleTiles.length > 0) {
                syncTileToHero(visibleTiles[0], false);
            }

            const resetBtn = document.getElementById("festival-reset-btn");
            if (resetBtn) {
                resetBtn.hidden = activeMonth === "all" && activeFilter === "all";
                if (!resetBtn.dataset.hasListener) {
                    resetBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
                        app.dataset.activeFilter = "all";
                        filterBtns.forEach((b) => {
                            const isAll = b.dataset.festivalFilter === "all";
                            b.classList.toggle("is-active", isAll);
                            b.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(isAll));
                        });
                        selectMonthTab(app.querySelector('[data-month-filter="all"]'), false);
                    });
                    resetBtn.dataset.hasListener = "true";
                }
            }

            if (moreBtn && moreLabel) {
                const hiddenExtraCount = matchingAll.length - visibleTiles.length;
                if (isFilteringSpecific || (hiddenExtraCount === 0 && !isExpanded)) {
                    moreBtn.hidden = true;
                } else {
                    moreBtn.hidden = false;
                    moreBtn.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isExpanded));
                    moreLabel.textContent = isExpanded
                        ? "Thu gọn danh sách sự kiện"
                        : `+ Xem thêm ${hiddenExtraCount} sự kiện khác trong năm`;
                }
            }

            if (totalCountEl) {
                totalCountEl.textContent = `Đang hiển thị ${visibleTiles.length} / ${matchingAll.length} sự kiện`;
            }
        };

        if (moreBtn) {
            moreBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
```
