```js
    const getBranchIndexFromHour = (hour24 = 0) => {
        const h = Number(hour24) % 24;
        if (h === 23 || h === 0) return 0;
        return Math.floor((h + 1) / 2);
    };

    const getDayAuspiciousStatus = (lunarMonth, dayChiIndex) => {
        const startChi = ((lunarMonth - 1) * 2) % 12;
        const offset = (dayChiIndex - startChi + 12) % 12;
        if ([0, 1, 4, 7].includes(offset)) return "good";
        if ([2, 3, 6, 8].includes(offset)) return "bad";
        return "";
    };

    const getSolarTermName = (dd, mm) => {
        const code = mm * 100 + dd;
        if (code < 105) return "Đông Chí";
        if (code < 120) return "Tiểu Hàn";
        if (code < 204) return "Đại Hàn";
        if (code < 219) return "Lập Xuân";
        if (code < 305) return "Vũ Thủy";
        if (code < 321) return "Kinh Trập";
        if (code < 405) return "Xuân Phân";
        if (code < 420) return "Thanh Minh";
        if (code < 505) return "Cốc Vũ";
        if (code < 521) return "Lập Hạ";
        if (code < 606) return "Tiểu Mãn";
        if (code < 621) return "Mang Chủng";
        if (code < 707) return "Hạ Chí";
        if (code < 723) return "Tiểu Thử";
        if (code < 807) return "Đại Thử";
        if (code < 823) return "Lập Thu";
        if (code < 908) return "Xử Thử";
        if (code < 923) return "Bạch Lộ";
        if (code < 1008) return "Thu Phân";
        if (code < 1023) return "Hàn Lộ";
        if (code < 1107) return "Sương Giáng";
        if (code < 1122) return "Lập Đông";
        if (code < 1207) return "Tiểu Tuyết";
        if (code < 1222) return "Đại Tuyết";
        return "Đông Chí";
    };

    const getHolidayBadge = (sDay, sMonth, lDay, lMonth, lLeap) => {
        const solarKey = `${sDay}/${sMonth}`;
        const lunarKey = `${lDay}/${lMonth}`;

        if (SOLAR_HOLIDAYS[solarKey]) return { badgeText: SOLAR_HOLIDAYS[solarKey], isHoliday: true };
        if (!lLeap && LUNAR_HOLIDAYS[lunarKey]) return { badgeText: LUNAR_HOLIDAYS[lunarKey], isHoliday: true };
        if (lDay === 1) return { badgeText: "Mùng 1", isHoliday: true };
        if (lDay === 15) return { badgeText: "Rằm", isHoliday: true };
        return { badgeText: "", isHoliday: false };
    };

    const getFullDayInfo = (sDay, sMonth, sYear, sHour = new Date().getHours()) => {
        const lunar = convertSolar2Lunar(sDay, sMonth, sYear);
        const { lunarDay, lunarMonth, lunarYear, lunarLeap, isFullMonth, jd } = lunar;

        const yearCanIdx = (lunarYear + 6) % 10;
        const yearChiIdx = (lunarYear + 8) % 12;
        const monthCanIdx = ((yearCanIdx % 5) * 2 + lunarMonth + 1) % 10;
        const monthChiIdx = (lunarMonth + 1) % 12;
        const dayCanIdx = (jd + 9) % 10;
        const dayChiIdx = (jd + 1) % 12;
        const ratHourStemIdx = ((dayCanIdx % 5) * 2) % 10;
        const currentBranchIdx = getBranchIndexFromHour(sHour);

        const yearCanChi = `${THIEN_CAN[yearCanIdx]} ${DIA_CHI[yearChiIdx]}`;
        const monthCanChi = `${THIEN_CAN[monthCanIdx]} ${DIA_CHI[monthChiIdx]}`;
        const dayCanChi = `${THIEN_CAN[dayCanIdx]} ${DIA_CHI[dayChiIdx]}`;
        const yearNapAm = getNapAm(yearCanIdx, yearChiIdx);
        const monthNapAm = getNapAm(monthCanIdx, monthChiIdx);
        const dayNapAm = getNapAm(dayCanIdx, dayChiIdx);

        const dateObj = new Date(sYear, sMonth - 1, sDay);
        const bilingualWeekday = `${viWeekdayFormatter.format(dateObj).toUpperCase()} • ${enWeekdayFormatter.format(dateObj).toUpperCase()}`;
        const solarMonthEnLabel = enMonthFormatter.format(new Date(sYear, sMonth - 1, 1));
        const solarMonthVnLabel = VI_TRADITIONAL_SOLAR_MONTHS[sMonth] || `THÁNG ${sMonth}`;

        const baseLunarMonthName = VI_TRADITIONAL_LUNAR_MONTHS[lunarMonth] || `THÁNG ${lunarMonth}`;
        const lunarMonthFullLabel = `${baseLunarMonthName}${lunarLeap ? " NHUẬN" : ""}${isFullMonth ? " (Đ)" : " (T)"}`;

        const fortuneStatus = getDayAuspiciousStatus(lunarMonth, dayChiIdx);
        const holidayInfo = getHolidayBadge(sDay, sMonth, lunarDay, lunarMonth, lunarLeap);
        const solarTerm = getSolarTermName(sDay, sMonth);
        const trucObj = TRUC_LIST[(dayChiIdx - monthChiIdx + 12) % 12] || TRUC_LIST[0];
        const ageHarmony = AGE_HARMONY_MAP[dayChiIdx] || AGE_HARMONY_MAP[0];
        const goodIndices = GOOD_HOUR_INDICES[dayChiIdx] || [0, 1, 3, 6, 8, 9];
        const dayLucDieuIdx = (lunarMonth + lunarDay - 2) % 6;

        const goodHoursShort = [];
        const badHoursShort = [];
        const goodHourCards = [];
        const hours12 = [];

        HOUR_SLOTS.forEach((slot, idx) => {
            const hStemIdx = (ratHourStemIdx + idx) % 10;
            const canchi = `${THIEN_CAN[hStemIdx]} ${slot.vn}`;
            const hourCanChi = `Giờ ${canchi}`;
            const napAm = getNapAm(hStemIdx, idx);
            const elemObj = resolveElementInfo(napAm);
            const isGood = goodIndices.includes(idx);
            const lucDieuObj = LUC_DIEU_6[(dayLucDieuIdx + idx) % 6] || LUC_DIEU_6[0];
            const energyScore = Math.min(98, Math.max(42, (isGood ? 78 : 58) + lucDieuObj.bonus));

            if (isGood) {
                goodHoursShort.push(slot.shortRange);
                goodHourCards.push({
                    hourCanChi,
                    range: slot.range,
                    icon: slot.icon,
                });
            } else {
                badHoursShort.push(slot.shortRange);
            }

            hours12.push({
                ...slot,
                stemIdx: hStemIdx,
                canchi,
                hourCanChi,
                napAm,
                elementKey: elemObj.key,
                elementName: elemObj.name,
                elementIcon: elemObj.icon,
                isGood,
                lucDieuName: lucDieuObj.name,
                lucDieuGood: lucDieuObj.isGood,
                lucDieuDesc: lucDieuObj.desc,
                energyScore,
            });
        });

        return {
            sDay,
            sMonth,
            sYear,
            lunarDay,
            lunarMonth,
            lunarYear,
            lunarLeap,
            isFullMonth,
            jd,
            yearChiIdx,
            monthChiIdx,
            dayChiIdx,
            currentBranchIdx,
            yearCanChi,
            monthCanChi,
            dayCanChi,
            ratHourCanChi: `${THIEN_CAN[ratHourStemIdx]} Tý`,
            yearNapAm,
            monthNapAm,
            dayNapAm,
            elementInfo: resolveElementInfo(dayNapAm),
            zodiacFile: ZODIAC_FILES[dayChiIdx] || "mouse",
            monthZodiacFile: ZODIAC_FILES[monthChiIdx] || "dog",
            yearZodiacFile: ZODIAC_FILES[yearChiIdx] || "horse",
            directions: {
                hy: HY_THAN_BY_STEM[dayCanIdx] || "Tây Bắc",
                tai: TAI_THAN_BY_STEM[dayCanIdx] || "Đông Nam",
            },
            fortuneStatus,
            badgeText: holidayInfo.badgeText,
            isHoliday: holidayInfo.isHoliday,
            solarTerm,
            trucObj,
            ageHarmony,
            goodHoursStr: goodHoursShort.join(", "),
            badHoursStr: badHoursShort.join(", "),
            goodHourCards,
            hours12,
            bilingualWeekday,
            solarMonthVnLabel,
            solarMonthEnLabel,
            lunarMonthFullLabel,
        };
    };

    window.LunarEngine = {
        THIEN_CAN,
        DIA_CHI,
        ZODIAC_FILES,
        getBranchIndexFromHour,
        convertSolar2Lunar,
        convertLunar2Solar,
        getFullDayInfo,
    };
})();
```

### month-calendar.js

```js
// assets/js/month-calendar.js
// Chuyên trách duy nhất Trang Chủ (index.html): Lưới 42 ô ngày, Tờ Lịch Bloc & Fetch ./api/thang-${month}.json
(() => {
    const DEFAULT_COVER_IMG = "./assets/images/calendar-covers/thang-11/nguyen-thi-minh-khai-01-11-1910-28-08-1941.jpg";

    let currentYearNumber = 2026;
    let currentMonthNumber = 11;
    let selectedSolarDay = 3;

    const monthApiCache = new Map();
    let activeDayVnEvents = [];
    let activeDayIntlEvents = [];
    let currentVnEventIndex = 0;
    let currentIntlEventIndex = 0;

    const fetchMonthHistoryData = async (monthNum) => {
        const m = Number(monthNum) || 11;
        if (monthApiCache.has(m)) return monthApiCache.get(m);

        const paddedMonth = String(m).padStart(2, "0");
        const candidatePaths = [`./api/thang-${m}.json`, `./api/thang-${paddedMonth}.json`];

        for (const path of candidatePaths) {
            try {
                const res = await fetch(path);
                if (res.ok) {
                    const data = await res.json();
                    const normalized = Array.isArray(data) ? data : data?.data || data?.events || [];
                    monthApiCache.set(m, normalized);
                    return normalized;
                }
            } catch (err) {
                // Thử đường dẫn tiếp theo
            }
        }

        monthApiCache.set(m, []);
        return [];
    };

    const buildDetailUrlFromItem = (item, monthNum) => {
        if (!item) return "./detail.html";
        const rawSlug = item.image_slug || item.slug || "";
        const cleanActorFolder = rawSlug.replace(/-\d{2}-\d{2}-\d{4}.*$/, "") || rawSlug;
        const catStr = String(item.category_id || item.loai || "").toLowerCase();
        const typeFolder = catStr.includes("event") || catStr.includes("su-kien") ? "events" : "figures";

        const params = new URLSearchParams({
            type: typeFolder,
            folder: cleanActorFolder,
            slug: rawSlug,
            month: String(monthNum),
        });
        return `./detail.html?${params.toString()}`;
    };

    const setCoverImageWithFallback = (imgEl, monthNum, imageSlug, altText) => {
        if (!imgEl) return;
        if (altText) imgEl.alt = altText;

        if (!imageSlug) {
            imgEl.onerror = null;
            imgEl.src = DEFAULT_COVER_IMG;
            return;
        }

        const fileNameOnly = imageSlug.split("/").pop().trim();
        const cleanSlug = fileNameOnly.replace(/\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/i, "");
        const basePath = `./assets/images/calendar-covers/thang-${monthNum}/${cleanSlug}`;

        imgEl.onerror = () => {
            imgEl.onerror = () => {
                imgEl.onerror = null;
                imgEl.src = DEFAULT_COVER_IMG;
            };
            imgEl.src = `${basePath}.webp`;
        };

        imgEl.src = `${basePath}.jpg`;
    };

    const renderHistoryItemToBloc = (item, monthNum, solarDay) => {
        const imgEl = document.getElementById("detail-history-img");
        const mediaLinkEl = document.getElementById("detail-history-link");
        const captionLinkEl = document.getElementById("detail-history-caption");
        const captionTextEl = document.getElementById("detail-history-caption-text");
        const quoteTextEl =
            document.getElementById("detail-quote-text") || document.querySelector(".fengshui-quote__text");
        const quoteAuthorEl =
            document.getElementById("detail-quote-author") || document.querySelector(".fengshui-quote__author");

        const paddedDay = String(solarDay).padStart(2, "0");
        const paddedMonth = String(monthNum).padStart(2, "0");

        if (!item) {
            if (imgEl) {
                imgEl.onerror = null;
                imgEl.src = DEFAULT_COVER_IMG;
            }
            if (captionTextEl) {
                captionTextEl.textContent = `Sự kiện & Nhân vật lịch sử ngày ${paddedDay}/${paddedMonth}`;
            }
            if (mediaLinkEl) mediaLinkEl.href = `./detail.html?day=${solarDay}&month=${monthNum}`;
            if (captionLinkEl) captionLinkEl.href = `./detail.html?day=${solarDay}&month=${monthNum}`;
            if (quoteTextEl) {
                quoteTextEl.textContent = '"Dân ta phải biết sử ta, cho tường gốc tích nước nhà Việt Nam."';
            }
            if (quoteAuthorEl) {
                quoteAuthorEl.textContent = "- Chủ tịch Hồ Chí Minh -";
            }
            return;
        }

        const occasionText = item.hien_thi_nhan_dip || item.ten || `Sự kiện lịch sử ngày ${paddedDay}/${paddedMonth}`;
        const detailHref = buildDetailUrlFromItem(item, monthNum);

        setCoverImageWithFallback(imgEl, monthNum, item.image_slug || item.slug, occasionText);

        if (captionTextEl) captionTextEl.textContent = occasionText;
        if (mediaLinkEl) mediaLinkEl.href = detailHref;
        if (captionLinkEl) captionLinkEl.href = detailHref;

        if (quoteTextEl) {
            const rawQuote = (item.quote || item.danh_ngon || "").trim();
            quoteTextEl.textContent = rawQuote
                ? rawQuote.startsWith('"')
                    ? rawQuote
                    : `"${rawQuote}"`
                : `"${occasionText}"`;
        }

        if (quoteAuthorEl) {
            const authorName = item.ten || item.tac_gia || "Lịch sử Việt Nam";
            quoteAuthorEl.textContent = `- ${authorName} -`;
        }
    };

    const updateHistoryForSelectedDate = async (solarDay, monthNum) => {
        const sameDayBtn = document.getElementById("btn-same-day-events");
        const sameDayLabel = document.getElementById("label-same-day-events");
        const intlBtn = document.getElementById("btn-intl-events");
        const intlLabel = document.getElementById("label-intl-events");

        const paddedDay = String(solarDay).padStart(2, "0");
        const paddedMonth = String(monthNum).padStart(2, "0");
        const targetDatePadded = `${paddedDay}/${paddedMonth}`;
        const targetDateShort = `${Number(solarDay)}/${Number(monthNum)}`;
        const targetSlugDate = `-${paddedDay}-${paddedMonth}-`;

        const allMonthItems = await fetchMonthHistoryData(monthNum);

        const matchedItems = allMonthItems.filter((entry) => {
            const displayDate = String(entry.ngay_hien_thi || entry.ngay_duong || entry.date || "").trim();
            const occasionStr = String(entry.hien_thi_nhan_dip || "");
            const slugStr = String(entry.image_slug || "");

            return (
                displayDate.startsWith(targetDatePadded) ||
                displayDate.startsWith(targetDateShort) ||
                displayDate === String(solarDay) ||
                Number(entry.day) === Number(solarDay) ||
                occasionStr.includes(`(${targetDatePadded}`) ||
                slugStr.includes(targetSlugDate)
            );
        });

        activeDayIntlEvents = matchedItems.filter((entry) => {
            const scope = String(entry.pham_vi || entry.scope || entry.category_id || "").toLowerCase();
            return Boolean(entry.is_international) || scope.includes("quoc-te") || scope.includes("international");
        });

        activeDayVnEvents = matchedItems.filter((entry) => !activeDayIntlEvents.includes(entry));
        currentVnEventIndex = 0;
        currentIntlEventIndex = 0;

        if (sameDayBtn) {
            if (activeDayVnEvents.length > 1) {
                sameDayBtn.hidden = false;
                sameDayBtn.classList.add("is-active");
                if (sameDayLabel) {
                    sameDayLabel.textContent = `Sự kiện cùng ngày (${activeDayVnEvents.length})`;
                }
            } else {
                sameDayBtn.hidden = true;
                sameDayBtn.classList.remove("is-active");
            }
        }

        if (intlBtn) {
            if (activeDayIntlEvents.length > 0) {
                intlBtn.hidden = false;
                intlBtn.classList.remove("is-active");
                if (intlLabel) {
                    intlLabel.textContent = `Sự kiện quốc tế (${activeDayIntlEvents.length})`;
                }
            } else {
                intlBtn.hidden = true;
                intlBtn.classList.remove("is-active");
            }
        }

        const primaryItem = activeDayVnEvents[0] || activeDayIntlEvents[0] || null;
        renderHistoryItemToBloc(primaryItem, monthNum, solarDay);
    };

    const syncCellToBlocCard = (cell) => {
        if (!window.LunarEngine) return;

        const sDay = Number(cell.dataset.solar) || 1;
        const sMonth = Number(cell.dataset.month) || currentMonthNumber;
        const sYear = Number(cell.dataset.year) || currentYearNumber;

        const info = window.LunarEngine.getFullDayInfo(sDay, sMonth, sYear);

        document.getElementById("detail-solar-year").textContent = String(sYear);
        document.getElementById("detail-solar-month-vn").textContent = info.solarMonthVnLabel;
        document.getElementById("detail-solar-month-en").textContent = info.solarMonthEnLabel;
        document.getElementById("detail-solar-day").textContent = String(sDay);
        document.getElementById("detail-lunar-year").textContent = `NĂM ${info.yearCanChi.toUpperCase()}`;
        document.getElementById("detail-weekday").textContent = info.bilingualWeekday;

        const statusEl = document.getElementById("detail-day-status");
        if (statusEl) {
            if (info.badgeText) statusEl.textContent = `Ngày ${info.badgeText}`;
            else if (info.fortuneStatus === "good") statusEl.textContent = "Ngày Hoàng Đạo";
            else if (info.fortuneStatus === "bad") statusEl.textContent = "Ngày Hắc Đạo";
            else statusEl.textContent = "Ngày bình thường";
        }

        document.getElementById("detail-lunar-day").textContent = String(info.lunarDay);
        document.getElementById("detail-lunar-month").textContent = info.lunarMonthFullLabel;
        document.getElementById("detail-canchi-month").textContent = `Tháng ${info.monthCanChi}`;
        document.getElementById("detail-canchi-day").textContent = `Ngày ${info.dayCanChi}`;
        document.getElementById("detail-canchi-hour").textContent = `Giờ ${info.ratHourCanChi}`;
        document.getElementById("detail-element").textContent = info.dayNapAm;
        document.getElementById("detail-solar-term").textContent = info.solarTerm;
        document.getElementById("detail-truc-name").textContent = info.trucObj.name;

        document.getElementById("detail-good-hours").textContent = info.goodHoursStr;
        document.getElementById("detail-bad-hours").textContent = info.badHoursStr;
        document.getElementById("detail-truc-title").textContent = info.trucObj.name;
        document.getElementById("detail-truc-desc").textContent = info.trucObj.desc;
        document.getElementById("detail-age-good").textContent = info.ageHarmony.good;
        document.getElementById("detail-age-bad").textContent = info.ageHarmony.bad;

        updateHistoryForSelectedDate(sDay, sMonth);
    };

    const syncToolbarDropdownUI = (monthNum, yearNum) => {
        const monthDropdown = document.getElementById("cal-month-dropdown");
        const yearDropdown = document.getElementById("cal-year-dropdown");
        const monthLabel = document.getElementById("cal-month-label");
        const yearLabel = document.getElementById("cal-year-label");

        if (monthLabel) monthLabel.textContent = `Tháng ${monthNum}`;
        if (yearLabel) yearLabel.textContent = String(yearNum);

        if (monthDropdown) {
            monthDropdown
                .querySelector(".month-cal__summary")
                ?.setAttribute("aria-label", `Chọn tháng, hiện tại là Tháng ${monthNum}`);
            monthDropdown.querySelectorAll(".month-cal__option").forEach((opt) => {
                const isSelected = Number(opt.dataset.month) === Number(monthNum);
                opt.classList.toggle("is-selected", isSelected);
                if (isSelected) opt.setAttribute("aria-selected", "true");
                else opt.removeAttribute("aria-selected");
            });
        }

        if (yearDropdown) {
            yearDropdown
                .querySelector(".month-cal__summary")
                ?.setAttribute("aria-label", `Chọn năm, hiện tại là ${yearNum}`);
            const yearList = yearDropdown.querySelector(".month-cal__dropdown-menu--year");

            if (yearList && !yearList.querySelector(`[data-year="${yearNum}"]`)) {
                const li = document.createElement("li");
                const btn = document.createElement("button");
                btn.type = "button";
                btn.dataset.year = String(yearNum);
                btn.className = "month-cal__option";
                btn.textContent = String(yearNum);
                li.appendChild(btn);
                yearList.appendChild(li);
            }

            yearDropdown.querySelectorAll(".month-cal__option").forEach((opt) => {
                const isSelected = Number(opt.dataset.year) === Number(yearNum);
                opt.classList.toggle("is-selected", isSelected);
                if (isSelected) opt.setAttribute("aria-selected", "true");
                else opt.removeAttribute("aria-selected");
            });
        }
    };

    const renderMonthCalendarGrid = (yearNum, monthNum, preferredActiveDay = null) => {
        const grid = document.getElementById("month-cal-grid");
        if (!grid || !window.LunarEngine) return;

        currentYearNumber = Number(yearNum);
        currentMonthNumber = Number(monthNum);
        syncToolbarDropdownUI(currentMonthNumber, currentYearNumber);

        const firstDayJs = new Date(currentYearNumber, currentMonthNumber - 1, 1).getDay();
        const leadingMutedCount = (firstDayJs + 6) % 7;
        const daysInCurrentMonth = new Date(currentYearNumber, currentMonthNumber, 0).getDate();

        const prevMonth = currentMonthNumber === 1 ? 12 : currentMonthNumber - 1;
        const prevYear = currentMonthNumber === 1 ? currentYearNumber - 1 : currentYearNumber;
        const daysInPrevMonth = new Date(prevYear, prevMonth, 0).getDate();

        const nextMonth = currentMonthNumber === 12 ? 1 : currentMonthNumber + 1;
        const nextYear = currentMonthNumber === 12 ? currentYearNumber + 1 : currentYearNumber;

        const realNow = new Date();
        const realDay = realNow.getDate();
        const realMonth = realNow.getMonth() + 1;
        const realYear = realNow.getFullYear();

        let targetActiveDay = preferredActiveDay;
        if (!targetActiveDay) {
            targetActiveDay = currentYearNumber === realYear && currentMonthNumber === realMonth ? realDay : 1;
        }
        if (targetActiveDay > daysInCurrentMonth) targetActiveDay = daysInCurrentMonth;
        selectedSolarDay = targetActiveDay;

        const fragment = document.createDocumentFragment();
        const generatedCells = [];
        let activeCellRef = null;

        for (let i = 0; i < 42; i++) {
            let cellDay;
            let cellMonth;
            let cellYear;
            let isMuted = false;

            if (i < leadingMutedCount) {
                cellDay = daysInPrevMonth - leadingMutedCount + 1 + i;
                cellMonth = prevMonth;
                cellYear = prevYear;
                isMuted = true;
            } else if (i < leadingMutedCount + daysInCurrentMonth) {
                cellDay = i - leadingMutedCount + 1;
                cellMonth = currentMonthNumber;
                cellYear = currentYearNumber;
            } else {
                cellDay = i - (leadingMutedCount + daysInCurrentMonth) + 1;
                cellMonth = nextMonth;
                cellYear = nextYear;
                isMuted = true;
            }

            const dayInfo = window.LunarEngine.getFullDayInfo(cellDay, cellMonth, cellYear);
            const isToday = cellDay === realDay && cellMonth === realMonth && cellYear === realYear;
            const isActive = !isMuted && cellDay === targetActiveDay;

            const statusTokens = [];
            if (dayInfo.fortuneStatus) statusTokens.push(dayInfo.fortuneStatus);
            if (dayInfo.isHoliday) statusTokens.push("holiday");
            if (isToday) statusTokens.push("today");

            const btn = document.createElement("button");
            btn.type = "button";
            btn.className = "month-cal__cell";
            if (isMuted) btn.classList.add("month-cal__cell--muted");
            if (isToday) btn.classList.add("month-cal__cell--today");
            if (isActive) {
                btn.classList.add("month-cal__cell--active");
                btn.setAttribute("aria-current", "date");
                activeCellRef = btn;
            }

            btn.dataset.solar = String(cellDay);
            btn.dataset.month = String(cellMonth);
            btn.dataset.year = String(cellYear);
            btn.dataset.lunar = `${dayInfo.lunarDay}/${dayInfo.lunarMonth}`;
            if (statusTokens.length > 0) btn.dataset.status = statusTokens.join(" ");

            const badgeHtml = dayInfo.badgeText ? `<span class="month-cal__badge">${dayInfo.badgeText}</span>` : "";

            let dotsInnerHtml = "";
            if (dayInfo.fortuneStatus === "good") {
                dotsInnerHtml += `<span class="month-cal__dot month-cal__dot--good"></span>`;
            } else if (dayInfo.fortuneStatus === "bad") {
                dotsInnerHtml += `<span class="month-cal__dot month-cal__dot--bad"></span>`;
            }
            if (dayInfo.isHoliday) {
                dotsInnerHtml += `<span class="month-cal__dot month-cal__dot--holiday"></span>`;
            }

            btn.innerHTML = `
                ${badgeHtml}
                <div class="month-cal__date-head">
                    <span class="month-cal__solar">${cellDay}</span>
                    <span class="month-cal__lunar">${dayInfo.lunarDay}/${dayInfo.lunarMonth}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="month-cal__dots">${dotsInnerHtml}</div>
                <img
                    src="./assets/icons/zodiac/${dayInfo.zodiacFile}.svg"
                    alt=""
                    aria-hidden="true"
                    class="month-cal__zodiac-img"
                />
            `;

            btn.onclick = () => {
                if (isMuted) {
                    renderMonthCalendarGrid(cellYear, cellMonth, cellDay);
                    return;
                }

                generatedCells.forEach((item) => {
                    item.classList.remove("month-cal__cell--active");
                    item.removeAttribute("aria-current");
                });

                btn.classList.add("month-cal__cell--active");
                btn.setAttribute("aria-current", "date");
                selectedSolarDay = cellDay;
                syncCellToBlocCard(btn);
            };

            generatedCells.push(btn);
            fragment.appendChild(btn);
        }

        grid.innerHTML = "";
        grid.appendChild(fragment);

        if (activeCellRef) syncCellToBlocCard(activeCellRef);
    };

    const initMonthCalendar = () => {
        const grid = document.getElementById("month-cal-grid");
        if (!grid || grid.dataset.bound === "true") return;
        grid.dataset.bound = "true";

        const prevMonthBtn = document.getElementById("cal-prev-month");
        const nextMonthBtn = document.getElementById("cal-next-month");
        const todayBtn = document.getElementById("cal-today-btn");
        const calDropdowns = document.querySelectorAll(".js-cal-dropdown");
        const legendPills = document.querySelectorAll(".month-cal__legend-pill");
        const sameDayBtn = document.getElementById("btn-same-day-events");
        const intlBtn = document.getElementById("btn-intl-events");

        if (prevMonthBtn) {
            prevMonthBtn.onclick = () => {
                const targetMonth = currentMonthNumber === 1 ? 12 : currentMonthNumber - 1;
                const targetYear = currentMonthNumber === 1 ? currentYearNumber - 1 : currentYearNumber;
                renderMonthCalendarGrid(targetYear, targetMonth, null);
            };
        }

        if (nextMonthBtn) {
            nextMonthBtn.onclick = () => {
                const targetMonth = currentMonthNumber === 12 ? 1 : currentMonthNumber + 1;
                const targetYear = currentMonthNumber === 12 ? currentYearNumber + 1 : currentYearNumber;
                renderMonthCalendarGrid(targetYear, targetMonth, null);
            };
        }

        if (todayBtn) {
            todayBtn.onclick = () => {
                const now = new Date();
                renderMonthCalendarGrid(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() + 1, now.getDate());
            };
        }

        if (sameDayBtn) {
            sameDayBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                e.stopPropagation();
                if (!activeDayVnEvents.length) return;

                currentVnEventIndex = (currentVnEventIndex + 1) % activeDayVnEvents.length;
                sameDayBtn.classList.add("is-active");
                intlBtn?.classList.remove("is-active");

                renderHistoryItemToBloc(activeDayVnEvents[currentVnEventIndex], currentMonthNumber, selectedSolarDay);
            };
        }

        if (intlBtn) {
            intlBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                e.stopPropagation();
                if (!activeDayIntlEvents.length) return;

                const nextItem = activeDayIntlEvents[currentIntlEventIndex];
                currentIntlEventIndex = (currentIntlEventIndex + 1) % activeDayIntlEvents.length;
                intlBtn.classList.add("is-active");
                sameDayBtn?.classList.remove("is-active");

                renderHistoryItemToBloc(nextItem, currentMonthNumber, selectedSolarDay);
            };
        }

        calDropdowns.forEach((current) => {
            current.ontoggle = () => {
                if (current.open) {
                    document.querySelectorAll(".js-header-dropdown[open], .js-cal-dropdown[open]").forEach((other) => {
                        if (other !== current) other.removeAttribute("open");
                    });
                }
            };

            current.onclick = (e) => {
                const opt = e.target.closest(".month-cal__option");
                if (!opt) return;
                e.stopPropagation();

                const nextMonth = opt.dataset.month ? Number(opt.dataset.month) : currentMonthNumber;
                const nextYear = opt.dataset.year ? Number(opt.dataset.year) : currentYearNumber;

                current.removeAttribute("open");
                renderMonthCalendarGrid(nextYear, nextMonth, null);
            };
        });

        const highlightCellsByStatus = (statusKey) => {
            grid.querySelectorAll(".month-cal__cell").forEach((cell) => {
                if (!statusKey) {
                    cell.classList.remove("is-dimmed", "is-highlighted");
                    return;
                }
                const cellStatus = cell.dataset.status || "";
                const isMatch = cellStatus.split(" ").includes(statusKey);
                cell.classList.toggle("is-highlighted", isMatch);
                cell.classList.toggle("is-dimmed", !isMatch);
            });
        };

        legendPills.forEach((pill) => {
            const filterKey = pill.dataset.filter;
            pill.onmouseenter = () => highlightCellsByStatus(filterKey);
            pill.onmouseleave = () => highlightCellsByStatus(null);
            pill.onfocus = () => highlightCellsByStatus(filterKey);
            pill.onblur = () => highlightCellsByStatus(null);
        });

        window.addEventListener("converter-jump-to-date", (e) => {
            if (e.detail) {
                renderMonthCalendarGrid(e.detail.year, e.detail.month, e.detail.day);
            }
        });

        const urlDateParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("date");
        if (urlDateParam && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(urlDateParam)) {
            const [y, m, d] = urlDateParam.split("-").map(Number);
            renderMonthCalendarGrid(y, m, d);
        } else {
            renderMonthCalendarGrid(currentYearNumber, currentMonthNumber, selectedSolarDay);
        }
    };

    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", initMonthCalendar);
})();
```

### register.js

```js
import { showError, showSuccess, emailRegex, strongPasswordRegex } from "./validation.js";

const form = document.getElementById("form-signup");
const email = document.getElementById("email");
const password = document.getElementById("password");
const confirmPassword = document.getElementById("confirm-password");

if (form) {
    // --- 1. XỬ LÝ KHI NGƯỜI DÙNG CLICK RA NGOÀI (BLUR) ---
    email.addEventListener("blur", () => {
        if (email.value.trim() !== "") {
            if (!emailRegex.test(email.value.trim())) {
                showError(email, "Email không đúng định dạng");
            } else {
                showSuccess(email);
            }
        }
    });

    password.addEventListener("blur", () => {
        if (password.value.trim() !== "") {
            if (!strongPasswordRegex.test(password.value.trim())) {
                showError(password, "Mật khẩu tối thiểu 8 ký tự, gồm chữ hoa, thường, số và ký tự đặc biệt");
            } else {
                showSuccess(password);
            }
        }
    });

    confirmPassword.addEventListener("blur", () => {
        if (confirmPassword.value.trim() !== "") {
            if (confirmPassword.value !== password.value) {
                showError(confirmPassword, "Mật khẩu không khớp");
            } else {
                showSuccess(confirmPassword);
            }
        }
    });

    // --- 2. XÓA LỖI NGAY KHI BẮT ĐẦU GÕ LẠI (INPUT) ---
    email.addEventListener("input", () => showSuccess(email));
    password.addEventListener("input", () => showSuccess(password));
    confirmPassword.addEventListener("input", () => showSuccess(confirmPassword));

    // --- 3. XỬ LÝ KHI BẤM NÚT SIGN UP (SUBMIT) ---
    form.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        let isValid = true;

        // Check Email
        if (!emailRegex.test(email.value.trim())) {
            showError(email, "Email không đúng định dạng");
            isValid = false;
        }

        // Check Password (Mật khẩu mạnh)
        if (!strongPasswordRegex.test(password.value.trim())) {
            showError(password, "Mật khẩu tối thiểu 8 ký tự, gồm chữ hoa, thường, số và ký tự đặc biệt");
            isValid = false;
        }

        // Check Confirm Password
        if (confirmPassword.value.trim() === "" || confirmPassword.value !== password.value) {
            showError(confirmPassword, "Mật khẩu không khớp");
            isValid = false;
        }

        // Chuyển trang nếu mọi thứ hợp lệ
        if (isValid) {
            console.log("Form hợp lệ, đang xử lý...");
            window.location.href = "./index-logined.html";
        }
    });
}

// --- 4. TÍNH NĂNG ẨN/HIỆN MẬT KHẨU ---
const togglePasswordIcons = document.querySelectorAll(".js-toggle-password");

togglePasswordIcons.forEach((icon) => {
    icon.addEventListener("click", function () {
        const input = this.closest(".form__text-input").querySelector(".form__input");

        if (input) {
            if (input.type === "password") {
                input.type = "text";
                this.src = "./assets/icons/eye.svg"; // Icon mắt mở
            } else {
                input.type = "password";
                this.src = "./assets/icons/eye-slash.svg"; // Icon mắt nhắm
            }
        }
    });
});
```

### script.js

```js
const $ = document.querySelector.bind(document);
const $$ = document.querySelectorAll.bind(document);

/**
 * Hàm tải template
 *
 * Cách dùng:
 * <div id="parent"></div>
 * <script>
 *  load("#parent", "./path-to-template.html");
 * </script>
 */
// Trong assets/js/script.js (Dòng 13 - 29)
function load(selector, path) {
    const targetEl = $(selector);
    if (!targetEl) {
        console.warn(`[load] Không tìm thấy thẻ "${selector}" trong index.html!`);
        return;
    }

    const cached = localStorage.getItem(path);
    if (cached && cached.trim() !== "") {
        targetEl.innerHTML = cached;
    }

    fetch(path)
        .then((res) => {
            if (!res.ok) {
                throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status} - Không tìm thấy file tại đường dẫn: ${path}`);
            }
            return res.text();
        })
        .then((html) => {
            if (html.trim() === "") {
                console.warn(`[load] File "${path}" đang RỖNG (0 ký tự). Hãy kiểm tra và bấm Ctrl+S trong VS Code!`);
                return;
            }
            if (html !== cached) {
                targetEl.innerHTML = html;
                localStorage.setItem(path, html);
            }
        })
        .catch((err) => {
            console.error(`[load] Lỗi khi nạp "${path}":`, err.message);
        })
        .finally(() => {
            window.dispatchEvent(new Event("template-loaded"));
        });
}

/**
 * Hàm kiểm tra một phần tử
 * có bị ẩn bởi display: none không
 */
function isHidden(element) {
    if (!element) return true;

    if (window.getComputedStyle(element).display === "none") {
        return true;
    }

    let parent = element.parentElement;
    while (parent) {
        if (window.getComputedStyle(parent).display === "none") {
            return true;
        }
        parent = parent.parentElement;
    }

    return false;
}

/**
 * Hàm buộc một hành động phải đợi
 * sau một khoảng thời gian mới được thực thi
 */
function debounce(func, timeout = 300) {
    let timer;
    return (...args) => {
        clearTimeout(timer);
        timer = setTimeout(() => {
            func.apply(this, args);
        }, timeout);
    };
}

/**
 * Hàm tính toán vị trí arrow cho dropdown
 *
 * Cách dùng:
 * 1. Thêm class "js-dropdown-list" vào thẻ ul cấp 1
 * 2. CSS "left" cho arrow qua biến "--arrow-left-pos"
 */
const calArrowPos = debounce(() => {
    if (isHidden($(".js-dropdown-list"))) return;

    const items = $$(".js-dropdown-list > li");

    items.forEach((item) => {
        const arrowPos = item.offsetLeft + item.offsetWidth / 2;
        item.style.setProperty("--arrow-left-pos", `${arrowPos}px`);
    });
});

// Tính toán lại vị trí arrow khi resize trình duyệt
window.addEventListener("resize", calArrowPos);

// Tính toán lại vị trí arrow sau khi tải template
window.addEventListener("template-loaded", calArrowPos);

/**
 * Giữ active menu khi hover
 *
 * Cách dùng:
 * 1. Thêm class "js-menu-list" vào thẻ ul menu chính
 * 2. Thêm class "js-dropdown" vào class "dropdown" hiện tại
 *  nếu muốn reset lại item active khi ẩn menu
 */
window.addEventListener("template-loaded", handleActiveMenu);

function handleActiveMenu() {
    const dropdowns = $$(".js-dropdown");
    const menus = $$(".js-menu-list");
    const activeClass = "menu-column__item--active";

    const removeActive = (menu) => {
        menu.querySelector(`.${activeClass}`)?.classList.remove(activeClass);
    };

    const init = () => {
        menus.forEach((menu) => {
            const items = menu.children;
            if (!items.length) return;

            removeActive(menu);
            if (window.innerWidth > 991) items[0].classList.add(activeClass);

            Array.from(items).forEach((item) => {
                item.onmouseenter = () => {
                    if (window.innerWidth <= 991) return;
                    removeActive(menu);
                    item.classList.add(activeClass);
                };
                item.onclick = () => {
                    if (window.innerWidth > 991) return;
                    removeActive(menu);
                    item.classList.add(activeClass);
                    item.scrollIntoView();
                };
            });
        });
    };

    init();

    dropdowns.forEach((dropdown) => {
        dropdown.onmouseleave = () => init();
    });
}

/**
 * JS toggle
 *
 * Cách dùng:
 * <button class="js-toggle" toggle-target="#box">Click</button>
 * <div id="box">Content show/hide</div>
 */
window.addEventListener("template-loaded", initJsToggle);

// function initJsToggle() {
//     $$(".js-toggle").forEach((button) => {
//         const target = button.getAttribute("toggle-target");
//         if (!target) {
//             document.body.innerText = `Cần thêm toggle-target cho: ${button.outerHTML}`;
//         }
//         button.onclick = (e) => {
//             e.preventDefault();

//             if (!$(target)) {
//                 return (document.body.innerText = `Không tìm thấy phần tử "${target}"`);
//             }
//             const isHidden = $(target).classList.contains("hide");

//             requestAnimationFrame(() => {
//                 $(target).classList.toggle("hide", !isHidden);
//                 $(target).classList.toggle("show", isHidden);
//             });
//         };

//         document.onclick = function (e) {
//             if (!e.target.closest(target)) {
//                 const isHidden = $(target).classList.contains("hide");
//                 if (!isHidden) {
//                     button.click();
//                 }
//             }
//         };
//     });
// }

function initJsToggle() {
    $$(".js-toggle").forEach((button) => {
        // Đọc qua dataset.toggleTarget chuẩn HTML5 (hỗ trợ lùi về toggle-target cũ nếu còn sót)
        const target = button.dataset.toggleTarget || button.getAttribute("toggle-target");
        if (!target) return;

        button.onclick = (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            const targetEl = $(target);
            if (!targetEl) return;

            const isHidden = targetEl.classList.contains("hide");
            requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                targetEl.classList.toggle("hide", !isHidden);
                targetEl.classList.toggle("show", isHidden);
                button.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isHidden));
            });
        };
    });
}

window.addEventListener("template-loaded", () => {
    const links = $$(".js-dropdown-list > li > a");

    links.forEach((link) => {
        link.onclick = () => {
            if (window.innerWidth > 991) return;
            const item = link.closest("li");
            item.classList.toggle("navbar__item--active");
        };
    });
});
```

### sidebar.js

```js
// assets/js/sidebar.js
// Chuyên trách duy nhất Sidebar: Bật/tắt ngăn kéo Mobile Drawer, Khóa cuộn <body> & Đồng bộ Active Link
(() => {
    let isSidebarGlobalBound = false;

    const toggleSidebar = (forceClose = false) => {
        const sidebar = document.getElementById("calendar-sidebar");
        const burgerBtn = document.getElementById("burger-btn");
        const backdrop = document.getElementById("sidebar-backdrop");
        if (!sidebar || !burgerBtn || !backdrop) return;

        const isOpen = sidebar.classList.contains("is-open");
        const nextState = forceClose ? false : !isOpen;

        sidebar.classList.toggle("is-open", nextState);
        burgerBtn.classList.toggle("is-active", nextState);
        backdrop.classList.toggle("is-active", nextState);
        burgerBtn.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(nextState));

        document.body.style.overflow = nextState ? "hidden" : "";
    };

    const syncActiveSidebarLink = () => {
        const rawPage = window.location.pathname.split("/").pop() || "index.html";
        let targetNavPage = rawPage;
        if (rawPage === "detail.html") {
            targetNavPage = "index.html";
        } else if (rawPage === "iching-detail.html" || rawPage === "tarot-detail.html") {
            targetNavPage = "utilities.html";
        }

        const sidebarLinks = document.querySelectorAll(".sidebar__link[data-nav-page]");
        sidebarLinks.forEach((link) => {
            const isMatch = link.dataset.navPage === targetNavPage;
            link.classList.toggle("sidebar__link--active", isMatch);
            if (isMatch) link.setAttribute("aria-current", "page");
            else link.removeAttribute("aria-current");
        });
    };

    const initSidebar = () => {
        const burgerBtn = document.getElementById("burger-btn");
        const backdrop = document.getElementById("sidebar-backdrop");

        if (burgerBtn) burgerBtn.onclick = () => toggleSidebar();
        if (backdrop) backdrop.onclick = () => toggleSidebar(true);

        syncActiveSidebarLink();
    };

    if (!isSidebarGlobalBound) {
        isSidebarGlobalBound = true;

        window.addEventListener("resize", () => {
            const sidebar = document.getElementById("calendar-sidebar");
            if (window.innerWidth >= 768 && sidebar?.classList.contains("is-open")) {
                toggleSidebar(true);
            }
        });

        document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
            if (e.key === "Escape") toggleSidebar(true);
        });
    }

    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", initSidebar);
    window.addEventListener("template-loaded", initSidebar);
})();
```

### validation.js

```js
export function showError(inputElement, message) {
    inputElement.setCustomValidity(message); // Kích hoạt viền đỏ CSS

    const formGroup = inputElement.closest(".form__group");
    const errorElement = formGroup.querySelector(".form__error");
    if (errorElement) {
        errorElement.innerText = message;
    }
}

// Hàm xóa lỗi
export function showSuccess(inputElement) {
    inputElement.setCustomValidity(""); // Tắt viền đỏ CSS
}

// Biểu thức kiểm tra email
export const emailRegex = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

// Biểu thức kiểm tra mật khẩu mạnh (tối thiểu 8 ký tự, có hoa, thường, số, ký tự đặc biệt)
export const strongPasswordRegex = /^(?=.*[a-z])(?=.*[A-Z])(?=.*\d)(?=.*[@$!%*?&])[A-Za-z\d@$!%*?&]{8,}$/;
```

### week-calendar.js

```js
// assets/js/week-calendar.js
(() => {
    const renderActionChips = (listEl, rawCsv) => {
        if (!listEl) return;
        const items = String(rawCsv || "")
            .split(",")
            .map((s) => s.trim())
            .filter(Boolean);
        listEl.innerHTML = items.map((text) => `<li class="fengshui-actions__chip">${text}</li>`).join("");
    };

    const initWeekCalendar = () => {
        const rootEl = document.getElementById("week-calendar-root");
        const app = document.getElementById("week-cal-app");
        if (!app) return;

        if (rootEl) rootEl.setAttribute("aria-busy", "false");
        if (app.dataset.bound === "true") return;
        app.dataset.bound = "true";

        const cards = Array.from(app.querySelectorAll(".week-cal__card"));
        const legendPills = app.querySelectorAll(".month-cal__legend-pill");
        const todayBtn = document.getElementById("week-today-btn");

        const selectDayCard = (targetCard, shouldFocus = false) => {
            if (!targetCard) return;

            // 1. Đọc dữ liệu từ data-* thông qua dataset
            const {
                date,
                solar,
                weekday,
                lunar,
                canchi,
                napam,
                truc,
                term,
                zodiac,
                fortune,
                fortuneLabel,
                goodHours,
                do: doCsv,
                dont: dontCsv,
            } = targetCard.dataset;

            // 2. Đồng bộ trạng thái Active (Nền màu theme mờ 9%) & Roving Tabindex
            cards.forEach((card) => {
                const isSelected = card === targetCard;
                card.classList.toggle("week-cal__card--active", isSelected);
                card.setAttribute("tabindex", isSelected ? "0" : "-1");
                if (isSelected) card.setAttribute("aria-current", "date");
                else card.removeAttribute("aria-current");
            });

            // 3. Cập nhật Thẻ Detail bên trái (Flat UI)
            app.dataset.activeDate = date;
            document.getElementById("hero-solar-display").textContent = solar;
            document.getElementById("hero-solar-display").setAttribute("datetime", date);
            document.getElementById("hero-weekday-display").textContent = weekday;
            document.getElementById("hero-lunar-display").innerHTML =
                `Ngày ${lunar} Âm lịch • <strong>${canchi}</strong> (${napam})`;

            const fortuneTextEl = document.getElementById("hero-day-fortune-text");
            if (fortuneTextEl) fortuneTextEl.textContent = fortuneLabel;
            document.getElementById("hero-day-fortune").dataset.fortune = fortune;

            // Cập nhật 2 badge ngang Tiết khí & Trực
            document.getElementById("hero-term-val").textContent = term;
            document.getElementById("hero-truc-val").textContent = truc;

            // Cập nhật Giờ tốt & Danh sách Chip Nên làm / Không nên làm
            document.getElementById("hero-good-hours").textContent = goodHours;
            renderActionChips(document.getElementById("hero-do-list"), doCsv);
            renderActionChips(document.getElementById("hero-dont-list"), dontCsv);

            document.getElementById("hero-detail-link").href = `./index.html?date=${date}`;
            document.getElementById("hero-zodiac-img").src = `./assets/icons/zodiac/${zodiac}.svg`;

            if (shouldFocus) targetCard.focus();
        };

        cards.forEach((card, idx) => {
            card.addEventListener("click", () => selectDayCard(card));

            card.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
                let nextIdx = -1;
                if (e.key === "ArrowRight" || e.key === "ArrowDown") nextIdx = (idx + 1) % cards.length;
                if (e.key === "ArrowLeft" || e.key === "ArrowUp") nextIdx = (idx - 1 + cards.length) % cards.length;

                if (nextIdx !== -1) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    selectDayCard(cards[nextIdx], true);
                }
            });
        });

        if (todayBtn) {
            todayBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
                const todayCard = app.querySelector(".week-cal__card--today") || cards[4];
                if (todayCard) selectDayCard(todayCard, true);
            });
        }

        // Lọc sáng Hoàng Đạo / Hắc Đạo / Hôm Nay từ thanh Legend đồng bộ
        const highlightWeekCards = (statusKey, activePill = null) => {
            legendPills.forEach((p) => {
                const isMatch = p === activePill && Boolean(statusKey);
                p.classList.toggle("is-active", isMatch);
                p.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(isMatch));
            });

            cards.forEach((card) => {
                if (!statusKey) {
                    card.classList.remove("is-dimmed", "is-highlighted");
                    return;
                }
                const cardStatus = card.dataset.status || "";
                const isMatch = cardStatus.split(" ").includes(statusKey);
                card.classList.toggle("is-highlighted", isMatch);
                card.classList.toggle("is-dimmed", !isMatch);
            });
        };

        legendPills.forEach((pill) => {
            const filterKey = pill.dataset.filter;
            pill.addEventListener("mouseenter", () => highlightWeekCards(filterKey, pill));
            pill.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => highlightWeekCards(null));
            pill.addEventListener("focus", () => highlightWeekCards(filterKey, pill));
            pill.addEventListener("blur", () => highlightWeekCards(null));
        });
    };

    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", initWeekCalendar);
    window.addEventListener("template-loaded", initWeekCalendar);
})();
```

### year-calendar.js

```js
// assets/js/year-calendar.js
// Chuyên trách duy nhất Trang Lịch Năm (year.html): Vẽ động 12 tháng (504 ô) bằng LunarEngine, Prev/Next Năm & Bộ lọc
(() => {
    let activeYear = 2026;

    const renderFullYearGrid = (yearNum) => {
        const app = document.getElementById("year-cal-app");
        const monthsGrid = document.getElementById("year-months-grid");
        if (!app || !monthsGrid || !window.LunarEngine) return;

        activeYear = Number(yearNum);
        app.dataset.activeYear = String(activeYear);
        app.setAttribute("aria-label", `Lịch tổng hợp 12 tháng năm ${activeYear}`);
        monthsGrid.setAttribute("aria-label", `12 tháng trong năm ${activeYear}`);

        // Cập nhật tiêu đề Năm, nút Prev/Next và Dropdown
        const mainYearNum = document.getElementById("year-main-number");
        const summaryText = document.getElementById("cal-year-label");
        const prevBtn = document.getElementById("cal-prev-year");
        const nextBtn = document.getElementById("cal-next-year");
        const yearDropdown = document.getElementById("cal-year-dropdown");

        if (mainYearNum) {
            mainYearNum.textContent = String(activeYear);
            mainYearNum.setAttribute("datetime", String(activeYear));
        }
        if (summaryText) summaryText.textContent = `Năm ${activeYear}`;
        if (prevBtn) {
            prevBtn.setAttribute("aria-label", `Xem năm trước (${activeYear - 1})`);
            prevBtn.title = `Chuyển sang năm ${activeYear - 1}`;
        }
        if (nextBtn) {
            nextBtn.setAttribute("aria-label", `Xem năm sau (${activeYear + 1})`);
            nextBtn.title = `Chuyển sang năm ${activeYear + 1}`;
        }

        if (yearDropdown) {
            const yearList = document.getElementById("year-dropdown-list");
            if (yearList && !yearList.querySelector(`[data-year="${activeYear}"]`)) {
                const li = document.createElement("li");
                li.innerHTML = `<button type="button" role="option" aria-selected="false" data-year="${activeYear}" class="month-cal__option" title="Xem lịch năm ${activeYear}">Năm ${activeYear}</button>`;
                yearList.appendChild(li);
            }
            yearDropdown.querySelectorAll(".month-cal__option").forEach((opt) => {
                const isSelected = Number(opt.dataset.year) === activeYear;
                opt.classList.toggle("is-selected", isSelected);
                opt.setAttribute("aria-selected", String(isSelected));
            });
        }

        const realNow = new Date();
        const realDay = realNow.getDate();
        const realMonth = realNow.getMonth() + 1;
        const realYear = realNow.getFullYear();
        const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");

        const fragment = document.createDocumentFragment();
        let hasAssignedTabIndexZero = false;

        for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) {
            const article = document.createElement("article");
            article.className = "month-cal month-cal--mini";
            article.dataset.month = String(m);
            article.dataset.year = String(activeYear);
            article.setAttribute("aria-label", `Tháng ${m} năm ${activeYear}`);

            const firstDayJs = new Date(activeYear, m - 1, 1).getDay();
            const leadingMutedCount = (firstDayJs + 6) % 7;
            const daysInMonth = new Date(activeYear, m, 0).getDate();

            const prevMonth = m === 1 ? 12 : m - 1;
            const prevYear = m === 1 ? activeYear - 1 : activeYear;
            const daysInPrevMonth = new Date(prevYear, prevMonth, 0).getDate();

            const nextMonth = m === 12 ? 1 : m + 1;
            const nextYear = m === 12 ? activeYear + 1 : activeYear;

            let cellsHtml = "";
            for (let i = 0; i < 42; i++) {
                let cDay;
                let cMonth;
                let cYear;
                let isMuted = false;

                if (i < leadingMutedCount) {
                    cDay = daysInPrevMonth - leadingMutedCount + 1 + i;
                    cMonth = prevMonth;
                    cYear = prevYear;
                    isMuted = true;
                } else if (i < leadingMutedCount + daysInMonth) {
                    cDay = i - leadingMutedCount + 1;
                    cMonth = m;
                    cYear = activeYear;
                } else {
                    cDay = i - (leadingMutedCount + daysInMonth) + 1;
                    cMonth = nextMonth;
                    cYear = nextYear;
                    isMuted = true;
                }

                const info = window.LunarEngine.getFullDayInfo(cDay, cMonth, cYear);
                const isToday = cDay === realDay && cMonth === realMonth && cYear === realYear;
                const isDefaultActive =
                    !isMuted &&
                    ((activeYear === realYear && isToday) || (activeYear !== realYear && m === 1 && cDay === 1));

                const statusTokens = [];
                if (info.fortuneStatus) statusTokens.push(info.fortuneStatus);
                if (info.isHoliday) statusTokens.push("holiday");
                if (isToday) statusTokens.push("today");

                let dotsHtml = "";
                if (!isMuted) {
                    let innerDots = "";
                    if (info.fortuneStatus === "good") {
                        innerDots += `<i class="month-cal__dot month-cal__dot--good"></i>`;
                    }
                    if (info.isHoliday) {
                        innerDots += `<i class="month-cal__dot month-cal__dot--holiday"></i>`;
                    }
                    if (innerDots) {
                        dotsHtml = `<span class="month-cal__dots" aria-hidden="true">${innerDots}</span>`;
                    }
                }

                const isoDate = `${cYear}-${pad(cMonth)}-${pad(cDay)}`;
                const titleParts = [`${pad(cDay)}/${pad(cMonth)}/${cYear} (${info.lunarDay}/${info.lunarMonth} ÂL)`];
                if (info.badgeText) titleParts.push(info.badgeText);
                else if (info.fortuneStatus === "good") titleParts.push("Hoàng Đạo");

                const tabIndexVal = isDefaultActive && !hasAssignedTabIndexZero ? "0" : "-1";
                if (tabIndexVal === "0") hasAssignedTabIndexZero = true;

                cellsHtml += `
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        class="month-cal__cell ${isMuted ? "month-cal__cell--muted" : ""} ${isToday ? "month-cal__cell--today" : ""} ${isDefaultActive ? "month-cal__cell--active" : ""}"
                        tabindex="${tabIndexVal}"
                        ${isDefaultActive ? 'aria-current="date"' : ""}
                        data-date="${isoDate}"
                        data-solar="${cDay}"
                        data-lunar="${info.lunarDay}/${info.lunarMonth}"
                        ${statusTokens.length ? `data-status="${statusTokens.join(" ")}"` : ""}
                        title="${titleParts.join(" - ")}"
                    >
                        <span class="month-cal__solar">${cDay}</span>${dotsHtml}
                    </button>
                `;
            }

            article.innerHTML = `
                <h2 class="month-cal__mini-title">Tháng ${m}</h2>
                <div class="month-cal__weekdays" role="row">
                    <span class="month-cal__weekday" role="columnheader" title="Thứ Hai">Hai</span>
                    <span class="month-cal__weekday" role="columnheader" title="Thứ Ba">Ba</span>
                    <span class="month-cal__weekday" role="columnheader" title="Thứ Tư">Tư</span>
                    <span class="month-cal__weekday" role="columnheader" title="Thứ Năm">Năm</span>
                    <span class="month-cal__weekday" role="columnheader" title="Thứ Sáu">Sáu</span>
                    <span class="month-cal__weekday month-cal__weekday--sat" role="columnheader" title="Thứ Bảy">Bảy</span>
                    <span class="month-cal__weekday month-cal__weekday--sun" role="columnheader" title="Chủ Nhật">CN</span>
                </div>
                <div class="month-cal__grid" role="group" aria-label="Các ngày trong Tháng ${m}">
                    ${cellsHtml}
                </div>
            `;
            fragment.appendChild(article);
        }

        monthsGrid.innerHTML = "";
        monthsGrid.appendChild(fragment);

        bindYearCellEvents(app);
    };

    const bindYearCellEvents = (app) => {
        const allCells = Array.from(app.querySelectorAll(".month-cal__cell"));

        const selectYearCell = (targetCell, shouldFocus = false) => {
            if (!targetCell) return;
            allCells.forEach((item) => {
                const isSelected = item === targetCell;
                item.classList.toggle("month-cal__cell--active", isSelected);
                item.setAttribute("tabindex", isSelected ? "0" : "-1");
                if (isSelected) item.setAttribute("aria-current", "date");
                else item.removeAttribute("aria-current");
            });
            if (shouldFocus) targetCell.focus();
        };

        allCells.forEach((cell, idx) => {
            cell.onclick = () => selectYearCell(cell);

            cell.onkeydown = (e) => {
                let nextIdx = -1;
                if (e.key === "ArrowRight") nextIdx = Math.min(allCells.length - 1, idx + 1);
                if (e.key === "ArrowLeft") nextIdx = Math.max(0, idx - 1);
                if (e.key === "ArrowDown") nextIdx = Math.min(allCells.length - 1, idx + 7);
                if (e.key === "ArrowUp") nextIdx = Math.max(0, idx - 7);

                if (nextIdx !== -1 && nextIdx !== idx) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    selectYearCell(allCells[nextIdx], true);
                }
            };
        });
    };

    const initYearCalendar = () => {
        const rootEl = document.getElementById("year-calendar-root");
        const app = document.getElementById("year-cal-app");
        if (!app || !window.LunarEngine) return;

        if (rootEl) rootEl.setAttribute("aria-busy", "false");
        if (app.dataset.bound === "true") return;
        app.dataset.bound = "true";

        const prevYearBtn = app.querySelector("#cal-prev-year");
        const nextYearBtn = app.querySelector("#cal-next-year");
        const todayBtn = app.querySelector("#cal-current-year-btn");
        const yearDropdown = app.querySelector("#cal-year-dropdown");
        const legendPills = app.querySelectorAll(".month-cal__legend-pill");

        if (prevYearBtn) {
            prevYearBtn.onclick = () => renderFullYearGrid(activeYear - 1);
        }

        if (nextYearBtn) {
            nextYearBtn.onclick = () => renderFullYearGrid(activeYear + 1);
        }

        if (todayBtn) {
            todayBtn.onclick = () => {
                const currentRealYear = new Date().getFullYear();
                renderFullYearGrid(currentRealYear);
                const todayCell = app.querySelector(
                    ".month-cal__cell:not(.month-cal__cell--muted).month-cal__cell--today",
                );
                if (todayCell) {
                    todayCell.focus();
                    todayCell.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "center" });
                }
            };
        }

        if (yearDropdown) {
            const summary = yearDropdown.querySelector("summary");
            yearDropdown.ontoggle = () => {
                summary?.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(yearDropdown.open));
            };

            yearDropdown.onclick = (e) => {
                const opt = e.target.closest(".month-cal__option");
                if (!opt) return;
                e.stopPropagation();
                yearDropdown.removeAttribute("open");
                renderFullYearGrid(Number(opt.dataset.year) || 2026);
            };
        }

        const highlightYearCells = (statusKey, activePill = null) => {
            legendPills.forEach((p) => {
                const isMatch = p === activePill && Boolean(statusKey);
                p.classList.toggle("is-active", isMatch);
                p.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(isMatch));
            });

            app.querySelectorAll(".month-cal__cell").forEach((cell) => {
                if (!statusKey) {
                    cell.classList.remove("is-dimmed", "is-highlighted");
                    return;
                }
                const cellStatus = cell.dataset.status || "";
                const isMatch = cellStatus.split(" ").includes(statusKey);
                cell.classList.toggle("is-highlighted", isMatch);
                cell.classList.toggle("is-dimmed", !isMatch);
            });
        };

        legendPills.forEach((pill) => {
            const filterKey = pill.dataset.filter;
            pill.onmouseenter = () => highlightYearCells(filterKey, pill);
            pill.onmouseleave = () => highlightYearCells(null);
            pill.onfocus = () => highlightYearCells(filterKey, pill);
            pill.onblur = () => highlightYearCells(null);
        });

        renderFullYearGrid(Number(app.dataset.activeYear) || 2026);
    };

    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", initYearCalendar);
    window.addEventListener("template-loaded", initYearCalendar);
})();
```

### tarot-detail.js

```js
// assets/js/tarot-detail.js
// Trang chi tiết 78 Lá Bài Tarot: Đọc ./api/tarot.json, công tắc Chiều Xuôi / Chiều Ngược 180°,
// phân tích Nguyên tố theo Bộ, Lightbox phóng to & Sidebar Sticky 78 lá
(() => {
    const TAROT_IMG_DIR = "./assets/images/tarot";
    const TAROT_IMG_EXT = "png";
    const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");

    const SUIT_META = {
        major: {
            en: "Major Arcana",
            vn: "Bộ Ẩn Chính (22 lá)",
            short: "Ẩn Chính",
            element: "Tinh thần & Chu kỳ Lớn",
            domain: "Bước ngoặt cuộc đời & Sứ mệnh",
            icon: "./assets/icons/sparkle.svg",
            guide: "22 lá Bộ Ẩn Chính (Major Arcana) đại diện cho những bài học cốt lõi, bước ngoặt chiến lược và hành trình trưởng thành sâu sắc về nhận thức. Khi lá Ẩn Chính xuất hiện, thông điệp thường mang tầm ảnh hưởng dài hạn thay vì chỉ là chuyện nhất thời.",
        },
        wands: {
            en: "Suit of Wands",
            vn: "Bộ Gậy (14 lá)",
            short: "Bộ Gậy",
            element: "Nguyên tố Lửa (Hỏa)",
            domain: "Đam mê, Sáng tạo & Hành động",
            icon: "./assets/icons/fire.svg",
            guide: "Bộ Gậy gắn liền với nguyên tố Lửa — đại diện cho ý chí tiên phong, cảm hứng sáng tạo, động lực hành động và bản lĩnh dấn thân. Các lá Bộ Gậy khuyến khích sự chủ động, tốc độ và dám mở đường mới.",
        },
        swords: {
            en: "Suit of Swords",
            vn: "Bộ Kiếm (14 lá)",
            short: "Bộ Kiếm",
            element: "Nguyên tố Khí",
            domain: "Trí tuệ, Logic & Quyết định",
            icon: "./assets/icons/metal.svg",
            guide: "Bộ Kiếm gắn liền với nguyên tố Khí — đại diện cho tư duy phân tích, sự minh bạch, giao tiếp sắc bén và khả năng cắt bỏ những ràng buộc mơ hồ. Bộ bài này nhắc nhở dùng cái đầu lạnh và góc nhìn khách quan để giải quyết thử thách.",
        },
        cups: {
            en: "Suit of Cups",
            vn: "Bộ Cốc (14 lá)",
            short: "Bộ Cốc",
            element: "Nguyên tố Nước (Thủy)",
            domain: "Cảm xúc, Trực giác & Nhân duyên",
            icon: "./assets/icons/water.svg",
            guide: "Bộ Cốc gắn liền với nguyên tố Nước — đại diện cho đời sống nội tâm, lòng trắc ẩn, sự đồng điệu trong các mối quan hệ và cảm hứng nghệ thuật. Thông điệp Bộ Cốc thường nhấn mạnh sự lắng nghe trái tim và giữ gìn hòa khí.",
        },
        pentacles: {
            en: "Suit of Pentacles",
            vn: "Bộ Tiền (14 lá)",
            short: "Bộ Tiền",
            element: "Nguyên tố Đất (Thổ)",
            domain: "Tài chính, Sự nghiệp & Giá trị thực",
            icon: "./assets/icons/earth.svg",
            guide: "Bộ Tiền gắn liền với nguyên tố Đất — đại diện cho tài sản hữu hình, nền tảng sự nghiệp bền vững, sức khỏe thể chất và tính kỷ luật thực tế. Bộ bài này ủng hộ chiến lược đi từng bước chắc chắn để tích lũy thành quả lâu dài.",
        },
    };

    let allCards = [];
    let currentCard = null;
    let currentOrient = "upright";
    let activeSuitFilter = "all";
    let lastFocusedBeforeLightbox = null;

    if ("scrollRestoration" in window.history) {
        window.history.scrollRestoration = "manual";
    }
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, left: 0, behavior: "instant" });

    const setText = (id, text) => {
        const el = document.getElementById(id);
        if (el) el.textContent = text;
    };

    const getCardImgUrl = (card) => `${TAROT_IMG_DIR}/${card.suit}/${card.slug}.${TAROT_IMG_EXT}`;
    const getCardHref = (card, orient = "upright") =>
        `./tarot-detail.html?slug=${encodeURIComponent(card.slug)}&orientation=${orient}`;

    const applyOrientationState = (orient, { updateUrl = true } = {}) => {
        if (!currentCard) return;
        currentOrient = orient === "reversed" ? "reversed" : "upright";
        const isUp = currentOrient === "upright";

        const poster = document.getElementById("trd-poster-figure");
        const orientBadge = document.getElementById("trd-orient-badge");
        if (poster) poster.dataset.orientation = currentOrient;
        if (orientBadge) {
            orientBadge.dataset.orientation = currentOrient;
            orientBadge.textContent = isUp ? "Đang xem: Lá bài Xuôi (Upright)" : "Đang xem: Lá bài Ngược (Reversed)";
        }

        document.querySelectorAll("[data-set-orient]").forEach((btn) => {
            const isSel = btn.dataset.setOrient === currentOrient;
            btn.classList.toggle("is-active", isSel);
            btn.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(isSel));
        });

        setText("trd-hero-message", `"${isUp ? currentCard.msg_upright : currentCard.msg_reversed}"`);
        setText("trd-hero-keywords", `Từ khóa: ${isUp ? currentCard.keywords_upright : currentCard.keywords_reversed}`);
        setText("trd-stat-orient", isUp ? "Chiều Xuôi (Upright)" : "Chiều Ngược (Reversed)");

        document.getElementById("trd-box-upright")?.classList.toggle("div-detail-block--highlight", isUp);
        document.getElementById("trd-box-reversed")?.classList.toggle("div-detail-block--highlight", !isUp);

        if (updateUrl) {
            const url = new URL(window.location.href);
            url.searchParams.set("slug", currentCard.slug);
            url.searchParams.set("orientation", currentOrient);
            window.history.replaceState({}, "", url.toString());
        }
    };

    const openLightbox = () => {
        if (!currentCard) return;
        const lightbox = document.getElementById("detail-lightbox");
        const img = document.getElementById("detail-lightbox-img");
        const cap = document.getElementById("detail-lightbox-caption");
        if (!lightbox || !img) return;

        lastFocusedBeforeLightbox = document.activeElement;
        const title = `${currentCard.roman ? `${currentCard.roman}. ` : ""}${currentCard.vn} (${currentCard.en})`;
        const isRev = currentOrient === "reversed";

        img.src = getCardImgUrl(currentCard);
        img.alt = `Lá bài Tarot ${title}`;
        img.classList.toggle("is-reversed", isRev);
        if (cap) cap.textContent = `${title} • ${isRev ? "Chiều Ngược (Reversed)" : "Chiều Xuôi (Upright)"}`;

        lightbox.hidden = false;
        document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
        document.getElementById("detail-lightbox-close")?.focus();
    };

    const closeLightbox = () => {
        const lightbox = document.getElementById("detail-lightbox");
        if (!lightbox || lightbox.hidden) return;
        lightbox.hidden = true;
        document.body.style.overflow = "";
        if (lastFocusedBeforeLightbox && typeof lastFocusedBeforeLightbox.focus === "function") {
            lastFocusedBeforeLightbox.focus();
            lastFocusedBeforeLightbox = null;
        }
    };

    const renderRelatedSuitCards = () => {
        const grid = document.getElementById("trd-related-grid");
        if (!grid || !currentCard) return;

        const sameSuit = allCards.filter((c) => c.suit === currentCard.suit);
        const curPos = sameSuit.findIndex((c) => c.id === currentCard.id);
        const related = [];
        for (const offset of [-2, -1, 1, 2]) {
            const pick = sameSuit[(curPos + offset + sameSuit.length) % sameSuit.length];
            if (pick && pick.id !== currentCard.id && !related.some((r) => r.id === pick.id)) {
                related.push(pick);
            }
        }

        grid.innerHTML = related
            .map(
                (c) => `
                    <a
                        href="${getCardHref(c, "upright")}"
                        class="util-tarot-card"
                        title="Xem chi tiết lá bài ${c.vn} (${c.en})"
                    >
                        <div class="util-tarot-card__thumb">
                            <img
                                src="${getCardImgUrl(c)}"
                                alt="Lá bài Tarot ${c.vn} (${c.en})"
                                width="350"
                                height="600"
                                loading="lazy"
                                decoding="async"
                            />
                            <span class="util-tarot-card__badge">#${pad(c.id)}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="util-tarot-card__body">
                            <strong class="util-tarot-card__title">${c.roman ? `${c.roman}. ` : ""}${c.vn}</strong>
                            <span class="util-tarot-card__sub">${c.en}</span>
                        </div>
                    </a>
                `,
            )
            .join("");
    };

    const renderSidebar = () => {
        const listEl = document.getElementById("trd-sidebar-list");
        if (!listEl) return;

        const q = (document.getElementById("trd-sidebar-search")?.value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
        const filtered = allCards.filter((c) => {
            if (activeSuitFilter !== "all" && c.suit !== activeSuitFilter) return false;
            if (!q) return true;
            return `${pad(c.id)} ${c.id} ${c.roman} ${c.vn} ${c.en}`.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        });

        setText("trd-sidebar-count", `${filtered.length} lá`);

        if (!filtered.length) {
            listEl.innerHTML = `<p class="util-data-table__empty">Không có lá bài nào khớp bộ lọc.</p>`;
            return;
        }

        listEl.innerHTML = filtered
            .map((c) => {
                const isCur = currentCard && Number(c.id) === Number(currentCard.id);
                const suitShort = SUIT_META[c.suit]?.short || "Tarot";
                return `
                    <a
                        href="${getCardHref(c, "upright")}"
                        class="detail-sidebar__item div-sidebar-item ${isCur ? "is-current" : ""}"
                        ${isCur ? 'aria-current="page"' : ""}
                    >
                        <span class="div-sidebar-item__tarot-thumb" aria-hidden="true">
                            <img src="${getCardImgUrl(c)}" alt="" width="35" height="60" loading="lazy" decoding="async" />
                        </span>
                        <div class="detail-sidebar__item-info">
                            <strong class="detail-sidebar__item-name">${c.roman ? `${c.roman}. ` : ""}${c.vn}</strong>
                            <span class="detail-sidebar__item-occasion">${c.en} • ${suitShort}</span>
                        </div>
                    </a>
                `;
            })
            .join("");

        const activeEl = listEl.querySelector(".detail-sidebar__item.is-current");
        if (activeEl) {
            const topPos = activeEl.offsetTop - listEl.offsetTop - listEl.clientHeight / 2 + activeEl.clientHeight / 2;
            listEl.scrollTo({ top: Math.max(0, topPos), behavior: "smooth" });
        }
    };

    const initTarotDetailPage = async () => {
        try {
            const res = await fetch("./api/tarot.json");
            if (!res.ok) return;
            allCards = await res.json();
        } catch (err) {
            return;
        }

        if (!Array.isArray(allCards) || !allCards.length) return;

        const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        const slugParam = params.get("slug") || "";
        const idParam = params.has("id") ? Number(params.get("id")) : -1;
        const orientParam = params.get("orientation") === "reversed" ? "reversed" : "upright";

        currentCard =
            allCards.find((c) => c.slug === slugParam) ||
            allCards.find((c) => Number(c.id) === idParam) ||
            allCards.find((c) => Number(c.id) === 14) ||
            allCards[0];

        const curIdx = allCards.indexOf(currentCard);
        const prevCard = allCards[(curIdx - 1 + allCards.length) % allCards.length];
        const nextCard = allCards[(curIdx + 1) % allCards.length];

        document.getElementById("trd-prev-btn")?.setAttribute("href", getCardHref(prevCard, "upright"));
        document.getElementById("trd-next-btn")?.setAttribute("href", getCardHref(nextCard, "upright"));

        const suitInfo = SUIT_META[currentCard.suit] || SUIT_META.major;
        const displayTitle = `${currentCard.roman ? `${currentCard.roman}. ` : ""}${currentCard.vn} (${currentCard.en})`;

        document.title = `${displayTitle} - Tarot Rider-Waite • Lịch Phong Thủy`;

        setText("trd-suit-badge", suitInfo.short);
        setText("trd-id-badge", `Lá #${pad(currentCard.id)} / 78`);
        setText("trd-suit-eyebrow", `${suitInfo.vn} • ${suitInfo.en}`);
        setText("trd-main-title", displayTitle);

        const heroImg = document.getElementById("trd-hero-img");
        if (heroImg) {
            heroImg.src = getCardImgUrl(currentCard);
            heroImg.alt = `Lá bài Tarot ${displayTitle}`;
        }

        setText("trd-stat-suit", suitInfo.en);
        setText("trd-stat-suit-vn", suitInfo.vn);
        setText("trd-stat-element", suitInfo.element);
        setText("trd-stat-domain", suitInfo.domain);
        document.getElementById("trd-stat-elem-icon")?.setAttribute("src", suitInfo.icon);
        setText("trd-stat-roman", `${currentCard.roman || pad(currentCard.id)} • #${pad(currentCard.id)}`);
        setText("trd-stat-en", currentCard.en);

        setText("trd-up-keywords", currentCard.keywords_upright);
        setText("trd-up-msg", currentCard.msg_upright);
        setText("trd-rev-keywords", currentCard.keywords_reversed);
        setText("trd-rev-msg", currentCard.msg_reversed);
        setText("trd-body-career", currentCard.career);
        setText("trd-suit-guide-title", `Đặc Tính Nguyên Tố • ${suitInfo.vn}`);
        setText("trd-suit-guide-desc", suitInfo.guide);

        applyOrientationState(orientParam, { updateUrl: false });
        renderRelatedSuitCards();
        renderSidebar();

        // Công tắc Xuôi / Ngược
        document.querySelectorAll("[data-set-orient]").forEach((btn) => {
            btn.addEventListener("click", () => applyOrientationState(btn.dataset.setOrient, { updateUrl: true }));
        });

        // Lightbox phóng to (Chuột + Bàn phím)
        const poster = document.getElementById("trd-poster-figure");
        poster?.addEventListener("click", openLightbox);
        poster?.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
            if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") {
                e.preventDefault();
                openLightbox();
            }
        });
        document.getElementById("detail-lightbox-close")?.addEventListener("click", closeLightbox);
        document.getElementById("detail-lightbox-backdrop")?.addEventListener("click", closeLightbox);
        document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
            if (e.key === "Escape") closeLightbox();
        });

        // Bộ lọc Sidebar 78 lá
```
